# Ascent Schools Application — Project Guide

## Overview
Converting a legacy VB6 school management application (SAS) into a modern **SaaS** web + mobile platform.
Multi-tenant architecture: one DB per school group/society, shared master DB for SaaS control.

---

## Tech Stack

| Layer | Technology | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Frontend — School App | React JS + Vite | Ant Design UI, Zustand state |
| Frontend — Control App | React JS + Vite | Ant Design UI, Zustand state |
| Backend API | .NET Framework 4.8 Web API | Single API serving both apps |
| ORM / Data Access | Dapper | Lightweight, SQL-friendly, easy multi-tenant connection switching |
| Database | SQL Server | Master DB + one tenant DB per school group |
| Auth | JWT (access + refresh token) | See Auth section below |
| UI Component Library | Ant Design (AntD) | MIT license, fully free, enterprise tables/forms built-in |
| State Management | Zustand | Lightweight, simple, sufficient for this scale |
| HTTP Client | Native `fetch` API | No axios — avoids third-party dependency vulnerabilities |
| Mobile | Android — Kotlin + Jetpack Compose | MVVM, Retrofit, EncryptedSharedPreferences |

---

## Two Applications

### 1. Ascent Control App (`admin.edu-care.in`)
- **Who uses it:** Ascent internal team only (SaaS admin)
- **Purpose:** Onboard school groups, manage subscriptions, toggle modules, manage branding
- **Auth:** Against `ascent_master.control_users`
- **RBAC:** Simple — 3 roles as a column: `super_admin` / `support` / `billing`

### 2. Ascent School App (`{subdomain}.edu-care.in`)
- **Who uses it:** School staff (Principal, Admin Clerk, Fee Clerk, Teachers, etc.)
- **Purpose:** Full school management — students, fees, attendance, marks, library, transport
- **Auth:** Against tenant DB `users` table (after resolving subdomain → tenant DB)
- **RBAC:** Full role/permission system managed per school group

---

## SaaS Architecture: Two-Database Model

### Why two databases
- Schools are legally sensitive about student data → group-level isolation
- One group's load must not affect other groups
- Clean per-group backup / restore / offboarding
- Cross-branch reports within a group remain simple (same DB)

### Database 1 — `ascent_master` (Central / Control Plane)

| # | Table | Description |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `school_groups` | One row per paying client. Has `subdomain` for tenant routing. `db_username`/`db_password` store per-DB SQL credentials (fastwebhost assigns separate creds per DB). |
| 2 | `schools` | Individual branches under a group |
| 3 | `subscriptions` | SaaS plan, billing cycle, expiry, max_schools, max_students |
| 4 | `master_audit_logs` | SaaS-level events: logins, subscription changes, school creation |
| 5 | `control_users` | Ascent internal staff only |
| 6 | `refresh_tokens` | Hashed refresh tokens for control_users |
| 7 | `school_settings` | App configuration per school branch (1 row per branch) |
| 8 | `modules` | Master list of all system features, seeded with 7 modules |
| 9 | `group_modules` | Module on/off per school group (SaaS admin controls) |
| 10 | `school_modules` | Module on/off per branch (group admin controls, within group limits) |
| 11 | `group_branding` | Default branding for all branches in a group |
| 12 | `school_branding` | Branch-level branding override |
| 18 | `support_tickets` | Issue/Change tickets raised from the school app, managed from the control app across every group (Phase 132) — table numbering follows `master_tables.sql`; tables 13–17 (`parent_accounts`/`parent_children`/`parent_refresh_tokens`/`app_config`/`device_push_tokens`) exist in the DB but predate this table list being kept current |

### Database 2 — `ascent_group_{group_id}` (Tenant DB)
One DB per school group. DB name is always `ascent_group_{group_id}` — derived from PK, never stored.
Run `tenant_tables.sql` on onboarding (called automatically by `POST /control/school-groups`).

| # | Table | Description | Source (VB6) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `permissions` | Granular permission codes (seeded) | *(new)* |
| 2 | `roles` | Staff roles per group/branch (seeded with defaults) | *(new)* |
| 3 | `role_permissions` | Role ↔ permission mapping | *(new)* |
| 4 | `users` | School staff login users | *(new)* |
| 5 | `refresh_tokens` | Hashed refresh tokens for school staff | *(new)* |
| 6 | `user_roles` | User ↔ role ↔ branch assignment | *(new)* |
| 7 | `audit_logs` | Row-level change tracking (FK to users in same DB) | SAS_TransLogData |
| 8 | `academic_years` | Academic year config per school | SAS_AcdYear |

| 49 | `class_teacher_assignments` | Class (+ optional section) → teacher(s) per academic year; `section_id` NULL = whole class; drives parent→teacher message routing; two filtered unique indexes | *(new)* |

| 55 | `marks_grade_master_bands` | The bands of a scale (raw marks, not %): `min_marks`, `max_marks`, `grade`, `grade_point` (text), `description`; `UNIQUE(scale_id, grade)` (Phase 119) | SAS_MarksGradeMaster |
| 45 | `grade_types` | Marks-to-grade bands per subject; now has a CRUD form (Master Data → Grade Types) — was migrate-only | SAS_MarksGrade |

| 30 | `student_marks` | Per-student per-subject marks per exam; gained `exam_id` (FK → exam_master), `activity_marks`, `activity_max_marks` (total = marks_obtained + ISNULL(activity_marks,0)) | *(new)* |

> **Migration:** Run `Database/sections_migration.sql` on existing tenant DBs to add the `sections` table and `section_id` column on `students`. Run `Database/fee_tables_migration.sql` for fee receipt tables. Run `Database/fee_periods_migration.sql` for fee_periods table + payment_type/fee_period_id columns. Run `Database/student_unique_id_migration.sql` to add and backfill student_unique_id on students. Run `Database/fee_concessions_migration.sql` for fee_concessions table. For the exam/marks feature (Phase 106) run `Database/class_subjects_migration.sql`, `Database/exam_master_exam_name_migration.sql`, and `Database/student_marks_exam_activity_migration.sql`. For the total-marks grade bands (Phase 117) run `Database/marks_grade_master_migration.sql`, then `Database/marks_card_migration.sql` (Phase 118), then **`Database/marks_grade_scales_migration.sql`** (Phase 119 — standalone scales; drops what the first two built). New groups get all 55 tables automatically via the updated `tenant_tables.sql`.

## Authentication & Tenant Routing

### Subdomain-based tenant identification
Each school group gets a unique subdomain:
```
srividya.edu-care.in      → resolves to group_id → ascent_group_1
ascent-public.edu-care.in → different group      → ascent_group_2
admin.edu-care.in         → control app (master DB only)
```

### JWT Strategy — Two token types

#### Control App tokens (`tokenType=control`)
- Claims: `tokenType=control`, `userId`, `fullName`, `role`
- Generated by `JwtHelper.GenerateControlAccessToken(ControlTokenClaims)`
- Validated by `ControlAuthAttribute` on all control controllers
- Refresh token stored in `ascent_master.refresh_tokens` (cookie name: `controlRefreshToken`)

#### School App tokens (`tokenType=school`)
- Claims: `tokenType=school`, `userId`, `groupId`, `schoolId`, `dbName`, `perm[]`
- Generated by `JwtHelper.GenerateAccessToken(TokenClaims)`
- Validated by global `JwtAuthenticationFilter`, populates `TenantContext.Current`
- `JwtAuthenticationFilter` skips TenantContext for `tokenType=control` tokens
- Refresh token stored in tenant DB `refresh_tokens` (cookie name: `schoolRefreshToken`)

**Access token:** web/control 480 min / 8 h (`Jwt:AccessTokenMins` in Web.config; was 30); **mobile (parent/student/teacher) 180 days** (`Jwt:MobileAccessTokenMins` = `259200`, code-default 259200 — Phase 96/97; the mobile app does NO cold-start refresh, so this lifetime alone keeps a parent signed in between opens) | **Refresh token:** web apps 7 days (`Jwt:RefreshTokenDays`); **mobile (parent/student/teacher) 365 days** (`Jwt:MobileRefreshTokenDays`, defaults to 365 in code — Phase 80, cuts SMS re-OTP cost); both sliding expiry, HttpOnly cookie, SHA-256 hash in DB

> **Refresh cookie Path = `/`** (Phase 56). All refresh cookies (`schoolRefreshToken`, `controlRefreshToken`, `parentRefreshToken`, `studentRefreshToken`, `teacherRefreshToken`) MUST use `Path=/`. They previously used the route-specific path (e.g. `/school/auth`), which silently broke silent-refresh in production: the API is served under `/api` (`https://edu-care.in/api`), so the real refresh URL is `/api/school/auth/refresh` and a cookie scoped to `/school/auth` is never sent (path mismatch) → 401 → logout the moment the access token expired. It only worked in local dev because there's no `/api` prefix there.

### Password hashing
SHA-256 (same as `JwtHelper.HashRefreshToken`) — used for both `control_users.password_hash` and `users.password_hash`.

### School App login flow
```
1. React app loads → GET /branding (public, no auth) → applies school colors/logo
2. Silent refresh attempted on mount → restores session if cookie is valid
3. User submits login form → POST /school/auth/login with X-Subdomain header
4. API resolves subdomain → group_id → ascent_group_{group_id}
5. Validates username + password_hash against tenant.users
6. Determines schoolId (from users.school_id or first from user_roles)
7. Loads permissions for that school
8. Returns: access token (JWT body) + sets HttpOnly schoolRefreshToken cookie
9. React stores schoolId in localStorage (for refresh calls across page reloads)
10. React stores access token in Zustand memory only (XSS protection)
11. All API calls send Authorization: Bearer {access_token} + X-Subdomain header
```

### School App refresh flow
```
POST /school/auth/refresh?schoolId={id}
Headers: X-Subdomain: {subdomain}
Cookie: schoolRefreshToken (HttpOnly)
→ Rotates refresh token, returns new access token
```

## RBAC Design (Tenant DB)

### Tables
- `permissions` — codes like `STUDENT_FEE.COLLECT`, format: `MODULE_CODE.ACTION`
- `roles` — named roles, can be group-wide (`school_id NULL`) or branch-specific
- `role_permissions` — many-to-many
- `users` — school staff; `school_id NULL` = access to all branches
- `user_roles` — user ↔ role ↔ branch (different roles in different branches supported)

### Default permissions seeded (24 codes)
| Module | Permission Codes |
|---|---|
| STUDENT_PROFILE | VIEW, CREATE, EDIT, DELETE |
| STUDENT_FEE | VIEW, COLLECT, EDIT, CANCEL_RECEIPT, CONCESSION |
| ATTENDANCE | VIEW, MARK, EDIT |
| MARKS | VIEW, ENTER, EDIT, PUBLISH |
| LIBRARY | VIEW, ISSUE, RETURN, MANAGE |
| TRANSPORT | VIEW, MANAGE |
| HOSTEL | VIEW, MANAGE |

### Default roles seeded
`Principal`, `Admin Clerk`, `Fee Clerk`, `Class Teacher`, `Librarian`

### API permission check (every protected school endpoint)
```
[RequireModule(ModuleCodes.StudentFee)]           ← checks group_modules + school_modules
[RequirePermission(PermissionCodes.StudentFee.Collect)]  ← checks permissions[] in JWT
public HttpResponseMessage CollectFee(...) { }
     ↓
SchoolAuthAttribute       → TenantContext.Current == null → 401
RequireModuleAttribute    → module disabled for group/branch → 403
RequirePermissionAttribute → permission missing in JWT claims → 403
     ↓
Controller executes
```

### Filter execution order
Declare `[RequireModule]` before `[RequirePermission]` — Web API runs attribute filters in declaration
order within the same scope. `SchoolAuthAttribute` is always first (applied at class level on
BaseSchoolController). Module check runs second, permission check last.

### ModuleRepository.IsModuleEnabled(groupId, schoolId, moduleCode)
Single SQL query covering the full three-level hierarchy:
- group row disabled (is_enabled=0) → CAST(0 AS BIT) — hard block
- school row exists                 → use school is_enabled value
- no school row                     → ISNULL(group is_enabled, 1) — inherit, default on
- module code not in modules table  → returns null → treated as false (disabled)

---

## Module Access Control

### Three-level hierarchy
```
modules (system-wide, Ascent manages)
  └── group_modules (per society, SaaS admin controls)
        └── school_modules (per branch, group admin controls)
```

### Rules
- Group disabled → branch **cannot** override (hard block)
- Group enabled → branch can disable for their branch
- No row in `school_modules` → inherits group setting
- `modules.plan_tier` — Basic / Standard / Premium (enforced against subscription)

### Known modules (seeded in master DB)
| Code | Name | Min Plan |
|---|---|---|
| STUDENT_PROFILE | Student Profile | Basic |
| STUDENT_FEE | Student Fee | Basic |
| ATTENDANCE | Student Attendance | Basic |
| MARKS | Student Marks | Standard |
| LIBRARY | Library | Standard |
| TRANSPORT | Transport | Basic |
| HOSTEL | Hostel | Premium |

---

## Branding

### What is configurable per school/group
- Logo (header, login page, receipts/reports)
- Favicon
- Primary color, secondary color, header background, nav text color
- Display name (override school name shown in UI)
- Tagline (shown on login page)
- Receipt/report footer text
- Login page background image

### Hierarchy
```
group_branding   ← default for all branches
  └── school_branding  ← overrides group when set for a specific branch
```

### How React applies it (School App)
1. Public endpoint `GET /branding` called before login (no auth needed)
2. API resolves subdomain → returns most specific branding (branch → group fallback)
3. React applies via Ant Design `ConfigProvider` theme tokens:
   ```jsx
   <ConfigProvider theme={{ token: { colorPrimary: branding.primaryColor } }}>
   ```
4. CSS variables set on `document.documentElement` for non-AntD elements.

---

## Tenant DB Provisioning (on school group onboarding)
Tenant DB is provisioned **manually** — the control app UI only creates the master DB record.

**Control app flow:**
1. `POST /control/school-groups` with `{ groupName, subdomain, description }`
2. API inserts into `school_groups` → gets `group_id`, seeds `group_modules` (all enabled)
3. UI shows success message with the DB name to provision (`ascent_group_{group_id}`)

**Manual DB setup (run in SSMS on SQL Server):**
1. Connect to SQL Server
2. Run `Database/tenant_tables.sql` while connected to `master` — creates `ascent_group_{N}` DB with all 52 tables + seeds permissions and roles
3. Connect to `ascent_group_{N}`
4. Run `Database/seed_tenant_data.sql` — seeds role-permission mappings, payment modes (Cash/Cheque/Online), and default admin login
5. Set `@SchoolId` in `seed_tenant_data.sql` to the `school_id` from `ascent_master.schools` (add a school branch first via the control app)
6. In control app → school group → **Edit Settings** → fill in **DB Name**, **DB Username**, **DB Password** → Save
7. School group is ready to use

Phase 4a ✅  Student Profile module
Phase 4b ✅  Student Fee module (fee structure setup, fee collection, receipts + cancel)
Phase 4b+ ✅ Payment Gateway integration (Razorpay; pluggable for Cashfree/PayU; webhook support)

Phase 4d ✅  Marks module (exam types + marks entry grid built in Phase 6C)
Phase 4e ⏸   Library module (deferred — schema not yet designed)
Phase 4f ✅  Transport module (buses, routes, bus fee structure, student assignment)
Phase 5  ✅  Dashboard + cross-module summary (students, attendance, fee trend, recent receipts, upcoming homework)
Phase 6A ✅  Mobile app — DB schema (all new tables)
Phase 6B ✅  Mobile app — Backend APIs (auth + data endpoints)
Phase 6C ✅  Mobile app — Web UI additions (marks entry, homework, announcements)
Phase 6D ✅  Mobile app — Android app (Kotlin + Jetpack Compose)
Phase 7  ✅  Bulk CSV import — students (POST /school/students/bulk) + fee structure (POST /school/fees/structure/bulk); papaparse client-side parsing, preview table, example data, error report download
Phase 8  ✅  Student promotion — multi-year model (one row per student per academic year); PromoteStudentsPage: from year+class+section → preview → to year+class+section (required) → confirm; mobile auth re-points to latest student_id on login
Phase 9  ✅  Android app — parent-only login (student login removed); product flavors for white-label multi-school builds (one APK per school, unique applicationId + SCHOOL_CODE baked in per flavor)
Phase 10 ✅  Android app — SMS OTP auth (replaces PIN login); device binding (one device per parent); teacher staff login via bottom sheet; SmsHelper for smslogin.mobi gateway
Phase 11 ✅  School events gallery — video + image gallery tab in mobile app; media stored externally (YouTube for videos, Cloudinary for images); SQL Server stores metadata + URLs only; school web app has Events Gallery management page
Phase 12 ✅  SMS Center — send Absent / Fee Due / Custom SMS to parents; client-side batching (25/50 per API call) + server-side Task.WhenAll parallel dispatch; smslogin.mobi gateway; sms_logs audit trail; SMS History tab
Phase 13 ✅  Student lifecycle flags — Block (status=Blocked, excluded from attendance+SMS) and Detain (is_detained flag, excluded from promotion, remains Active for daily ops); warn on promote preview with detained count
Phase 14 ✅  Blood Group Search — dedicated page under Students; filters GET /school/students by bloodGroup param; colour-coded blood group tags; PDF + CSV export
Phase 15 ✅  Reports additions — Regular Absentees (threshold-based, date range, class/section filter) + Detained Students report; both with PDF/CSV export
Phase 16 ✅  Per-DB SQL credentials — `db_username`/`db_password` added to `school_groups`; `TenantConnectionFactory` looks them up with 15-min cache, falls back to template; control app Edit Settings modal exposes all three DB fields; cache invalidated on save
Phase 17 ✅  Parent OTP auto-link — `request-otp` gates on student existing in school's DB by `father_mobile` (no pre-existing parent account required); `verify-otp` auto-creates `parent_accounts` + auto-links all matching students from that school; no manual SQL or link-child flow needed
Phase 18 ✅  School-scoped children + child link upsert — `GET /mobile/auth/parent/children` filters by `group_id` (current school's group from X-School-Code) so each white-label app only shows its own school's children; `POST /mobile/auth/parent/select-child` validates link belongs to current school (defense-in-depth); `verify-otp` auto-link loop changed to upsert by `admission_no` — updates `class_name`, `student_name`, `student_id` on existing links rather than skipping them (fixes NULL class_name on old links and handles post-promotion student_id changes)
Phase 19 ✅  Homework section field — `section_id` (nullable FK) added to `homework` table; web UI HomeworkPage shows Section dropdown (loaded by class); mobile homework feed filters by `section_id IS NULL OR section_id = @sectionId` so class-wide entries always show but section-specific entries only reach matching section
Phase 20 ✅  Bulk student import extended — 7 new columns added: `MotherMobile`, `AadharNo`, `Caste`, `CasteCode`, `Religion`, `JoiningClass`, `MotherTongue`; `StudentsImportPage` column reference table + example rows updated; `StudentBulkRow` DTO + `BulkCreate` INSERT extended
Phase 21 ✅  Android login screen logo + app icon fix — school logo (`R.mipmap.ic_launcher_round`) displayed above school name in `SmsAuthScreen`; `AndroidManifest.xml` fixed from `@drawable/ic_launcher` → `@mipmap/ic_launcher` (was loading old vector XML, bypassing Image Asset mipmap files); flavor background XML changed to plain white; `versionCode` bumped to 2
Phase 22 ✅  Android UI polish — custom navy/gold palette in `Theme.kt`; login screen: diagonal gradient bg + frosted glass card + `AsyncImage` logo (Coil); shimmer skeletons on all data screens; attendance: monthly calendar grid + summary card + stat pills; marks: animated bar charts per subject + score badge + staggered entrance; `HomeScreen`: `AnimatedContent` tab transitions + navy `TopAppBar` + navy-tinted `NavigationBar`; `NavigationBar` crash fixed (removed `tonalElevation = Dp.Unspecified`); version string shown on login screen (`BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME`)

Phase 68 ✅  Android child-context restore after refresh (Android-only) — fixes "Please select a child first" on every screen after closing & reopening the app (or after a mid-session token refresh). Cause: the parent **refresh** endpoint returns a parent token WITHOUT child context (by design — the refresh cookie is parent-level), and nothing re-selected the child; data endpoints (`[MobileAuth(requireChildContext:true)]`) then reject with `MobileAuthAttribute`'s "Please select a child first." The Phase 65 session-keepalive surfaced it — previously token expiry forced a full re-login (which re-selected the child); now the session persists via refresh, exposing the missing child context. Fix: persist the selected child's `parent_children.link_id` as `TokenStore.childLinkId` (set in `AuthRepository.selectChild`; cleared on logout); then re-establish child context after every refresh — (a) **cold start** (`MainActivity` `silentRefresh().onSuccess { if childLinkId>0 selectChild(childLinkId) }`), and (b) **mid-session** (`RetrofitClient.refreshAccessToken` now, for a parent, calls `/mobile/auth/parent/select-child` with the refreshed token + stored linkId and returns that child-context token). linkId is stable across promotions (link upsert by admission_no, Phase 18), so re-selecting is safe. Android-only, compile-verified; ships in the next build. Separately confirmed (user issue): a phone stuck on an old versionCode despite a bumped Gradle is a Play **review/rollout/track** matter, not a build bug — the built AAB's versionName/versionCode are correct (verify with `aapt2 dump xmltree --file base/manifest/AndroidManifest.xml <aab>` / sideload `assemble<Flavor>Release` APK).
Phase 71 ✅  Mobile update gate made opt-in + manual "Update App" button — Phase 57's launch-time force/soft update prompt fired for every published app the moment `min`/`latest` versionCodes were configured, with no way to disable the auto-prompt without un-arming the row. Added an `auto_update_enabled BIT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` column to `ascent_master.app_config` (migration `Database/app_config_auto_update_migration.sql`, idempotent; **defaults 0 = OFF for all existing rows so auto-prompts stop immediately on apply**). `AppConfigDto` gains `AutoUpdateEnabled`; `AppConfigRepository.GetConfig` selects it; `MobileAppConfigController.GetConfig` now ANDs both computed booleans with `cfg.AutoUpdateEnabled` — when the flag is 0 the server always returns `updateRequired=updateAvailable=false` regardless of the reported versionCode (still **fails open** when no config row exists). Turn auto-prompts back on per app with `UPDATE app_config SET auto_update_enabled=1 WHERE application_id='...' AND platform='android'`. **Android:** `AppConfigRepository.kt` (`checkVersion()` → `UpdateStatus.Ok/Soft/Forced`, fail-open on any error) reads `BuildConfig.APPLICATION_ID`/`VERSION_CODE`; `SmsAuthScreen` login footer now shows `Version {VERSION_NAME} (build {VERSION_CODE})` and a manual **"Update App"** `TextButton` → `openPlayStore(context, null)` (always available regardless of the flag, so users can self-update even when auto-prompts are off). `app/build.gradle.kts` `versionCode` 10→18, `versionName` "1.1"→"1.7" (release signing config from Phase 47 still applies). Auto-prompt UI (`ForceUpdateScreen`/`UpdateAvailableDialog`, Phase 57) unchanged — just gated by the new flag.
Phase 87 ✅  Duplicate controller-name routing fix (control branding logo upload + R2 endpoints were 404) — the control app's branding logo upload failed with `Unexpected token '<', "<!DOCTYPE "... is not valid JSON` — the raw `fetch` to `POST /control/uploads/image` got an **HTML 404 page** and the frontend called `res.json()` before checking `res.ok`, masking the real status. Root cause: **two controller classes were both named `UploadsController`** — `Controllers/Control/UploadsController.cs` (`[RoutePrefix("control/uploads")]`, the pre-existing branding uploader) and `Controllers/School/UploadsController.cs` (`[RoutePrefix("school")]`, added in Phase 84 for R2). ASP.NET **Web API resolves controllers by their simple class name**, so two controllers sharing a name collide and **neither's attribute routes get registered** → every route on both 404s (confirmed live: `control/uploads/image`, `school/uploads/presign`, `school/media` all returned `HTTP 404 text/html`). This silently meant the **entire R2 feature (Phase 84/85) was dead** on the server, not just branding. Fix: renamed the classes to unique names — `ControlUploadsController` and `SchoolUploadsController` (+ its constructor) — **route templates unchanged** (they're defined by `[RoutePrefix]`/`[Route]`, not the class name), so no frontend URL changes. After redeploy both routes return `HTTP 401 application/json` (reachable; 401 only = no token). **Rule: controller class names must be globally unique across the whole API project, even in different namespaces/folders** — when adding a controller, don't reuse a bare name like `UploadsController`/`SettingsController`; prefix it (`SchoolUploadsController`). Backend-only fix; API redeployed. (No control-app rebuild — there's no `deploy-control.ps1`; control app is deployed manually to `admin.edu-care.in`, but this fix didn't need it.)
Phase 88 ✅  Mobile explicit refresh token — fix "logged out after ~1 day" (Android session persistence) — the 365-day mobile session (Phase 80) never worked in the field: users were back at the OTP mobile-number screen after ~1 day. Root cause: staying logged in depends on the **8-hour access token** (`Jwt:AccessTokenMins`) + a **successful silent refresh**, and the refresh endpoint read the refresh token **only from the HttpOnly `parentRefreshToken` cookie** (`GetRefreshCookie`). On Android that cookie only survives app-kill via the `PersistentCookieJar` (serializing an HttpOnly cookie to SharedPreferences, Phase 34/70) — a fragile round-trip (host-only/domain semantics lost on cookie rebuild, prefs can clear). When it fails, the overnight cold-start `silentRefresh()` 401s → `tokenStore.clear()` → OTP screen. The 365-day cookie/DB expiry were correct server-side but never got a chance to matter. **Fix — the app holds its own refresh token and sends it explicitly** (standard native pattern; stops depending on the cookie): **Backend:** `MobileAuthResponse`/`TeacherAuthResponse` gain `RefreshToken` (returned in the JSON body from `BuildParentAuthResponse`/`BuildStudentAuthResponse`/`BuildTeacherAuthResponse`); the parent/student/teacher refresh endpoints now read the token via a new `ResolveRefreshToken(cookieName)` = **`X-Refresh-Token` header FIRST, then cookie** (header wins because the app-held copy is the reliable one — a stale cookie rotated out from under the app would otherwise false-401; web clients send no header so they fall back to the cookie unchanged). Cookie is still set/rotated for web/back-compat. **Android:** `TokenStore.refreshToken` in EncryptedSharedPreferences (cleared on logout via `clear()`); saved from every login/verify-OTP/refresh response (**server ROTATES on each refresh — must re-save the new value or the next refresh reuses a revoked token**); `ApiService.refreshParentToken`/`refreshTeacherToken` gained an `@Header("X-Refresh-Token")` param; `AuthRepository.silentRefresh`/`silentRefreshTeacher` pass the stored token + persist the rotated one; `RetrofitClient.refreshAccessToken` (the OkHttp `Authenticator` 401 path) sends the header + saves the rotated `refreshToken` from the body. `PersistentCookieJar` kept as belt-and-suspenders but no longer load-bearing. `versionCode` 21→**22**, `versionName` "2.0"→**"2.1"**. **One-time transition:** first launch after update sends an empty header → server falls back to the still-valid cookie (if present) and the app captures the body token seamlessly; if the cookie is already dead, one OTP re-login then sets it for 365 days. No DB/CORS change (browser web app never sends the header). API compiles (MSBuild Release). Android edits compile-ready (DTOs under `data.api.**` already ProGuard-kept). Deploy API first, then ship APK.
Phase 89 ✅  Mobile update gate → Play In-App Updates (replaces the app_config versionCode gate) — the Phase 57/71 custom gate compared a server-held `app_config.latest_version_code` against `BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE`; when the DB number was set ahead of the versionCode Play actually serves a device (e.g. a build not yet propagated / in staged rollout / never uploaded), it produced a false "update available" every launch, and tapping Update went to Play which showed **"Open"** (nothing newer) → an unwinnable loop. Root lesson: a hand-maintained server version number inevitably drifts from what Play serves per-device (propagation is gradual — ~95% within 24h, not instant, even at full rollout). **Fix: switch to Google's official Play In-App Updates API (`AppUpdateManager`)**, which asks Play **directly** whether a newer build is available to *this* device — structurally impossible to false-prompt. **New:** `ui/update/InAppUpdater.kt` (wraps `AppUpdateManagerFactory`; `checkForUpdate()` → if `UpdateAvailability.UPDATE_AVAILABLE`, picks **IMMEDIATE** when `updatePriority()>=4` OR `clientVersionStalenessDays()>=7`, else **FLEXIBLE**; registers an `ActivityResultContracts.StartIntentSenderForResult` launcher + an `InstallStateUpdatedListener`; exposes `flexibleDownloaded: State<Boolean>` for a Compose "Restart to install" prompt → `completeUpdate()`; `onResume()` resumes an interrupted IMMEDIATE + surfaces a downloaded FLEXIBLE). **`MainActivity`:** constructs `InAppUpdater(this)` in `onCreate` (launcher must register before STARTED) + calls `checkForUpdate()`; overrides `onResume`/`onDestroy`; removed the old `AppConfigRepository.checkVersion()`/`updateStatus`/`ForceUpdateScreen`/`UpdateAvailableDialog` gate (loading now gates only on `isCheckingSession`); shows the flexible-downloaded `AlertDialog`. **Removed:** `data/repository/AppConfigRepository.kt` (+ `UpdateStatus`) — now dead; `UpdateScreens.kt` trimmed to just `openPlayStore()` (still used by the login-screen manual "Update App" button + `InAppUpdater.openInPlayStore()`; `ForceUpdateScreen`/`UpdateAvailableDialog` deleted). **build.gradle.kts:** `com.google.android.play:app-update:2.1.0` + `app-update-ktx:2.1.0`; `proguard-rules.pro` gains a defensive `-keep class com.google.android.play.core.**`. **Server side untouched & now unused for the gate:** `app_config` table, `MobileAppConfigController`, `AppConfigRepository`, `ApiService.getAppConfig` all remain (harmless; can be retired later). **Testing caveat:** in-app updates ONLY work when installed from Google Play or **Internal App Sharing** — a sideloaded/`adb install`/debug build always reports "no update", so this can't be verified from Android Studio run; use Internal App Sharing. Compile-verified (`gradle :app:compileStannsasfReleaseKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL). `versionCode` stays **22** (Phase 88 bump, not yet released). Android-only; no API/DB deploy needed. Ships in the next APK.
Phase 90 ✅  Teacher class/section announcements (mobile Feature 1) — teachers can post announcements to their class from the app, optionally targeting a single section; parents only see section-targeted ones for their child's section (class-wide reach everyone). **DB:** nullable `section_id INT FK → sections` added to `announcements` (`tenant_tables.sql` + idempotent `Database/announcements_section_migration.sql`; NULL = whole class). **Backend:** School `AnnouncementDto`/`SaveAnnouncementRequest` gain `SectionId` (+ `SectionName`); `AnnouncementsRepository` Create/Update/Get carry it (Get LEFT JOINs `sections`). Parent read: `MobileDataRepository.GetAnnouncements` gained a `sectionId` param filtering `section_id IS NULL OR = @sectionId`; `MobileStudentController.GetAnnouncements` resolves the child's section via the existing `GetStudentSectionId` (read-time, NOT via token — no login-flow change). New teacher endpoints on `MobileTeacherController`: `GET /mobile/teacher/announcements?classId=` + `POST /mobile/teacher/announcements` (scope forced 'Class', section optional) + `TeacherCreateAnnouncementRequest` DTO. **Web:** `AnnouncementsPage.jsx` gains a Section dropdown (loads per class; "All Sections (whole class)"/`allowClear`; shown when scope=Class); scope column shows section. **Android:** `TeacherAnnouncementDto`/`TeacherCreateAnnouncementRequest`, `ApiService`+`TeacherRepository`+`TeacherViewModel` announcement methods, new `TeacherAnnouncementScreen` (section picker → title/body → Post; list of existing), "Announcements" card on `TeacherHomeScreen` (Campaign icon), `MainActivity` `TeacherScreen.Announcement` route. `versionCode` 25→26, `versionName` "2.5"→"2.6". API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) + Android (`:app:compileStannsasfReleaseKotlin`) all compile-verified. **Not yet deployed** — run `announcements_section_migration.sql` per tenant DB → deploy API → deploy school app → ship APK.
Phase 91 ✅  Push notifications — FCM (mobile Feature 3) — parents get a push when new content is posted; sent server-side from EVERY creation path so web + mobile both notify. Uses **FCM HTTP v1** (legacy server-key API retired). **DB (MASTER):** `device_push_tokens` (one row per device install; parent OR teacher owns each token; `fcm_token` UNIQUE upsert target) — `master_tables.sql` (table 17) + idempotent `Database/device_push_tokens_migration.sql` (**run ONCE on ascent_master, NOT per tenant**). **Backend:** `DevicePushTokenRepository` (master; upsert parent/teacher token, delete, resolve parent tokens by student-set or whole-school via `parent_children` join); `FcmPushService` (Helpers) — **hand-rolls the v1 OAuth, no SDK** (house style, cf. R2 SigV4): RS256-signed JWT from the service account → Google token endpoint → `POST /v1/projects/{projectId}/messages:send` per token; manual PKCS#8 key parse + `RSACng` SHA256 signing; access token cached ~55m; reads the JSON path from Web.config `Fcm:ServiceAccountPath`; **unconfigured/missing → silent no-op (never breaks a create)**. `PushNotifier` (Helpers) — resolves target parent tokens (class/section → current-year students → parents; or school-wide) and fans out **fire-and-forget on `Task.Run`, capturing dbName/groupId/schoolId on the request thread** (never touches `TenantContext` inside the task — Phase 61 rule). Triggers wired into `AnnouncementsController` (web create), `HomeworkController` (create + `/batch`), `SchoolEventsController` (create), and `MobileTeacherController` teacher-announcement create. Register endpoints: `MobileNotificationsController` (`POST /mobile/notifications/register-token` | `unregister-token`, no auth attribute — reads `MobileContext`/`TeacherContext` populated by the global filter, accepts parent OR teacher). **Secret handling:** service-account JSON moved to `API/AscentSchools.API/App_Data/firebase-service-account.json` (**IIS denies HTTP access to App_Data** so it's never served); added as csproj Content (`PreserveNewest`) so it deploys; **gitignored** (`**/firebase-service-account.json`); `google-services.json` also gitignored. **Android:** `com.google.gms.google-services` plugin (root + app) + `firebase-bom:33.5.1` + `firebase-messaging`; `push/AscentFirebaseMessagingService` (onNewToken re-register if logged-in; onMessageReceived → local notification); channel `ascent_default` (`AscentApp.onCreate` + manifest default-channel meta-data); `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` permission + runtime prompt on Android 13+ (`MainActivity` launcher); `data/repository/PushRepository` (fetch FCM token via `suspendCancellableCoroutine`, no play-services-await dep) called on login success + cold-start refresh, unregister on logout/change-school; `ApiService`+`ApiModels` register/unregister; `versionCode` 26→27, `versionName` "2.6"→"2.7"; defensive proguard keep for `com.google.firebase.**` + `push.**`. See **`FIREBASE_SETUP.md`** (repo root) for the console setup + how to add a new white-label app (new `google-services.json`, same service account). API (MSBuild Release) + Android (compile) verified. **Not yet deployed** — run `device_push_tokens_migration.sql` on master → deploy API (ensure the App_Data JSON uploads) → ship APK. **Testing:** FCM works on any install (unlike Play In-App Updates), so push is testable from Android Studio via a device's FCM token / console *Send test message*. **Play Data safety:** declare FCM token = "Device or other IDs".
Phase 92 ✅  Class-teacher assignment + parent↔teacher messaging (mobile Feature 2) — parents chat with their child's class teacher(s); teachers reply from the app. **Prerequisite — class→teacher mapping (nothing mapped a class to a teacher before; the RBAC "Class Teacher" role binds nothing):** new tenant table `class_teacher_assignments` (academic_year_id, class_id, `section_id NULL = whole class`, user_id, school_id) — `tenant_tables.sql` table 49 + idempotent `Database/class_teacher_assignments_migration.sql`. **TWO filtered unique indexes** (`UQ_cta_section` WHERE section_id IS NOT NULL; `UQ_cta_class` WHERE section_id IS NULL) — a single index would silently allow the same teacher twice class-wide because SQL Server treats NULLs as distinct (decision #58 trap). `ClassTeacherRepository` (+ routing helpers `GetTeachersForClass`, `GetAssignmentsForTeacher`); `ClassTeachersController` `[RoutePrefix("school/class-teachers")]`; web `ClassTeachersTab.jsx` in Master Data (after Sections). Unassign = hard DELETE. **Decisions (user):** multiple teachers per class+section allowed (shared thread, any can reply); no teacher assigned → parent is blocked with a clear message (no school-inbox fallback); the teacher class dropdown for attendance/homework/announcements stays UNRESTRICTED (assignment drives messaging only — deploying can't lock live teachers out). **Messaging DB:** tenant tables 50-52 `message_threads`, `messages`, `message_reports` + idempotent `Database/messaging_migration.sql` (run AFTER the class_teacher migration). **Threads key on `student_unique_id`, NOT `student_id`** — student_id is a fresh IDENTITY each year (decision #54), so keying on it would fork every conversation at promotion; routing is computed LIVE from the child's current-year class+section, so a promoted child's thread follows them to the new class teacher with zero migration. One thread per (child, parent) — a single continuous chat, no subjects; the parent side is scoped to the selected child so it needs no thread list. `parent_id`/`sender_id` point at `ascent_master.parent_accounts` — cross-DB, so **no FK**; `sender_name` is a display snapshot so history survives a staff member leaving. `read_at` = when the OTHER side first read it (any assigned teacher reading marks it read). **Backend:** `MessagingRepository`; `MobileMessagesController` (`mobile/messages`, `[MobileAuth(requireChildContext:true)]` — GET one-call chat view [canMessage/reason/teachers/messages], POST send, read, report, block, unblock); `MobileTeacherMessagesController` (`mobile/teacher/messages`, `[MobileTeacherAuth]` — thread list, GET/POST reply/read/report/block/unblock per `{threadId}`, every op guarded by `TeacherCanAccessThread`); `MessageReportsController` (`school/message-reports`, admin review) gated on the **existing SETTINGS.MANAGE — deliberately no new permission code**, which would need a perms migration + forced re-login (the Phase 50/53 trap). **Push:** `DevicePushTokenRepository.GetTokensForParent`/`GetTokensForTeachers` + `PushNotifier.NotifyTeachers`/`NotifyParent` (fire-and-forget, primitives captured on the request thread — Phase 61). **Play UGC (required for release):** report (message-level → `message_reports`), block (thread-level; only the side that blocked may unblock — a teacher can lift a school block but not a parent's), and admin removal (`messages.status='Removed'` → shown as "This message was removed." both sides) via the new **Settings → Reported Messages** page (`pages/settings/MessageReportsPage.jsx`, `/settings/message-reports`). **Android:** parent `ui/messages/MessagesViewModel`+`MessagesScreen` (8th "Messages" tab on `HomeScreen`; recipient header, bubbles, long-press → report, block/unblock, composer disabled with the server's reason when no teacher is assigned); teacher `TeacherMessagesScreen` (inbox w/ unread count) + `TeacherChatScreen` (reply, overflow block/unblock, long-press report), `TeacherScreen.Messages`/`.Chat(threadId)` routes, "Messages" `ActionCard` on `TeacherHomeScreen` (outside the class picker — the inbox spans all assigned classes). `versionCode` 27→**28**, `versionName` "2.7"→**"2.8"**. **Privacy pages updated** (chakin + stannsasf + holyspiritjm + depaul): new "Messages" + "Push notification token" collect bullets and a "3. Messaging and Reported Content" section — note the **FCM token was never disclosed by Phase 91**, fixed here. API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) + Android (`:app:compileAscentReleaseKotlin`, `--rerun-tasks`) all compile-verified. **Not yet deployed** — run `class_teacher_assignments_migration.sql` then `messaging_migration.sql` per tenant DB → deploy API → deploy school app → assign class teachers in Master Data (messaging stays off for any class with none) → ship APK. **Play Data safety: declare "Messages / other in-app messages" (linked to user).** **Not built:** push deep-link — a message notification opens the app, not the thread (`type`/`entityId` ride in the FCM data block but nothing reads them; `AscentFirebaseMessagingService` has no extras plumbing). **Keyboard-overlap fix (during testing):** both chat screens (`MessagesScreen`, `TeacherChatScreen`) got `Modifier.imePadding()` on their root — the app is edge-to-edge (`enableEdgeToEdge()`), where `windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize"` no longer resizes content on its own, so without imePadding the soft keyboard covered the message composer. Material3 `Scaffold` already consumes the nav-bar inset, so imePadding lifts the composer to sit exactly above the keyboard.
Phase 93 ✅  Homework "due date" retired (all layers) — schools didn't use it. Column kept but made NULLABLE and no longer collected or shown. **DB:** `homework.due_date` NOT NULL → NULL (`tenant_tables.sql` + idempotent `Database/homework_due_date_nullable_migration.sql`; the `IX_homework_class_due` index is left as-is, harmless with NULLs). **API — DueDate made `DateTime?` everywhere it's mapped** (this is the load-bearing part: `due_date` now returns NULL and Dapper THROWS mapping NULL → non-nullable `DateTime`): school `HomeworkDto`+`SaveHomeworkRequest.DueDate`, mobile `HomeworkDto.DueDate`, and the private `MobileDataRepository.HomeworkRow.DueDate` (would have crashed the parent homework tab otherwise). `HomeworkController.CreateHomework` drops the "Due date is required" 400; single-create inserts NULL (client omits it); `CreateBatchHomework` now inserts `(DateTime?)null` (was `= assignedDate`). Both homework list reads re-ordered `ORDER BY due_date DESC` → `assigned_date DESC, homework_id DESC` (school `HomeworkRepository.GetHomework` + `MobileDataRepository.GetHomework` — NULLs would otherwise sink new homework to the bottom). **Dashboard:** the due-date-based "Upcoming Homework" card is **repurposed to "Recent Homework"** (user decision) — `DashboardRepository` query now `TOP 7 ... ORDER BY assigned_date DESC` (no `due_date >= today` filter); DTO renamed `UpcomingHomeworkDto`→`RecentHomeworkDto` (`DueDate`→`AssignedDate`), property `UpcomingHomework`→`RecentHomework` (JSON key `recentHomework`); `DashboardPage.jsx` retitled + uses `assignedDate`. **Reports:** both Homework reports hit `GET /school/reports/homework-statement`, which **filtered AND grouped by due_date** — re-based on `assigned_date` (`ReportsRepository.GetHomeworkStatement` WHERE/ORDER by assigned_date; `HomeworkStatementRowDto.DueDate` removed); `HomeworkStatementReport.jsx` groups by assignedDate + "Due:"→"Date:" labels + "Due From/To"→"From/To" pickers + Due Date dropped from CSV; `SubjectHomeworkReport.jsx` Due Date column removed from table+PDF(+columnStyles reindexed)+CSV, picker relabeled. **Teacher mobile app (user decision — remove too):** `MobileTeacherController.CreateHomework` drops the dueDate validation + sends `DueDate=null`; `TeacherCreateHomeworkRequest` loses `DueDate` (both C# DTO + Kotlin `ApiModels`); Android `TeacherHomeworkScreen` create sheet loses the date-picker field + dialog (and the now-dead `Modifier.clickable` helper / date imports), `HomeworkCard` no longer shows "Due:"; `TeacherViewModel.createHomework(classId,title,desc)` drops the `dueDate` param+validation; `TeacherHomeworkDto.dueDate` → `String?`. **Parent mobile app:** `HomeworkScreen` drops the "Due: …" line (`HomeworkDto.dueDate` already `String?`). **Web add-homework form (`HomeworkPage.jsx`):** Due Date DatePicker removed (Assigned Date now full-width), openCreate/openEdit/handleSave no longer set/send `dueDate`, Due Date table column removed, `Tag` import dropped. `versionCode` stays **29** (unreleased — rides with Phase 92 messaging + the IME fix). API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) + Android (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin`) all compile-verified. **Deploy:** run `homework_due_date_nullable_migration.sql` per tenant DB → deploy API → deploy school app → ship APK. Existing homework rows keep their historical due_date; only new rows are NULL.
Phase 94 ✅  Homework web list — server-side pagination + assigned_date indexes (unbounded-growth fix) — the admin `GET /school/homework` returned EVERY non-cancelled homework row for the school (only the AntD table paged client-side), so the payload grew without limit as years of homework accumulated. (Mobile was already fine — `MobileDataRepository.GetHomework` is `SELECT TOP (@count)` with count=20.) **Backend:** new `HomeworkRepository.GetHomeworkPaged(dbName, schoolId, classId, sectionId, assignedDate, page, pageSize)` → `(IEnumerable<HomeworkDto> Items, int Total)`; two Dapper calls — `ExecuteScalar<int>` COUNT + a `Query<HomeworkDto>` with `OFFSET @skip ROWS FETCH NEXT @take ROWS ONLY` (page clamped ≥1, pageSize clamped 1..200). **Deliberately NOT `QueryMultiple`** — Dapper's `GridReader` implements `IAsyncDisposable`, which on .NET Framework 4.8 makes a `using` block fail to compile (`CS0012 Microsoft.Bcl.AsyncInterfaces` not referenced); two separate queries avoid the GridReader entirely (COUNT is cheap with the new index). `HomeworkController.GetHomework` now takes `[FromUri] classId, sectionId, assignedDate, page=1, pageSize=20` and returns `{ items, total, page, pageSize }` (shape change — was a bare list). The old `HomeworkRepository.GetHomework(dbName, schoolId, classId)` is KEPT unchanged for `MobileTeacherController` (different endpoint, not paged). **Optional sectionId/assignedDate filters** were added so `DailyHomeworkPage` (the other consumer of `/school/homework`) can fetch one class+section+date precisely instead of pulling all history and filtering client-side — it now calls `?classId=&sectionId=&assignedDate=&pageSize=200` and reads `data.items`. **Frontend `HomeworkPage.jsx`:** added `page`/`pageSize`/`total` state; `loadHomework(classId, p, ps)` reads `data.items`+`data.total`; Table pagination is now controlled (`current/pageSize/total/showSizeChanger/showTotal/onChange`); class-filter change → page 1; create → jump to page 1 (newest); edit/delete → stay on current page. **DB indexes (Phase 94):** dropped obsolete `IX_homework_class_due` (indexed the retired due_date) and added `IX_homework_school_assigned (school_id, assigned_date DESC, homework_id DESC)` for the paged web list + `IX_homework_class_assigned (class_id, school_id, status, assigned_date DESC)` for the mobile feed — both match the `ORDER BY assigned_date DESC` so the top-N is a seek, not a scan+sort. `tenant_tables.sql` updated + idempotent `Database/homework_index_migration.sql`. API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) compile-verified; no mobile change. **Deploy:** run `homework_index_migration.sql` per tenant DB → deploy API → deploy school app. (Ships alongside Phase 93.)
Phase 96 ✅  Mobile "re-asks OTP every day" — REAL fix (server-only) + stable refresh token + 15-day mobile access token — the daily OTP re-login survived Phase 80 (365-day refresh) AND Phase 88 (app-held `X-Refresh-Token` header) because the true cause was elsewhere. **Root cause:** `JwtAuthenticationFilter` is a **global `IAuthenticationFilter`** that runs on EVERY request incl. `[AllowAnonymous]` ones; when a Bearer token was present but **expired**, it set `context.ErrorResult = UnauthorizedResult(...)` → **401 short-circuited the request before the action ran**. On cold start the Android app's main OkHttp interceptor **always** attaches `Authorization: Bearer <access token>`, and by morning that 8h access token is expired — so `silentRefresh()` → `parent/refresh` carried the expired token → the global filter 401'd it → `ParentRefresh` never read the valid `X-Refresh-Token` → app `tokenStore.clear()` → OTP screen, **every day**. Why it hid: **login** works (no token yet after clear → no header), **mid-session refresh** works (uses the separate `refreshClient` with NO interceptor → no stale token); only the cold-start refresh carried the expired token. **Fix (JwtAuthenticationFilter.cs):** an invalid/expired token is now treated like "no token" — just `return;` WITHOUT setting `ErrorResult` (both the `principal == null` and `claims == null` branches). Rejection is the auth attribute's job — `[SchoolAuth]`/`[MobileAuth]`/etc. still 401 when their context is unset, so protected endpoints are unchanged; `[AllowAnonymous]` endpoints (refresh, branding, login, webhook, `/version`) now run even if a stale Bearer is attached. **Rule: an authentication filter must NEVER set ErrorResult for a malformed/expired token — it runs on AllowAnonymous too.** **Retroactive:** server-only, NO new APK — every build-29 phone stays logged in on its next open (its stored 365-day refresh token is still valid; the refresh is simply no longer blocked → no re-OTP even once). **Hardening shipped in the same deploy (also server-only):** (1) **Stable mobile refresh token** — `MobileAuthController.ParentRefresh` + `MobileTeacherAuthController.Refresh` no longer ROTATE (revoke+recreate) the refresh token on each use; they call the new `ExtendRefreshToken(tokenId, expiry)` (added to `MobileParentAuthRepository` + `SchoolAuthRepository`) to slide `expires_at` forward (sliding 365-day window) and return the SAME token — removes the "device failed to persist the rotated token → logout" failure class entirely. The web school/control refresh endpoints STILL rotate (different endpoints, unchanged). (2) **Mobile access token 8h → 15 days** — new `Jwt:MobileAccessTokenMins` (Web.config `21600`; `JwtHelper.MobileAccessMins` **code-default 21600** so it works even if Web.config isn't re-uploaded) used ONLY by the 3 mobile token generators (`GenerateMobileParentToken`/`GenerateMobileStudentToken`/`GenerateMobileTeacherToken` → `BuildToken(claims, MobileAccessMins)`); web/control tokens still use `Jwt:AccessTokenMins` (480/8h) so staff/admin web sessions stay short + revocable. **Tradeoff (accepted):** an access token is a self-contained JWT and CANNOT be revoked before expiry, so a lost phone stays valid up to 15 days (the refresh token IS revocable via device-binding on next login). API built (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + deployed twice via full `deploy-api.ps1`; verified `GET /version` fresh each time. Files touched: `Filters/JwtAuthenticationFilter.cs`, `Helpers/JwtHelper.cs`, `Web.config`, `Controllers/Mobile/MobileAuthController.cs` + `MobileTeacherAuthController.cs`, `Repositories/Mobile/MobileParentAuthRepository.cs` + `Repositories/School/SchoolAuthRepository.cs`. No Android/APK change for any of it.
Phase 98 ✅  Fee payment date showed the PREVIOUS day on mobile — server-timezone fix (IST) + created_at skew — parents reported that a payment made from the Android app was stamped with yesterday's date. **Root cause:** the production server runs in **US Eastern (UTC-4/-5)** (see Phase 83) while schools are in India (UTC+5:30, ~9.5–10.5 h ahead), and the mobile order path stamped the date with **`DateTime.Today` = server-local Eastern** (`MobileFeesController.CreatePaymentOrder`, was line 118). So any payment made in India between midnight and ~9:30–10:30 AM IST landed on the *previous* calendar day (evening IST payments were fine — the server had rolled over by then), which is exactly the "sometimes previous date" symptom. The school web app was NEVER affected: its manual/online collection sends the browser's IST date via `FeeCollectionBase.jsx:213` (`paymentDate` = `dayjs()`), so only the **server-stamped** paths were wrong. **Fix (all server-side; the mobile app just displays what the API returns — no APK/web rebuild):** new **`Helpers/TimeHelper.cs`** (`IstNow()`/`IstToday()` via `TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById("India Standard Time")` — the server is Windows/IIS so that ID resolves; registered in `AscentSchools.API.csproj` per the old-style-csproj rule [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]). Replaced the four **server-stamped** date sources: (1) `MobileFeesController` order create `DateTime.Today` → `TimeHelper.IstToday()` *(the actual cause)*; (2) `MobileFeesController` verify fallback `?? DateTime.UtcNow` → `?? IstToday()`; (3) `FeeController.ReconcileOrderToReceipt` fallback `?? DateTime.UtcNow` → `?? IstToday()` (covers webhook/reconcile of mobile-originated orders); (4) `FeeRepository.CollectFee` INSERT — the SQL `payment_date` fallback `CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)` → `CAST(SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time' AS DATE)`. **created_at skew (also fixed, same root cause):** `fee_receipts.created_at` relied on the table's server-local `GETDATE()` default (skews the dashboard "Recent Receipts" tiebreak, the EOD-sync `?createdAfter`, and the "Created after" receipt filter by ~10 h) — the `CollectFee` INSERT now sets `created_at` **explicitly** to `CAST(SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time' AS DATETIME)` so it's correct on deploy for every mobile/online/manual receipt. **`SYSDATETIMEOFFSET()` carries the server's real offset**, so `AT TIME ZONE` is correct regardless of the box's timezone; needs SQL Server 2016+ (already required by temporal `students`, Phase 79). **SQL (user runs — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]]):** `tenant_tables.sql` `fee_receipts` `payment_date` + `created_at` DEFAULTs changed to the IST expression (new tenant DBs); new idempotent **`Database/fee_receipts_ist_default_migration.sql`** drops the two auto-named default constraints and re-adds IST ones on existing tenant DBs (safety net for the DB-default fallback + legacy `BulkImportReceipts`, which still relies on the default — the main fee path is already fixed in code, so the migration is non-blocking). **API built (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`, 35 files); verified `GET /version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-07-25 05:04 UTC`. **Rule: any server-STAMPED business date (payment_date, created_at, etc.) must be computed in IST via `TimeHelper.IstToday()`/`IstNow()` (C#) or `SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time'` (SQL) — never `DateTime.Today`/`DateTime.Now`/`GETDATE()`, because the server runs US Eastern. Client-sent dates (browser `dayjs()`) are already IST and need no conversion.** **NOT done (forward-looking fix only):** existing receipts keep their wrong date; no back-correction script was run (some rows are legitimately back-dated/legacy-imported, so a blanket shift is unsafe — would need a targeted rule like `source='webapp'` online receipts where `payment_date < IST(created_at)`).
Phase 97 ✅  Mobile daily OTP re-login — the ACTUAL client-side cause (cold-start refresh wiped the session) + plain-prefs session storage — Phases 80 (365-day refresh), 88 (app-held `X-Refresh-Token`) and 96 (auth-filter fix + 15-day access token) all made the *token* last longer, but the **client threw the token away before the server ever got a say**, so users kept landing on the OTP mobile-number screen after a single night. **Root cause (`MainActivity.kt`):** the cold-start `LaunchedEffect(Unit)` awaited `silentRefresh()` behind a blocking `CircularProgressIndicator` and called `tokenStore.clear()` inside a single `.onFailure { }`. Because `silentRefresh()` is wrapped in `runCatching`, **every** failure collapsed into that one branch — no network yet (the common case: the phone has just woken from Doze and Wi-Fi hasn't reassociated when `LaunchedEffect` fires on process start), DNS failure, socket timeout, a 502 while IIS recycles the app pool, an unparseable body — all indistinguishable from "your refresh token is genuinely revoked". A perfectly valid 365-day session was deleted by a transient hiccup, **every morning**. `MainActivity.kt:146` was the ONLY involuntary logout in the app (all 7 other `tokenStore.clear()` call sites are deliberate user actions). Trivially reproducible: **airplane mode → open app → OTP screen**. Compounding: the blocking spinner sat behind a 75s `callTimeout`, so on a weak signal the user waited >1 min and *then* got logged out. **Fix — "local-first session" (chosen over making the refresh smarter):** the cold-start refresh is **deleted**, not repaired. Opening the app now does **zero blocking auth work** — no spinner, no network call, straight to home. Safe because nothing there was load-bearing: the stored access token **already carries child context** (`selectChild` → `saveSession` writes the child-context token), it is long-lived, and mid-session expiry is already handled by the OkHttp `Authenticator` (Phase 65), which retries and never clears state. Replaced by two narrow behaviours: (1) an **opportunistic** background refresh after the UI is up — new `AuthRepository.refreshSessionQuietly()`, fire-and-forget, purely to slide the server's 365-day window; it **rolls back to the previous token** if the post-refresh `selectChild` fails (the refresh endpoint returns a parent token WITHOUT child context, and committing a child-less token would break every data screen with "Please select a child first"); (2) **the one and only automatic logout** — `RetrofitClient.sessionExpired: MutableStateFlow<Boolean>`, raised ONLY when a live API call 401s **and** the follow-up refresh is explicitly rejected **401/403** (two independent rejections, so a flaky network can't trip it); `MainActivity` collects it and clears the session. Without (2) a genuinely dead session would strand the user in a working shell with every screen failing and no route back to login. **Server:** `Jwt:MobileAccessTokenMins` 15 days → **180 days** (`259200`; `JwtHelper.MobileAccessMins` **code-default also raised to 259200** so it holds even if Web.config isn't re-uploaded). With no cold-start refresh, this lifetime alone is what keeps a parent signed in between opens. Web/control still `Jwt:AccessTokenMins` (480/8h). **Tradeoff (accepted, same shape as Phase 96):** a lost phone stays valid up to 180 days; the refresh token remains revocable and device binding invalidates the old device on the next login. **Storage (`TokenStore.kt`) — secondary cause:** session data moved from `EncryptedSharedPreferences` to **app-private plain `SharedPreferences`** (`ascent_session_prefs`). `androidx.security-crypto` is **deprecated by Google**, and its Keystore-backed keyset is known to fail on some OEM devices and after a backup / device-to-device transfer; when it failed, the old code fell back to a **DIFFERENT, EMPTY prefs file** (`ascent_fallback_prefs`) which reads as "never logged in" — a silent logout with no error surfaced. App-private storage is unreadable by other apps or without root, and is where **Firebase Auth keeps its own tokens**. **Two one-time migrations** run in `init` so this build doesn't itself log everyone out: from the encrypted store, then (only if no access token was recovered) from the old fallback file — which catches exactly the users the bug hit hardest. Guarded by a `migrated_from_encrypted` flag that **`clear()` deliberately re-writes** — otherwise a logout would be undone by a re-import of the stale session on the next launch. `security-crypto` dependency is KEPT (needed for the migration read). **`AndroidManifest.xml`: `allowBackup` true → `false`** — a restored session belongs to a different device, which device binding rejects anyway, and restoring prefs whose Keystore key doesn't exist on the new device is a known cause of silent logouts (cost: a parent moving phones logs in once with OTP). `versionCode` 31→**32**, `versionName` "3.1"→**"3.2"**. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + Android (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin`, `:app:compileStannsasfReleaseKotlin`, `:app:compileAscentReleaseKotlin`, all exit 0) compile-verified. No DB migration. **Deploy order matters: API FIRST** (full `deploy-api.ps1` — `JwtHelper.cs` changed, not just Web.config), **then the APK**, so build 32's first opportunistic refresh mints a 180-day token. **Expect one final OTP login** on phones whose installed build predates versionCode 22 (Phase 88 never shipped, so they hold no refresh token) — after that the session is durable. **NOT built (deliberately out of the chosen scope, still open):** remembering the last mobile number across `clear()` (a re-login currently means retyping 10 digits) and recording the logout reason on the login screen for diagnosis. **Rule: a mobile app must never delete its own credentials because a network call failed — only an explicit server rejection (401/403) may end a session.**
Phase 99 ✅  AscentSyncTool — academic-year filter on legacy exports + version stamp — the two legacy-source tabs (Export Students, Export Receipts) now filter by academic year in addition to the date range; the Download Receipts tab (new-app API by `created_at`) is untouched. **`Data/LegacyDb.cs`:** new `GetAcademicYears()` → `SELECT DISTINCT AcademicYear FROM SAS_AcdYear WHERE AcademicYear IS NOT NULL AND LTRIM(RTRIM(AcademicYear)) <> '' ORDER BY AcademicYear DESC` (string DESC gives latest for the "YYYY-YY" format, matching how the app resolves "latest" elsewhere); `GetReceipts`/`GetStudents` gained an optional `string academicYear = null` param → `AND (@acdYear IS NULL OR AcdYear = @acdYear)` (receipts, col **`AcdYear`**) / `AND (@acdYear IS NULL OR StuAcdYear = @acdYear)` (students, col **`StuAcdYear`**); the `@acdYear IS NULL` guard is defensive (empty/no year → date-range only). **`SyncTab.cs`:** optional `Academic Year:` `ComboBox` (`DropDownStyle=DropDownList`) gated by a new `ShowAcademicYearFilter` bool (hidden by default); `SelectedAcademicYear` property (null when hidden/empty) + `SetAcademicYears(IEnumerable<string>)` which selects the **first (latest)** item; combo disabled while busy. **`MainForm.cs`:** `public const string Version = "1.1"` is the single source of truth (no `AssemblyInfo.cs` is compiled in this project) — shown in the title bar AND as a large bold navy header `Label` (`Segoe UI 18pt Bold`, docked Top above the `TabControl`); enables `ShowAcademicYearFilter` on `_tabStudents` + `_tabReceipts`; a `Load` handler calls `GetAcademicYears()` once and populates both legacy tabs (latest pre-selected), wrapped in try/catch so a legacy-DB failure shows a status message instead of crashing startup; both legacy `OnShow` callbacks pass `_tab.SelectedAcademicYear` into the query (no `OnShow` signature change). Needed `using System.Drawing;` in `MainForm.cs` for `Font`/`Color`/`FontStyle`/`ContentAlignment`. Built clean (MSBuild Debug, VS18). No API/DB change; redistribute the rebuilt `AscentSyncTool.exe`.
Phase 103 ✅  Admin "Conversations" viewer — read-only parent↔teacher message browsing in the school web app (new `MESSAGES.VIEW` permission) — schools wanted to review parent↔teacher chats from the web app, role-gated, with date + entity filters. **Reuses the entire messaging schema** (`message_threads`/`messages`/`class_teacher_assignments`) — no new tables, no mobile change. **Backend:** new `SchoolMessagesAdminController` `[RoutePrefix("school/messages")]` (registered in csproj per [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]), both endpoints enforced with `[RequirePermission(PermissionCodes.Messages.View)]` — **the FIRST endpoints to actually use `RequirePermissionAttribute`** (Phase 50 shipped the filter but left it unused; the app was frontend-gated only). `GET /school/messages/threads?academicYearId=&classId=&sectionId=&studentUniqueId=&teacherUserId=&dateFrom=&dateTo=` → filtered thread list (**defaults to the last 5 days** when the range is omitted; controller computes it with `DateTime.Today` — see the IST caveat below); `GET /school/messages/threads/{id}` → full conversation (reuses `GetThread`+`GetMessages`). New `MessagingRepository.GetThreadsForAdmin(...)` — one query: joins `students` on `student_unique_id + ISNULL(@academicYearId, currentYear)` so **class/section reflect the student's placement in the SELECTED year** (a promoted child appears under the class they were in that year; a thread whose student isn't placed in the selected year won't appear — user-accepted), teacher(s) via a `FOR XML PATH` concat of `class_teacher_assignments`, last-message body + count, and a date-window `EXISTS` on `messages.created_at`. New `ConversationListItemDto`. **Permission (`MESSAGES.VIEW`, user chose a dedicated code over reusing SETTINGS.MANAGE):** added to `PermissionCodes.cs` (`Messages.View`) + frontend `permissions.js` (`P.MESSAGES_VIEW` + `PATH_PERM['/messages/conversations']`) + `tenant_tables.sql` seed + idempotent `Database/messages_view_permission_migration.sql` (inserts the perm, maps to Principal). **Standard Phase-50 caveat: run the migration per tenant DB + users must LOG OUT/IN once** for the perm to land in their JWT (now also enforced server-side, so without it the endpoints 403). **Frontend:** `pages/messages/ConversationsPage.jsx` — filter bar (Academic Year default current, Class, Section, Student [by `student_unique_id`], Teacher [by `user_id`], date `RangePicker` default last 5 days), table (S.No/Student/Class·Section/Teachers/Last message/Last activity/#msgs/Status), row-click `Drawer` rendering the conversation read-only (parent-left/teacher-right bubbles, timestamps, removed messages greyed as "This message was removed."); nav item **"Conversations"** (`CommentOutlined`) + route in `App.jsx` (`Protected`) gated by `MESSAGES.VIEW`. **Read-only** (user decision) — moderation stays in Settings → Reported Messages; parent identity shown only via student + `sender_name` snapshots (no cross-DB `parent_accounts` lookup, user decision). **Privacy policy:** added a bullet to the Messaging section of all four per-school pages (chakin/stannsasf/holyspiritjm/depaul) — authorised school staff may view conversations for safety/moderation/record-keeping, access limited to granted roles, never used for ads or shared. **IST timestamp fix (shipped with this phase — Parts A+B of the IST plan, NOT the Part C back-correction):** messaging timestamps were stamped with **server-local `GETDATE()` = US Eastern** (Phase 98 fixed only *fee* dates), ~9.5–10.5 h off IST. Fixed forward-looking: `MessagingRepository` now stamps `created_at`/`last_message_at`/`read_at`/`blocked_at`/`reviewed_at` explicitly in IST via `CAST(SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time' AS DATETIME)` (shared `IstNow` const; `SendMessage`/`MarkRead`/`SetThreadBlocked`/`ResolveReport`/`GetOrCreateThread`/`ReportMessage`); the `created_at` column DEFAULTs on `message_threads`/`messages`/`message_reports` changed to the IST expr (`tenant_tables.sql` + idempotent `Database/messaging_ist_default_migration.sql`); and the viewer's default window uses `TimeHelper.IstToday()` instead of `DateTime.Today`. **No mobile change** — the chat screens just display `created_at`, so new messages show IST once the API is deployed. **Part C NOT done (deliberately, per user):** existing rows keep their Eastern-stamped `created_at` (a one-time precise Eastern→IST back-correction via double `AT TIME ZONE` is possible since all message times are server-generated, but was skipped). API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (vite) compile-verified. **Not yet deployed.** Deploy: run `messages_view_permission_migration.sql` + `messaging_ist_default_migration.sql` per tenant DB → `deploy-api.ps1` → `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` → `deploy-privacy.ps1` → assign `MESSAGES.VIEW` to roles + re-login. **Rule reinforced (Phase 98): server-stamped business timestamps must be IST (`SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time'` in SQL or `TimeHelper.IstNow()`/`IstToday()` in C#), never `GETDATE()`/`DateTime.Today` — the server runs US Eastern.**
Phase 104 ✅  Teacher homework (mobile staff login) — date + section on cards, server-side pagination, section-aware list, and a "Post to" section dropdown on Add Homework — four related changes to the teacher app's Homework screen; the backend already returned most of the data. **(1) Card shows date + section (Android):** the mobile `TeacherHomeworkDto` was missing `sectionName` (the endpoint via `HomeworkRepository.GetHomework` already returned `sectionName`+`assignedDate` — they were just dropped client-side). Added `sectionName` to the Kotlin DTO; `HomeworkCard` now shows a row `📅 dd-MM-yyyy · 🏫 {section|"All sections"}` (date formatted from the ISO string; `null` section → "All sections"). **(2) Server-side pagination (Option B) — the real fix for unbounded growth:** the teacher list showed EVERY non-cancelled homework for the class (`GET /mobile/teacher/homework` → unpaged `HomeworkRepository.GetHomework`), growing without limit as history accumulates (same problem Phase 94 fixed for the web admin list; the parent feed was already `TOP 20`). **Kept the legacy `GET /mobile/teacher/homework` route UNCHANGED** (bare list) so teachers on older APKs (<versionCode 36) aren't broken by the response-shape change, and added a NEW `GET /mobile/teacher/homework/paged?classId=&sectionId=&page=&pageSize=` returning `{ items, total, page, pageSize }`, backed by the existing `HomeworkRepository.GetHomeworkPaged` (OFFSET/FETCH + COUNT; the matching `assigned_date` index already exists from Phase 94). Android: `TeacherViewModel` accumulates pages (`loadHomework` resets+loads page 1, `loadMoreHomework()` appends with in-flight/overrun guards, `isLoadingMoreHomework` flow, page size 20); `TeacherHomeworkScreen` uses `rememberLazyListState` + a `snapshotFlow` watcher that calls `loadMoreHomework()` within 3 items of the end + a footer spinner. **(3) Section-aware list:** the teacher home Homework tile passed only `classId` (was class-scoped → showed ALL sections). Added an opt-in `bool includeClassWide=false` param to `GetHomeworkPaged` — when a `sectionId` is given AND the flag is set, the WHERE matches `section_id=@sectionId OR section_id IS NULL` (that section + class-wide); the web Daily Homework page leaves it false for its exact class+section match. The `/homework/paged` controller passes the teacher's selected `sectionId` with `includeClassWide:true`. Navigation now threads `sectionId` (home tile/card → `TeacherScreen.Homework(classId, sectionId)` → screen), so: section selected → that section + class-wide; class only → all. **(4) "Post to" section dropdown on Add Homework (posts to the section, not just class-wide):** teacher-created homework was ALWAYS class-wide (`section_id` NULL) — the create request had no section. Added `SectionId` (`int?`, NULL/0=whole class) to `TeacherCreateHomeworkRequest` (C# + Kotlin) → `MobileTeacherController.CreateHomework` passes it into `SaveHomeworkRequest.SectionId` (the repo INSERT already writes `section_id`). The `CreateHomeworkSheet` gained an `ExposedDropdownMenuBox` ("Post to": **Whole class (all sections)** + one item per section, loaded via `viewModel.loadSections(classId)`), defaulting to the home-screen section; `onSave` returns the chosen `sectionId`. **Also:** "Showing X of Y" caption above the list (`homework.size` of server `total`, live as you scroll); Homework card subtitle on the teacher home shows `Class · Section A` when a section is selected. **No DB change** (index pre-exists). `versionCode` 35→**36**, `versionName` "3.5"→**"3.6"**. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) built + **deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`, 35 files; `GET /version` → `1.1.0.0` `buildDate 2026-07-28 01:41 UTC`; `GET /mobile/teacher/homework/paged` → 401 = registered). Android `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (Demo flavor Kotlin only — **not** a signed AAB). **Deploy: API is LIVE; still PENDING — build/submit the per-flavor APK.** Order was API-first (additive; old apps keep the legacy route). Files: `Controllers/Mobile/MobileTeacherController.cs`, `Repositories/School/HomeworkRepository.cs`, Android `data/api/ApiModels.kt`+`ApiService.kt`, `data/repository/TeacherRepository.kt`, `ui/teacher/TeacherViewModel.kt`+`TeacherHomeworkScreen.kt`+`TeacherHomeScreen.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 105 ✅  Mobile tiles-view "News" ticker → latest 2 announcements as "date: notice" + announcements `created_at` stamped IST — the parent tiles-dashboard "News" marquee (`HomeScreen.kt`, tiles view only; teacher home has no ticker) previously joined **every** loaded announcement title with bullets. **Changed to show the latest TWO announcements** as `dd-MM-yyyy: {title}` (`sortedByDescending { createdAt }.take(2)`, each via new `tickerDate()` helper = `createdAt.take(10)` → `dd-MM-yyyy`; joined with bullets; fallback "Welcome to {school}." when none). **Deliberately NOT date-filtered** — an interim version filtered to *today only*, but a notice posted for a **future** day (e.g. tomorrow's holiday) would then be hidden, so it was replaced with "latest 2, unfiltered" so upcoming notices still show. The **Notices tab + its tile badge are unchanged** (still the full list) — only the ticker changed. **Backend IST fix (the load-bearing part for the displayed date):** `announcements.created_at` used `DEFAULT GETDATE()` = **US Eastern** (server runs Eastern; Phases 98/103 fixed only fee + messaging times), so the ticker's `date:` prefix would read ~9.5–10.5 h behind IST. `AnnouncementsRepository.CreateAnnouncement` now stamps `created_at` **explicitly** via `CAST(SYSDATETIMEOFFSET() AT TIME ZONE 'India Standard Time' AS DATETIME)` — this one repo method is shared by BOTH the school-web (`AnnouncementsController`) and teacher-mobile (`MobileTeacherController`) create paths, so both are covered. `tenant_tables.sql` `announcements.created_at` DEFAULT changed to the IST expr; new idempotent `Database/announcements_ist_default_migration.sql` (drops the auto-named default, re-adds `DF_announcements_created_at`; forward-looking — existing Eastern-stamped rows are NOT back-corrected). **The client does NO timezone math** — it just slices the date portion of the server's `created_at` string — so the date is correct **only** because the server now stamps IST; **deploy API before the APK** (a today's post would otherwise carry an Eastern date the ticker shows a day behind). **NOT built (out of scope):** an `event_date` column so a notice could carry its own holiday date — the ticker date is the **posted** date (`created_at`), not an event date; a holiday posted today *for* tomorrow shows today's date + whatever the title says. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) built + **deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`, 35 files; `GET /version` → `buildDate 2026-07-28 03:40 UTC`). Android `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. `versionCode` 36→**37**, `versionName` "3.6"→**"3.7"** (rides the same unreleased APK as Phase 104). **Deploy: API is LIVE; you run `announcements_ist_default_migration.sql` per tenant DB; then build/ship the APK (37).** **Rule reinforced (Phase 98/103): server-stamped business timestamps must be IST — never `GETDATE()`.** Files: `Repositories/School/AnnouncementsRepository.cs`, `Database/tenant_tables.sql` + `announcements_ist_default_migration.sql`, Android `ui/home/HomeScreen.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 106 ✅  Exam / marks build-out — class→subject mapping, grade types, exam master, marks-model change (`exam_id` + activity), web grid rewrite + mobile marks entry, reports on `exam_id` — built the exam+marks feature in stages. **Root gap:** `subjects` were year-scoped but NOT class-scoped, so the marks grid loaded EVERY school subject for every class; the migrate-only `exam_master`/`grade_types` tables had no UI/API. **(1) `class_subjects` (new tenant table 53):** which subjects a class studies in a year (`academic_year_id`,`class_id`,`subject_id`,`display_order`,`is_optional`,`status`; single `UNIQUE(school_id,academic_year_id,class_id,subject_id)` — all key cols NOT NULL, no filtered-index NULL trap). `ClassSubjectRepository`/`ClassSubjectsController` (`school/class-subjects`: GET mapping, `available-subjects?academicYearId=` [year-scoped, for the mapping form], `for-class?classId=` [**no year filter** — distinct subjects across all years, for exam setup], POST bulk-replace). Web **Master Data → Class Subjects** tab (`ClassSubjectsTab.jsx`): pick Year+Class → editable Subject/Order/Elective rows → Save replaces the set. **User decision: NO `max_marks`/`pass_marks` on class_subjects** (those live on the exam). **(2) Grade Types (`grade_types` CRUD):** new `GradeTypeRepository`/`GradeTypesController` (`school/grade-types`: GET `?subjectId=`, POST/PUT/DELETE) + `GradeTypesTab.jsx` (Grade Name/Subject[optional=all]/Min/Max/Grade/Remarks/Status). **Single source of truth** — removed the duplicate grade-types read from `ExamMaster` and repointed the exam form's grade dropdown to `school/grade-types`. **(3) Exam Master form + 2 columns:** `exam_master` gained **`exam_name VARCHAR(200)`** + **`activity_max_marks DECIMAL(6,2)`** (set = this subject-exam has an activity component). `ExamMasterRepository`/`ExamMasterController` (`school/exam-master`: GET `?academicYearId=&examTypeId=&classId=`, POST [one row per selected subject; **plain INSERT with an IF-NOT-EXISTS guard, NOT `MERGE`** — legacy dupes on the same key+different `exam_category` would make MERGE throw "matched more than once"], PUT/{id}, DELETE/{id}) + `ExamMasterTab.jsx` (Year+ExamType+Class filter → list w/ **per-row Edit + Delete**; Add modal multi-selects the class's subjects [`for-class`, no year filter] → one exam row per subject sharing header fields incl. Activity Max). **(4) `student_marks` model change (nullable, no backfill):** `+ exam_id` (FK → `exam_master.id`), `+ activity_marks`, `+ activity_max_marks`. `exam_id` FK added via `ALTER` AFTER `exam_master` (student_marks is tenant table 30, created before exam_master table 46 — inline FK impossible). Total semantics (**user decision**): `marks_obtained` = written/main (or total when no activity); **subject total = `marks_obtained + ISNULL(activity_marks,0)`**, max = `max_marks + ISNULL(activity_max_marks,0)`. `exam_id` is stored on save but the report re-point (below) is the only reader for now. **(5) Shared marks backend rewrite (`MarksRepository`):** grid subjects now come from `class_subjects` (a class with none shows an empty grid → "map subjects first"); per-subject `max`/`activity max`/`exam_id` resolved from the matching `exam_master` row via a shared `OUTER APPLY` (falls back to max 100 / no activity when the exam isn't defined). `SaveMarks` upserts `exam_id`+`activity_marks`+`activity_max_marks`+per-subject `max_marks`; **blank cells skipped** (no junk 0 rows).
