Added `GetSubjectMarks` (single subject, mobile) + `GetCurrentAcademicYearId`. `SaveMarksRequest` moved MaxMarks per-entry (dropped the request-level MaxMarks + its controller check). **(6) Web `MarksEntryPage` rewrite:** Year·Exam·Class·Section (removed the global Max Marks box); grid shows **Activity + Marks boxes horizontally per subject** (Activity only when that subject-exam has Activity Max), header `Marks /100 · Act /20`, per-subject Absent. **(7) Mobile teacher Marks Entry (Android, staff login — mobile Feature):** new `MobileTeacherController` endpoints `exam-types`/`marks-subjects?classId=`/`marks?...&subjectId=`/POST `marks` (year resolved server-side — no mobile year picker). Android: `TeacherMarksScreen` (Exam + **Subject filter** [one subject at a time — a wide grid won't fit a phone] → student list → Marks field + Activity field [when the subject has activity] + Absent chip → Save), home Marks tile/card (needs class+section), `MainActivity` `TeacherScreen.Marks` route, ViewModel/Repository/ApiService/ApiModels marks additions. `versionCode` 38→**39**, `versionName` "3.8"→**"3.9"**. **(8) Marks reports repointed to `exam_id`** (`ReportsRepository`: `GetFailedStudents`/`GetClassToppers`/`GetExamToppers`/`GetAcademicYearToppers`): all totals now include the activity component (`+ ISNULL(activity_marks,0)` / `+ ISNULL(activity_max_marks,0)`); **Failed Students** now takes each subject's pass mark from `exam_master.subject_min_marks` (LEFT JOIN via `exam_id`) when present, else the old global pass-% fallback. **Fully backward-compatible** — existing rows have `exam_id`/`activity_*` NULL → `?? 0` + pass-% fallback reproduce the old numbers exactly; no report DTO/frontend change. **SQL (user runs — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]]):** `tenant_tables.sql` updated (new groups) + three idempotent migrations for existing tenant DBs — `Database/class_subjects_migration.sql`, `Database/exam_master_exam_name_migration.sql`, `Database/student_marks_exam_activity_migration.sql` (this one adds `exam_master.activity_max_marks` + `student_marks.exam_id`+FK+`activity_marks`+`activity_max_marks`). Deploy order: run the 3 migrations per tenant DB → API (`student_marks_exam_activity_migration.sql` is load-bearing — the new save/read/reports reference the new columns) → school app → ship APK (39). New API files registered in the old-style csproj [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]. **DEPLOYED:** user ran the 3 migrations; **API + all 6 school web apps deployed** (verified the new routes return 401 = registered, not 404). **STILL PENDING: the Android APK v39** (teacher Marks Entry) — build/sign per flavor + submit to Play (no gradlew wrapper in repo; Android Studio build). **NOT built (deferred, per user):** activity/written split on the student-facing report card / parent-mobile marks view; and adding `exam_name` to `student_marks` (user asked, analysis concluded it's redundant — `exam_id` already links to `exam_master.exam_name`; user said ignore/no change). **Follow-up tweaks (post-deploy, same phase):** (a) **Class Subjects subject picker no longer year-filtered** — `ClassSubjectRepository.GetAvailableSubjects` dropped the `academic_year_id` WHERE clause (returned empty → "Add Subject" disabled when the school's subjects were tagged to a different year); now returns ALL active school subjects. Subjects are a school-level master; the year lives on the `class_subjects` mapping, not the picker. (The Exam Master subject dropdown already used year-agnostic `for-class`; Grade Types uses `/school/master/subjects` — both fine.) (b) **Class Subjects prefill-all-when-empty** — `ClassSubjectsTab.loadMapping`: when a class has NO subjects mapped yet, the grid pre-fills with EVERY active subject (each a row); the user deletes the ones that class doesn't take, then Save. A class that already has a mapping loads exactly that (no overwrite). (c) **Grade Types bulk import** — `POST /school/grade-types/bulk` (`GradeTypeRepository.BulkCreate`, subject matched BY NAME [blank=all subjects; latest year on dup], bad rows reported not fatal, max 1000) + a "Bulk Import" modal on `GradeTypesTab` (download CSV template, papaparse upload, preview, import, per-row error table + download-errors). CSV cols: `GradeName,SubjectName,MinMarks,MaxMarks,Grade,Remarks,Status`. Existing single **Add Grade** kept. (d) **Exam Master bulk import** — `POST /school/exam-master/bulk` (`ExamMasterRepository.BulkImport`, all refs matched BY NAME: AcademicYear→year, ExamType [must exist for that year]→exam type, Class→class, Subject→subject [latest year on dup], GradeType [optional, first match]→grade type; **skips a row whose year+exam type+class+subject already exists** [same rule as single Add → `Skipped++`], max 2000) + a "Bulk Import" modal on `ExamMasterTab` (template/upload/preview/import/errors). CSV cols: `AcademicYear,ExamType,Class,Subject,ExamName,Category,ExamDate,TotalMarks,ExamMinMarks,SubjectMax,SubjectMin,ActivityMax,GradeType,Remarks,Status`. Both bulk imports reuse the shared `BulkImportResult`/`BulkRowError` (from `Students` DTO namespace) and needed a **typed** Dapper lookup class (NOT `dynamic` — the Data project doesn't reference `Microsoft.CSharp`, so `conn.Query(...)` untyped → `CS0656`). No schema change for either bulk feature; edited existing files (already csproj-registered). All follow-ups **compile-verified + deployed** (API + all 6 school web apps).
Phase 107 ✅  Mobile Calendar → almanac list view (Android-only, display change) — the parent **Calendar** tab (`ui/calendar/CalendarScreen.kt`, HomeScreen feature index 8; teacher has no calendar) shows the school-activity **calendar events** entered on the web **Calendar** page (`calendar` table via `CalendarController`/`CalendarRepository`; `CalendarEventDto` = Title/Description/**Category** [Holiday|Exam|Celebration|Event]/**StartDate..EndDate** range). It previously listed **only dates that had events** as coloured category cards ("No events this month" otherwise). **Reworked to the printed-diary almanac layout** (per a client reference image): **one row PER DAY of the month** — `DD · Weekday · - · Description` — with these rules (all **client-side derivations, no attendance, no API/DB change**): (a) **event(s) on a date → shown** (titles joined with ` · `; multi-day events fill every date in their `StartDate..EndDate` range via `containsDate`); (b) **Sunday with no event → "Holiday"**, rendered in red (`HolidayRed #D4380D` + light-red row tint) — **only Sunday is auto-defaulted** (a "Second Saturday - Holiday" etc. must be a school-entered Holiday-category calendar event, NOT an auto-rule); (c) **Sunday that ALSO has an event → shows the event**, still holiday-coloured (user decision); (d) **any Holiday-category event → red** too; (e) **everything else → "Working Day"** (muted grey); (f) footer **"Total Working Days : N"** = days-in-month − (Sundays ∪ Holiday-category-event days) — matches the reference (July 2026 = 31 − 4 Sundays − 1 second-Saturday holiday = 26). Uses `java.time.LocalDate`/`DayOfWeek`/`TextStyle` (already used elsewhere in the app — core-library desugaring on minSdk 24). `CalendarViewModel.CalendarUiState.Success` now carries `year`+`month` alongside `events` (captured at `load()` time) so the day list always matches the loaded month even on a fast prev/next switch. `LazyColumn` `item{}` is a `LazyListScope` member — **do NOT `import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.item`** (no such symbol; only `items` is imported), that was a compile error. Month prev/next nav + the `/mobile/...calendar?month=&year=` data source unchanged. `versionCode` 39→**40**, `versionName` "3.9"→**"4.0"** (rides the same still-unreleased APK as the Phase 106 teacher Marks Entry + the pending messaging/due-date builds). Android-only; `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL. **Not yet built into a signed APK / released.** Files: `ui/calendar/CalendarScreen.kt` (rewritten), `ui/calendar/CalendarViewModel.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 108 ✅  Daily Homework → multi-section posting (same homework to several/all sections) + the class-wide cancel-scope fix — the Daily Homework page could only post to **one section at a time**, so giving the same homework to every section meant re-entering it per section. **Design chosen (Option A of the impact analysis): a section MULTI-SELECT that COLLAPSES to a single class-wide row when every active section is picked** — `homework.section_id` was already nullable with `NULL = class-wide`, and the parent feed already matches `section_id IS NULL OR = @sectionId` (Phase 19), so class-wide is the representation the schema and the mobile app were *already* built for. Writing one NULL row instead of N per-section copies keeps the web list, the teacher app list and the two Homework reports free of duplicated subject rows. A **subset** of sections still writes one row set per selected section. **No DB migration, no APK** (nothing mobile changed — parents/teachers just read the rows). **Frontend (`DailyHomeworkPage.jsx`):** Section picker → `mode="multiple"` with an explicit **"All Sections (whole class)"** entry (sentinel value `0`) that is **mutually exclusive** with individual sections (handled in `onSectionChange`; clearing the box falls back to All). **Section is no longer required** — `ready = classId && date`, so a class with no sections defined can finally use the page. The dropdown now filters out **Inactive** sections (`GET /school/master/sections` has NO status filter — `MasterDataRepository.GetSections` returns every row — so "all" would otherwise have included retired sections). **Prefill rewritten:** the request now **omits `sectionId`** (in `GetHomeworkPaged` a NULL sectionId means *no section filter*, so one call returns every section's rows for that class+date — no API change needed), then keeps the rows the current selection would replace (class-wide rows always apply). Because the prefill map is keyed by `subjectId` alone, rows from different sections can collide — a **conflict is now detected and surfaced** as a warning `Alert` ("This date already has different homework across sections…") instead of silently showing whichever row came last. **DTO:** `BatchHomeworkRequest.SectionIds` (`List<int>`; **empty = class-wide**); the old `SectionId` is KEPT and only read when `SectionIds` is empty, so older clients are untouched. New `BatchHomeworkResult { SubjectCount, RowCount, Sections }` reports what was actually written (`Sections` = a single `null` entry when collapsed). **Repo (`HomeworkRepository.CreateBatchHomework`)** now returns `BatchHomeworkResult`, resolves targets via the new private `ResolveTargetSections` (empty list → fall back to `SectionId`; otherwise, if the picked set covers **every Active/legacy-'Y' section of the class**, collapse to `{ null }`), and inserts one row per subject **per target section**. **THE LOAD-BEARING FIX — cancel scope:** the old soft-cancel matched `ISNULL(section_id,0) = ISNULL(@sectionId,0)`, so class-wide and per-section rows were **invisible to each other** — save Section A homework, then save class-wide for the same date, and the A rows were never cancelled while the parent feed (`section_id IS NULL OR = @section`) showed **both**, i.e. duplicate/stale homework. Now **class-wide rows are ALWAYS cancelled** before writing (they reach the same students as anything we write), plus the sections being written; writing class-wide clears **every** section for that class+date. Sections NOT in the selection keep their own rows. **The section predicate is BRANCHED into the SQL string rather than parameterised** (`sectionFilter` is `""` when class-wide) so Dapper can never be handed an empty list for `IN @targetIds` — `targetIds` is empty *only* in the class-wide case. **Behaviour change worth telling staff: selecting a SUBSET of sections also clears that date's class-wide row** (it served those students too), so a section outside the selection that was relying on the shared class-wide row loses it — the accepted trade against showing duplicates. **Controller (`CreateBatchHomework`):** added the missing **`ClassId` required** check (the API previously accepted `classId = null`), returns the `BatchHomeworkResult` with a scope-aware message ("… in all sections" / "… in N section(s)"), and **push is now sent once per target section** (`NotifyClass` with a `null` section notifies the whole class) instead of one push using the raw request section. Also folded in the flagged `AND school_id = @schoolId` predicate on the batch subject-name lookup. **API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (vite, exit 0) compile-verified, and DEPLOYED** — full `deploy-api.ps1` (35 files; `GET /version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-08-01 09:33 UTC`) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 7 flavors). **Runtime-untested** — the cancel/collapse paths were verified by compilation only; worth one manual pass on a demo class. **Known pre-existing behaviours NOT changed:** a batch save still soft-cancels single-entry rows for that class+section+date (incl. teacher-app rows, which have `subject_id NULL` and are skipped by the prefill — they are cancelled and never re-inserted); the subject list is still school-wide, not `class_subjects`-driven; and re-saving still re-fires the parent push. Files: `Core/DTOs/School/Homework/HomeworkDto.cs`, `Data/Repositories/School/HomeworkRepository.cs`, `API/Controllers/School/HomeworkController.cs`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/homework/DailyHomeworkPage.jsx`.
Phase 109 ✅  AscentSyncTool — "Download Attendance" tab (monthly present-days → legacy `SAS_BulkAttendance`) + new `monthly-export` API endpoint — schools wanted the new app's attendance to land back in the legacy VB6 DB as a monthly summary. **Why an API endpoint was needed:** the sync tool has NO connection to the tenant DB — it only talks to the LOCAL legacy SQL server and the HOSTED API via `X-Api-Key` (Phase 35); the school's PC can't reach the hosted SQL server. So this tab follows the **Download Receipts** shape: API read → grid → legacy insert. **API (new):** `GET /school/attendance/monthly-export?month=&year=` (`AttendanceController`) → `AttendanceRepository.GetMonthlyPresentExport` → `AttendanceMonthlyExportDto { AdmissionNo, NoOfDaysPresent, AcademicYear }` (added to the existing `AttendanceDtos.cs` — **no new file, so no csproj registration needed** [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]). Query is the user-supplied one verbatim — `student_attendance` JOIN `students` JOIN `academic_years`, `status='Present'`, `GROUP BY admission_no, academic_year` — with the month range derived from month/year in C# (`new DateTime(y,m,1)` … `.AddMonths(1).AddDays(-1)`) and **`school_id` scoping added on both `sa` and `s`** (tenant DBs hold multiple branches; the raw query had none). Grouped by **admission_no, not student_id**, because the legacy table keys on the admission number. **Deliberately counts ONLY `status='Present'`** (per the supplied query) — **Late and HalfDay are NOT counted**, unlike the web app's attendance % which counts Late as a full day and HalfDay as 0.5 (Phase 73). **`SyncTab` gained a month/year mode** (the 3 existing tabs are untouched): `UseMonthYearFilter` swaps the From/To `DateTimePicker`s for **Month** + **Year** `ComboBox`es (month names from `CultureInfo`, default = current month; year list current+1 down to current−5) and routes Show through a NEW `OnShowPeriod(month, year)` callback instead of `OnShow(from, to)`; `RunShow` branches on the flag (and skips the from≤to check in month mode), `SetBusy` disables the new combos. The From:/To: labels became fields so they can be hidden — hidden controls take no space in a `FlowLayoutPanel`, so the bar re-flows cleanly. **Legacy write (`LegacyDb.SaveBulkAttendance`)**, one row per student: `AttnDat`=today, `AttnMonth`=**first day of the selected month as `yyyy-MM-dd`** (Jan 2027 → `2027-01-01`; the column is `varchar(15)`), `StuAdmn`/`AttnDys`/`AttnAcdYear` from the API row (`AttnDys` cast to double — the column is `float`), `AttnStatus`='A', `CrtBy`='SyncTool', `CrtDat`=now, `BranchID` from `SELECT TOP 1 BranchID FROM SAS_LicenceDet` (**fetched ONCE for the whole batch**, not per row), `MachID`/`DeltBy`/`TraId`=''. `AttnRmrks` left NULL (not in the supplied mapping). **Re-run behaviour (user decision): supersede, do NOT delete** — the earlier rows for that `StuAdmn + AttnMonth + AttnAcdYear` are `UPDATE`d to `AttnStatus='D'` (guarded `ISNULL(AttnStatus,'A') <> 'D'` so a second re-run doesn't re-count already-superseded rows) and a fresh `'A'` row is inserted, so the legacy side keeps its own history. Result dialog reports inserted / superseded / failed. **Tab is 4th, named "Download Attendance"** (tab title + save-result dialog title); tool `Version` 1.1→**1.2**. **API built (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + DEPLOYED** (full `deploy-api.ps1`, 35 files; `GET /version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-08-01 16:10 UTC`; `GET /school/attendance/monthly-export` → **401 `application/json`** = registered, not a 404 HTML page). Sync tool built clean (MSBuild Debug). **STILL PENDING: redistribute the rebuilt `AscentSyncTool.exe` (v1.2)** to the school machine (its `App.config` needs the usual `LegacyDb` connection string + `ApiKey`). Files: `Core/DTOs/School/Attendance/AttendanceDtos.cs`, `Data/Repositories/School/AttendanceRepository.cs`, `API/Controllers/School/AttendanceController.cs`, AscentSyncTool `MainForm.cs`/`SyncTab.cs`/`Data/LegacyDb.cs`/`Api/AscentApiClient.cs`/`Models/ApiModels.cs`.
Phase 110 ✅  School app — API failure popups now show the REAL reason (`Failed to save student.` → the server's message) — a student save failed with only "Failed to save student." in the toast and no way to tell what went wrong. **Two layers, both dropping the reason.** (1) **Client discarded it:** `StudentFormPage.jsx` did `if (!err?.errorFields) message.error('Failed to save student.')` — a hardcoded string — even though the fetch wrapper had ALREADY put the server's reason on the error (`axiosInstance.js` `new Error(errBody.message || …)`) and attached the body as `err.response.data`. So even a perfect message like "Admission number already exists." never reached the user. The same hardcoded-string-in-catch pattern was repeated **106 times across 52 files**. (2) **The wrapper read the wrong field for crashes:** an *unhandled* server exception does NOT come back in the `ApiResponse` envelope — it's Web API's `HttpError`, whose `message` is the fixed placeholder **"An error has occurred."** and whose real text is in **`exceptionMessage`** (+ `stackTrace`). `WebApiConfig.cs` already sets `IncludeErrorDetailPolicy.Always`, so production **was** sending the detail — the client just never looked at it, making a crash and a validation failure indistinguishable. **Fix (frontend only — no API change):** new `errorTextFrom(body, status)` in `axiosInstance.js` prefers `exceptionMessage` **only when the top-level message is that placeholder** (so genuine business messages are never overridden), and accepts both camelCase and PascalCase keys since whether the app's `CamelCasePropertyNamesContractResolver` renames `HttpError`'s dictionary keys is an implementation detail not worth depending on. New exported helper **`apiError(err, fallback)`** returns the server's reason or the caller's wording. **66 call sites in 43 files** rewritten to `catch (e) { message.error(apiError(e, '<old text>')) }` — old wording kept as the fallback, and `catch {` blocks with no binding got one whose name doesn't shadow anything the block already reads. Done with a **brace-aware Node script** (masks string/template/comment contents before scanning so a `{` inside a string can't break block matching) rather than by hand; the other 40 `message.error` calls were correctly skipped — they're input validation outside catch blocks ("Photo must be 1 MB or smaller") or already interpolate the error. **`apiError` returns the FALLBACK (not null) for AntD `validateFields()` rejections** — the first version returned `null`, which would have rendered an **empty toast** at the 66 sites that don't guard for it (several of those `try` blocks wrap `validateFields()`); `StudentFormPage`'s existing `if (!err?.errorFields)` guard still suppresses the toast entirely there. **Verified:** eslint diffed against a `git stash` baseline → **zero new errors** (and 5 pre-existing "'e' is defined but never used" errors fixed); vite build clean. **DEPLOYED** — `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 7 flavors); live bundle hashes spot-checked on demo/stannsasf/sfsgwk. **Staff may need one hard refresh** if a cached `index.html` is held. **Diagnosis tip that needs no code:** DevTools → Network → the failed request → Response tab already contains `exceptionMessage` + `stackTrace`. **NOT built (still open, offered):** the API has **no exception logging at all** — no `IExceptionLogger`, nothing to disk or DB — so an error a user hits and doesn't report leaves no trace; a global logger writing URL/tenant/user/stack to `App_Data/logs/` is the durable follow-up. Files: `ascent-school-app/src/api/axiosInstance.js` + 43 page/component files.
Phase 111 ✅  Teacher Homework — section filter ON the screen (Android-only) — teachers reported the Homework tile showing **every section's** homework for the selected class. **It was half-built already:** Phase 104 threaded `sectionId` all the way through (`TeacherScreen.Homework(classId, sectionId)` → `TeacherHomeworkScreen` → `TeacherViewModel.loadHomework` → `GET /mobile/teacher/homework/paged?classId=&sectionId=`), but the section came **only from the Home screen's dropdown**, and the Homework tile is `enabled = cls != null` — unlike Attendance/Marks Entry it opens with **no section required**, so picking just a class sends `sectionId = null` → all sections. There was also no way to change section without backing out to Home. **Added a section filter chip row at the top of `TeacherHomeworkScreen`:** a `LazyRow` of `FilterChip`s — `All sections` + one per section — fed by the `viewModel.loadSections(classId)` call the screen **already made** for the create sheet, so **no new API call, no backend/DB change** (the paged endpoint has accepted `sectionId` since Phase 104). New `filterSectionId` state initialises from the passed-in `sectionId` (so behaviour is unchanged when Home already picked one); the old `LaunchedEffect(Unit)` that loaded homework was **re-keyed to `LaunchedEffect(filterSectionId)`** so a chip tap reloads page 1 for that section, with infinite scroll paging within the filter. The Add-Homework sheet's `initialSectionId` now follows the **filter** rather than the value Home opened with, so viewing Section B and tapping + defaults "Post to" to Section B. **Layout decision (load-bearing):** the chip row is rendered ABOVE the loading/empty/list branches, inside a single root `Column`, with each branch taking `Modifier.fillMaxWidth().weight(1f)` instead of the old `fillMaxSize()`. The empty state previously filled the whole screen — had the chips lived inside the list branch, filtering to a section with **no** homework would have hidden the chips and trapped the teacher with no route back to "All sections". Empty text also switches to "No homework for this section" when a filter is on. **A section filter still returns whole-class rows** (`section_id IS NULL`) — existing `includeClassWide:true` behaviour from Phase 104 and correct (class-wide homework does apply to that section), but the extra rows read like a broken filter, so the caption now says `Showing X of Y · includes whole-class homework` (cards already tag `🏫 Section A` vs `🏫 All sections`). `versionCode` 40→**41**, `versionName` "4.0"→**"4.1"**. Android-only, `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (exit 0). **Nothing to deploy** — ships in the next APK, still the pending one carrying Phases 104/106/107. Files: `ui/teacher/TeacherHomeworkScreen.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 112 ✅  Parent app — "Home" destination renamed to "Profile" (+ person icon) and Date of Birth dropped from the profile card (Android-only) — the first parent destination was **labelled "Home" but always rendered `ProfileScreen`** (`FeatureContent(0)` — it was never a dashboard), so the name was simply wrong. Renamed in the `features` list in `ui/home/HomeScreen.kt` — **that list is the single source of truth for all 8 parent destinations (Phase 101)**, so ONE line updates every place the label surfaces: the 3-col IconTile grid (Tiles view), the bottom `NavigationBar` (Tabs view), AND the `TopAppBar` title shown when a tile is opened full-screen (`features.first { it.index == openedFeature }.label`). Icon changed `Icons.Default.Home` → **`Icons.Default.Person`** (no import needed — the file already wildcards `androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*`; `Person` is not one of the auto-mirrored icons, so no deprecation warning); navy gradient unchanged. **Date of Birth removed** from the "Personal Details" card in `ui/profile/ProfileScreen.kt` (the only DOB display in the parent app) — the card now reads Father's Name → Mother's Name → Gender → Blood Group → Mobile. **Display-only:** `dateOfBirth` remains on the mobile DTO and the API still returns it (removing it end-to-end would be a DTO + API change, deliberately out of scope). `versionCode` stays **41** (bumped in Phase 111, unreleased — these ride the same build). Android-only, no API/DB change; `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (exit 0). Ships in the pending APK alongside Phases 104/106/107/111. Files: `ui/home/HomeScreen.kt`, `ui/profile/ProfileScreen.kt`.
Phase 115 ✅  Fee Receipts — "Export PDF" (letterhead + totals) + `GET /school/settings/profile` — the Receipts page (Fees → Receipts) had Export CSV only; added an **Export PDF** button beside it producing a printable fee-collection listing modelled on a client reference PDF (school name / address / phone letterhead, table, grand total with per-payment-mode breakdown, page footer). **Row granularity (user decision): receipt-wise — the SAME columns as the grid/CSV** (S.No, Receipt No, Student, Adm No, Class, Date, Amount, Mode, Status), NOT the item-wise layout of the reference (which repeats a receipt per fee item and adds Term + Acd.Year — that would need the receipts LIST endpoint to join `fee_receipt_items`, which it does not; offered and declined). **Totals (user decision): cancelled receipts are listed but EXCLUDED** from the collected total and its per-mode breakdown, and reported on their own line ("Cancelled (excluded, N)") — a collection figure gets reconciled against a bank deposit, so voided receipts must not inflate it. **Backend — the one thing that needed API work:** the school app had no way to get the branch's postal address. It is in **`ascent_master.schools`** (address/city/district/state/pin_code/mobile/landline/email/website) and is **per branch**, whereas `GET /branding` resolves **group-wide and is called pre-login without a schoolId** (`App.jsx`), so branding was the wrong place for it. Added `SchoolProfileDto` + `SchoolSettingsRepository.GetProfile(schoolId)` (master DB) + **`GET /school/settings/profile`** on the existing `SchoolSettingsController` — deliberately reusing that controller/DTO file rather than adding new files, so **no csproj registration was needed** [[api-csproj-manual-registration]] and no new controller name could collide (the Phase 87 trap). Auth-only (`[SchoolAuth]`), not permission-gated, matching the existing settings GET; a fee clerk can therefore load it. **Frontend:** new reusable **`exportListingPdf`** in `pages/reports/reportUtils.js` (portrait A4) — letterhead **redrawn on every page** via autoTable's `didDrawPage` (page 1 is drawn before the table, pages 2+ from the hook), a right-aligned boxed totals block placed after `lastAutoTable.finalY` (spills to a new page if it would not fit), and a `Page X of Y` + generated-timestamp footer written in a **second pass** over `getNumberOfPages()` (the count is not known while the table is still being laid out). Logo support via **`loadImageDataUrl`** — loads the branding logo with `crossOrigin='anonymous'`, draws it to a canvas and returns a data URL, with a 4 s timeout and **null on any failure** (missing file, CORS-tainted canvas), plus a try/catch around `addImage`, so a logo problem can never stop the report downloading; R2/CDN must send CORS headers for the logo to appear. `ReceiptsPage.jsx` fetches `/school/settings/profile` once on mount (failure non-fatal → falls back to `branding.displayName` with no address block), builds the rows, and passes fixed `columnStyles` — portrait content width is 190 mm, the 8 fixed columns take 147 mm, leaving ~43 mm for the auto-width Student column. **Rupee symbol deliberately omitted** from the PDF: jsPDF's built-in helvetica is WinAnsi-encoded and cannot render `₹` (U+20B9) — it comes out blank/garbled; amounts use Indian digit grouping (`toLocaleString('en-IN')`) with no symbol. (**Pre-existing, NOT fixed:** `FeeConcessionPage.jsx` lines ~92/109 do put `₹` inside its jsPDF output and will have this glyph problem.) **Caveat:** the address block only appears if the branch's address fields are filled in the Control app (`schools` row) — otherwise the header degrades to just the school name. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint clean, vite build clean) verified. **PDF layout is compile-verified only, NOT visually verified** — a Node smoke-test of the real exporter was attempted and abandoned (jspdf's Node build exposes no default export, so `import jsPDF from 'jspdf'` is undefined outside a browser bundler; not worth shimming a DOM). Everything used matches the existing working exporters except `addImage`, which is the one genuinely new call and is fully guarded. **Not yet deployed** — needs BOTH `deploy-api.ps1` (new endpoint) and `deploy-school.ps1`. Files: `Core/DTOs/School/Settings/SchoolSettingsDtos.cs`, `Data/Repositories/School/SchoolSettingsRepository.cs`, `API/Controllers/School/SchoolSettingsController.cs`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/reports/reportUtils.js`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/fee/ReceiptsPage.jsx`.
Phase 116 ✅  Marks Entry — Export PDF (A4 landscape) + Export CSV of the loaded section's grid (frontend-only) — two buttons beside **Save All** on `MarksEntryPage.jsx`. **Exports SAVED marks only (user decision):** clicking export re-fetches `GET /school/marks?...` for the selected year/exam/class/section rather than reading the on-screen edits (the loaded `grid` is also stale after a Save All); a `dirty` flag (set on any edit, cleared on load + successful save) shows a warning that unsaved changes are excluded. **Columns:** S.No · Adm No · Student Name, then per subject **Act (/n) + Marks (/n)** as SEPARATE columns when the subject-exam has activity, else just Marks; **Total (/grandMax)** + **%** at the end (user decisions). Cells: `AB` absent, `-` not entered. **Total** = Σ(marks + activity) over ALL subjects out of Σ(max + activity max) — absent/not-entered count 0 but still carry their max; a student with nothing saved shows `-`. PDF uses a **two-row header** (subject name `colSpan` over its Act/Marks sub-columns; S.No/Adm/Name/Total/% `rowSpan:2`) — `reportUtils.exportPdf` gained an optional `head` param (multi-row header) that overrides `columns`; all existing callers unchanged. Font auto-shrinks 8→7→6pt by mark-column count to fit A4 landscape. CSV uses flat headers (`Maths Act (/20)`). Logic lives in a pure `buildMarksExport(grid)` in the page (smoke-tested with sample data). **No signature line** (user decision). eslint + vite clean; **deployed** to all 8 school apps (`deploy-school.ps1 -School all`); live bundle verified on demo/sfspamidi/stmary-ym. PDF layout not visually verified.
Phase 117 ✅  Marks Grade Master — total-marks grade bands per class + optional section (legacy `SAS_MarksGradeMaster`) — new tenant table **`marks_grade_master`** (table 54): `academic_year_id`, `class_id`, **`section_id NULL` = whole class** (legacy has no section; a section's own band set **overrides** the whole-class set — user chose option (a)), `min_marks`/`max_marks DECIMAL(8,2)` = **raw marks, not %** (user), `grade VARCHAR(10)`, `grade_point VARCHAR(10)` (**text** — legacy `MrksGradPoint` holds A/B, user), `description VARCHAR(100)` (widened from legacy 10), `status`, `school_id`, `created_by`, `created_at` (**IST default**). Legacy mapping: ClasID→class_id, AcdYear→academic_year_id, MinMrks/MaxMrks→min/max_marks, MrksGrad→grade, MrksGradPoint→grade_point, Descrpt→description, TraStatus→status, CrtBy/CrtDate→created_by/created_at, BranchID→school_id; TraID/MachID/DeleteBy dropped. `CK_mgm_range` (0 ≤ min ≤ max); **two filtered unique indexes** on grade within a set (`UQ_mgm_section` WHERE section_id IS NOT NULL AND status='Active'; `UQ_mgm_class` WHERE section_id IS NULL AND status='Active') — decision #58 NULL trap. **Distinct from `grade_types`** (per-SUBJECT bands, used by exam_master) — this is the grade for the student's TOTAL; kept as separate tables (user confirmed). **Backend:** `MarksGradeDtos.cs` (Core/DTOs/School/MarksGrades), `MarksGradeMasterRepository` (`GetForClass` returns whole-class + every section's set with section names; `SectionBelongsToClass`; `SaveSet` delete+insert of ONE scope in a transaction — the NULL scope is branched into the SQL, `section_id IS NULL`, never `= NULL`), `MarksGradeMasterController` `[RoutePrefix("school/marks-grades")]` (GET `?academicYearId=&classId=`, POST replaces one set; `sectionId` null/0 = whole class; **empty bands = remove the set** → a section falls back to whole-class; server validation: grade required ≤10, gradePoint ≤10, description ≤100, min/max required, 0 ≤ min ≤ max, **no duplicate grade (case-insensitive — collation)**, **no overlapping ranges** [next.min ≤ prev.max], ≤50 bands, section must belong to class). All 3 files registered in the old-style csprojs [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]. **Frontend:** `MarksGradeMasterTab.jsx` in **Master Data → Marks Grade Master** (after Grade Types; gated by MASTER_DATA like the rest — no new permission code): Year + Class + Section (**"All Sections (whole class)"** default; Inactive sections hidden) → editable Min/Max/Grade/Grade Point/Description rows → Save (Popconfirm) replaces that scope's set; tags show which scopes are defined; a section with no own bands shows it inherits the whole-class bands + a **"Start from whole-class bands"** button to create an override; "Remove section bands"/"Remove all bands". Client validation mirrors the server; errors via `apiError`. **Not wired into anything yet** (user will decide later where grades are applied — marks export, report cards). **Known caveat for that later step:** because bands are raw marks, one set only fits exams whose total max matches (e.g. FA /200 vs SA /600 in the same class can't share bands); and with decimal marks a total can fall in a gap between integer bands (90.5 between 81–90 and 91–100). **NOT built (offered, user didn't take up):** AscentMigration migrator for `SAS_MarksGradeMaster`, "copy from another class", CSV import. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint + vite) compile-verified. **DEPLOYED:** user ran `marks_grade_master_migration.sql`; full `deploy-api.ps1` (35 files; `GET /version` → `buildDate 2026-09-11 11:38 UTC`; `GET /school/marks-grades` → **401 application/json** = registered) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors; live bundles verified on demo/sfsgwk/stmary-ym). Runtime-untested against real data.
Phase 118 ✅  Marks Card (progress report) — Reports → Marks Card + named total-grade scales selected per exam — modelled on the client's `MARKS CARD.docx` (Holy Spirit): logo top-left, school name (Times bold, upper-cased) + branch address, **"PROGRESS REPORT - {exam} ({year})"** title line (user asked for it — the sample has none), a student box (Student Name / Admin No / Father Name · Class / Section / Rank), then **Subject | Internal Marks | Written Test Marks | Total | Grade** with a TOTAL row (total + total grade). **A4 portrait, one card per page; no signatures / % / "x / max" in the TOTAL row** (user decisions). **Right-side image only for holyspiritjm:** `src/assets/hs_char.png` (founder photo) imported in `MarksCardReport.jsx` and used only when `import.meta.env.VITE_SUBDOMAIN === 'holyspiritjm'` (the asset is emitted in every build but drawn only there). **Named scales (user: "select the marks grade master record in the exam"):** a class/section/year previously held ONE band set, so there was nothing to choose and raw-mark bands couldn't serve exams with different totals — `marks_grade_master` gained **`scale_name`** (e.g. "FA - out of 300"; a class can hold several; a section can override a scale), and new tenant table **`exam_grade_scales`** (table 55, `UNIQUE(school, year, exam_type, class)`, stored by scale NAME) records which scale grades each exam+class total. **Marks Grade Master tab** gained a Scale select (existing scales + "Add" new name via `popupRender` — antd 6 name for dropdownRender; a new name is local until its first Save); scope = (year, class, scale, section). **Exam Master tab** gained a **"Total Grade Scale"** bar (select + Save) shown when year + exam type + class are all selected. **Mapping:** Internal = `activity_marks`, Written = `marks_obtained`, Total = sum; subjects from `class_subjects` (grid order); a subject without activity shows "-" in Internal; the Internal column is dropped entirely when no subject has activity; max marks go in the column header when uniform across subjects, else each cell shows "x / max"; absent → AB (grade blank). **Subject grade** = Grade Types via the grade type on each subject's Exam Master row — `exam_master.grade_type_id` points at ONE `grade_types` row whose `grade_name` names the scheme; the bands are every Active row with that name (subject-specific rows win over "all subjects" rows) — matched on the subject total. **Total grade** = the exam+class scale; the section's own bands for that scale win, else whole-class. **Band pick rule (both):** the band with the highest min ≤ value — equals min ≤ v ≤ max for contiguous bands and puts a decimal total that falls in a gap (90.5 between 81–90 and 91–100) into the lower band. **Rank:** within the selected section, **competition ranking** (equal totals share, next skips: 1,1,3); students with NO marks at all are unranked ("-"); computed server-side on the whole section so a single-student print still carries the right rank. **Students = the SELECTED year only** (`students.academic_year_id` filter) — promotion leaves last year's rows Active, so class+section alone would mix years. **Marks Entry year-scope fix (shipped in the same deploy, user-approved):** `MarksRepository.GetMarksGrid` (web Marks Entry grid + its PDF/CSV export) and `GetSubjectMarks` (teacher mobile marks — passes the current year) filtered students by class+section+status but NOT academic year, so they listed last year's students of that class too (incl. migrated legacy years) — same class of bug Phase 66 fixed for attendance. Both student queries (and the grid's marks join) now add `academic_year_id = @academicYearId`. API-only; no APK needed. **Backend:** `MarksCardDtos.cs` (Core/DTOs/School/Marks), `MarksCardRepository` (header/subjects-with-grade-type/students/marks/grade_types/scale → per-student lines, totals, grades, rank), `MarksCardController` `[RoutePrefix("school/marks-card")]`; `MarksGradeMaster*` gained ScaleName + `GetExamScale`/`SaveExamScale` + GET/PUT `exam-scale`. New files registered in the csprojs [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]. **Printing:** the report opens a blank tab synchronously on click (popups opened after an `await` get blocked), generates the PDF, `doc.autoPrint()` and loads the blob URL into it; falls back to `doc.save` if the popup was blocked. Warnings on the page: no scale selected / scale has no bands / subjects without a grade type / no subjects mapped. **Verified:** API (MSBuild Release, exit 0), eslint + vite (holyspiritjm build emits `hs_char`), and the real `generateMarksCards` rendered in Node with the sample's data → visually matches the docx (2 pages, AB case correct). DB/repository logic compile-verified only. **SQL:** `tenant_tables.sql` updated (table 54 `scale_name` + indexes, table 55) + new idempotent `Database/marks_card_migration.sql` (run AFTER `marks_grade_master_migration.sql`). **Deploy order (load-bearing): SQL → API → school app, API + school app together** — the Phase 118 API requires `scale_name` on grade-master saves, so the already-live Phase 117 grade-master tab would 400 on Save until the new school app is out. **DEPLOYED:** user ran `marks_card_migration.sql`; full `deploy-api.ps1` (`GET /version` → `buildDate 2026-09-11 12:09 UTC`; `school/marks-card` + `school/marks-grades/exam-scale` → **401 application/json** = registered) then `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors; live bundles verified on demo/sfsgwk/stmary-ym). Runtime-untested against real data. **Post-deploy fix — school logo missing from the PDF (also fixed the Phase 115 Receipts PDF logo):** the branding logo is a relative `/Uploads/branding/…` path that `brandingStore.toAbsolute` prefixes with the API base (`https://edu-care.in/api/Uploads/…`) — **cross-origin** to the school app — and **IIS serves `/Uploads` static files WITHOUT CORS headers** (`Global.asax Application_BeginRequest` adds them for MANAGED requests only; verified: the file returned no `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` even with an `Origin` header). `loadImageDataUrl` sets `img.crossOrigin='anonymous'` (needed to read pixels into the canvas for jsPDF), so the load failed and the logo was silently skipped; the on-screen `<img>` logo was fine because merely displaying doesn't need CORS. **Fix:** new `GET /school/uploads/image-data?path=/Uploads/…` on `SchoolUploadsController` (existing file — no csproj change) returns the file as a `data:` URL via a managed route that carries the CORS headers; restricted to image extensions (png/jpg/jpeg/gif/webp) under `~/Uploads` (`..` rejected + full-path prefix check), ≤2 MB, `[SchoolAuth]` — local files only, never remote URLs (not an open proxy). `reportUtils.loadImageDataUrl` (now async) first runs `toReadableSrc(url)`: a URL under `${API_BASE}/Uploads/` is fetched through that endpoint, anything else (R2/CDN, bundled assets like `hs_char.png`) loads directly as before. `brandingStore` now exports `API_BASE`. **Rule: any PDF/canvas use of an API-hosted `/Uploads` image must go through `/school/uploads/image-data` — a direct cross-origin load taints/fails.** API + all 8 school apps redeployed (`/version` → `2026-09-11 12:20 UTC`; `image-data` → 401 **with** `Access-Control-Allow-Origin`). **Follow-up — Attendance Particulars, Remarks, signatures (format from a St Ann's progress-report photo; ALL schools, always on — user):** below the marks table the card now prints (1) **Attendance Particulars** — Month | Jun … Mar | Total | %, rows Working Days / Present Days; **10 months June (start year) → March (end year)** (user; April/May attendance is NOT shown), start year = first 4-digit number of the academic-year label (fallback: current Indian academic year); months with nothing marked are blank; **Working Days = distinct dates attendance was marked for the class+section that month** (selected-year students; `Holiday` rows excluded) — user choice (a), no calendar needed; **Present = Present + Late + 0.5 × HalfDay** (same weighting as the web attendance %, Phase 73 — NOT the Present-only count of the Phase 109 legacy export); a month the class had attendance but the student has no row counts 0; % = total present / total working, 1 dp (`rowSpan:2`); (2) **Remarks** — a blank box for handwriting (user: not typed, no storage); (3) **Parent Sign / Class Teacher Sign / Principal Sign** lines pinned near the page foot (hand-signed; no scanned signature image). **"Issued On" deliberately NOT printed** (user). The St Ann's table columns (Max/Min/Result/%/Subject Highest) were explicitly out of scope — the photo was reference for the attendance format only. **Backend:** `MarksCardDataDto` + `AttendanceMonths` (`{Year, Month, Label, WorkingDays?}` ×10) + `TotalWorkingDays`; `MarksCardStudentDto` + `PresentDays` (aligned list, null where the month has no working days) + `TotalPresent`; two grouped queries in `MarksCardRepository` (class-level distinct dates; per-student weighted sum) keyed by value-tuple dictionaries. **PDF:** `generateMarksCards` gains `attendanceMonths`/`totalWorkingDays`; attendance (~26 mm) + remarks label + min box (~24 mm) must fit above the signatures, else they continue on a second page (a normal 6–11 subject card stays on one page — both render-verified in Node); the Remarks box height flexes 14–28 mm to fill the gap. Marks Card page table gained an **Attendance** column (present / working). API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + eslint + vite clean. **DEPLOYED** (no SQL): `deploy-api.ps1` — the first run's FTP upload died at file ~26/35 with **`(550) File unavailable`** (a server file briefly locked while the app pool was busy); since the publish step had completed (Publish DLLs timestamped from that run), `deploy-api.ps1 -SkipBuild` re-uploaded the same fresh build cleanly (35/35) — `/version` → `buildDate 2026-09-11 14:36 UTC`, endpoints 401 json, public `/branding` OK. **Lesson: a mid-upload FTP 550 on the API is transient — re-run with `-SkipBuild` ONLY when the Publish folder is from the same run (check the DLL timestamps), else a full re-run** (cf. the Phase 81 stale-publish trap). Then `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8; live bundles verified on demo/sfsgwk/stmary-ym). Attendance queries runtime-untested against real data.
Phase 119 ✅  Marks Grade Master → standalone grading scales, picked per exam row — **user decision: the grade scale must NOT be tied to a class** ("any exam master configuration can assign itself with the selected marks grade master"). Phase 118's model (bands scoped to year+class+section, with the scale chosen per exam+class in `exam_grade_scales`) is replaced by a reusable library. **DB (destructive — user confirmed NO school had entered bands yet):** `marks_grade_master` becomes the **scale header** (`scale_name` UNIQUE per school, description, status — **no class/section/year**), new **`marks_grade_master_bands`** holds its bands (`UNIQUE(scale_id, grade)`, `CHECK 0 ≤ min ≤ max`), `exam_master` gains **`marks_grade_master_id`** + FK, and **`exam_grade_scales` is dropped**. `tenant_tables.sql` tables 54/55 rewritten (the FK is an `ALTER` after table 54 — exam_master is table 46, created earlier) + idempotent `Database/marks_grade_scales_migration.sql` (recognises the OLD table by its `class_id` column and drops it; `marks_card_migration.sql` marked SUPERSEDED). **Assignment lives on each exam row (user chose option A over a separate per exam+class setting):** a **"Marks Grade Master"** dropdown sits next to **Grade Type** in the Exam Master Add/Edit form — Grade Type grades each SUBJECT, the scale grades the TOTAL. Add applies it to every subject row created; the list has a **Total Scale** column; bulk import gained a **`MarksGradeMaster`** column (matched by name, optional — an unknown name fails the row). **Because it's per row, rows of one exam+class can disagree** — `MarksCardRepository` now resolves the scale by grouping the class's Active exam rows by scale and taking the most-used (`ORDER BY COUNT(1) DESC`), sets a new `ScaleConflict` flag, and the Marks Card page warns "subjects point at different grading scales — X is applied". Bands are then read from `marks_grade_master_bands`; **the section-override behaviour is gone** (scales have no section). **API:** `MarksGradeDtos` rewritten (`MarksGradeScaleDto` with BandCount/ExamCount, `MarksGradeBandDto`, save-scale / save-bands requests; the exam-scale DTOs deleted); `MarksGradeMasterRepository` rewritten (GetScales/GetBands/NameExists/CreateScale/UpdateScale/CountExamsUsing/DeleteScale/SaveBands); controller now GET list, POST, PUT/{id}, DELETE/{id} (**refused while exams use it — user didn't answer that question; Inactive stays available**), GET/PUT `{id}/bands`; `ExamMasterRepository` carries the new column through get/create/update/bulk. **Frontend:** `MarksGradeMasterTab` rewritten as a scale library (table + band-editor card, no Year/Class/Section pickers); `ExamMasterTab` lost the Total Grade Scale bar and gained the form field + column + import column; `MarksCardReport` warnings re-worded. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + eslint + vite clean. **DEPLOYED:** user ran `marks_grade_scales_migration.sql`; `deploy-api.ps1` (`/version` → `buildDate 2026-09-12 02:43 UTC`) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). Live probes: `school/marks-grades` and `school/marks-grades/{id}/bands` → **401 json** (registered), the retired `school/marks-grades/exam-scale` → **404** (gone), `school/marks-card` → 401. Runtime-untested against real data. **Migration gotcha seen in the field:** on a DB where the script had already run, the second run printed only "already exists — skipped" for all three steps (the drop guards found nothing) — that IS the idempotent no-op path, but it looks identical to "the old table survived and blocked the create", so verify with `SELECT c.name FROM sys.columns c WHERE c.object_id = OBJECT_ID('marks_grade_master')`: a `class_id` column means the OLD table is still there; `scale_name` + no `class_id` means the new shape. **Each tenant DB needs its own run** — only the DB the user checked is confirmed.
Phase 120 ✅  Student language fields → subject dropdowns + marks card skips subjects with no marks (school app only) — two small frontend changes, no API/DB/APK work. **(1) Student form languages:** `StudentFormPage.jsx` Other Info tab — **1st / 2nd / 3rd Language** were free-text `Input`s; now `Select`s (searchable, clearable) listing the school's subjects from `GET /school/master/subjects`. **Values are still saved as TEXT** (the `students.first_language`… columns are varchar and the DTO is unchanged), so nothing about storage or the API changes — the dropdown just supplies the string. Subjects repeat per academic year, so the list is **de-duplicated by name**, sorted, and filtered to Active (`status` Active / legacy `'Y'` / null). `languageOptions(current)` **appends a saved value that isn't in the subject list** so editing a student can't silently blank a legacy value. **Mother Tongue deliberately left as a text box** (not asked). Also removed a pre-existing dead `useAuthStore` import that was failing eslint in the committed file. **(2) Marks card:** `generateMarksCards` now prints **only the subjects that student has data for** — a subject with nothing entered is dropped from their card; **an absent subject IS data and stays (AB)**. Cards in one section can therefore have different row counts. The header max-marks figures are computed **per card** from the printed subjects (the old single `head`/`columnStyles` pair became `layoutFor(subs)`), so "(10)/(40)/(50)" stays right when the dropped subjects had different maximums. **A student with NOTHING entered keeps the full subject list** (all dashes) rather than printing an empty table. Totals/grade/rank are unchanged — they're server-side and already ignored missing subjects. Verified by rendering a card in Node: 3 marked + 1 absent → 4 rows (2 empty subjects gone), and the no-marks student → all 6 rows. eslint + vite clean. **DEPLOYED:** `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). **(3) Marks card header overlap fix (user-reported, same day):** on Holy Spirit the address ran UNDER both the logo and the right-hand photo. `generateMarksCards`'s `drawHeader` centred the school name on `pageW/2` with a hard-coded 120 mm shrink width and wrapped the address at a fixed **140 mm** — on a 210 mm page that band spans x≈35–175, straight through the logo (x≈19–47) and the right image (x≈163–187). Now the image widths are captured as `logoW`/`rightW` when drawn, and the name + address are laid out in the **gap between them** (`textL`/`textR`, centred on that gap's midpoint, wrapped to its width); the name shrink loop uses the same width in 0.5 pt steps down to 10 pt, the address may wrap to **3** lines (was 2), and the exam title — which spans the full width — is pushed to `max(y + 7, top + imgH + 6)` so it can never sit beside the images. A failed `addImage` now resets its width to 0, so the text reclaims the space. Verified by rendering both the real long case ("HOLI SPIRIT SCHOOL - JEEDIMETLA" + its 2-line address) and a short one in Node. Frontend-only; redeployed to all 8 flavors. **Rule: header text next to letterhead images must be bounded by the measured image widths, never by a fixed centred width.**
Phase 121 ✅  Bus Fee Structure — duplicate term/period rows fixed — Transport → Bus Fee Structure could show the same term (or fee period) more than once after Load, and the amount looked like it wasn't clearing between loads. **Root cause:** `bus_fee_structures` had NO unique constraint on `(route_id, academic_year_id, term_id/fee_period_id, school_id)`, and `SaveBusFeeStructure` did a plain DELETE-then-INSERT as two separate, non-transactional `conn.Execute` calls — if Save ever ran twice for the same route+year (a double-click, or a slow request overlapping a fresh one), the two calls could interleave and leave two saved rows for the same term; `GetBusFeeStructure`'s plain `LEFT JOIN` to `bus_fee_structures` then returned **one grid row per matching saved row**, so a term with 2 saved rows showed up twice — which is what looked like "not clearing, appending" on repeated Load. **Backend fix (`TransportRepository.cs`):** `GetBusFeeStructure`'s Term and Monthly queries changed from `LEFT JOIN` to `OUTER APPLY (SELECT TOP 1 ... ORDER BY bus_fee_structure_id DESC)` — this guarantees **exactly one** row per term/period even if stray duplicate rows still exist in the DB (defensive, takes effect immediately on API deploy, no migration needed first); `SaveBusFeeStructure` now (a) de-dupes `req.Items` by `(TermId, FeePeriodId)` before inserting (keeps the last), and (b) wraps the DELETE + all INSERTs in a single SQL transaction, closing the interleaving window that created the duplicates in the first place. **Frontend (`TransportPage.jsx` `BusFeeTab`):** `loadStructure` now (a) clears `structure`/`amounts` immediately when a Load starts instead of leaving the previous grid on screen until the response arrives, (b) tags each request with a `loadSeq` ref and discards the response if a newer Load has since fired (protects against an out-of-order/slow response overwriting a fresher one), and (c) defensively de-dupes the returned `terms` array by row key before building the grid/amounts map (belt-and-suspenders on top of the backend fix). **DB (data cleanup + guarantee):** new `Database/bus_fee_structures_dedupe_migration.sql` — deletes any existing duplicate rows (keeps the highest `bus_fee_structure_id` per route+year+term/period) then adds two filtered unique indexes, `UQ_bus_fee_structures_term` (`school_id, route_id, academic_year_id, term_id WHERE term_id IS NOT NULL`) and `UQ_bus_fee_structures_period` (same, `fee_period_id`), mirroring the `fee_concessions` two-index pattern (decision #58 — term_id/fee_period_id are mutually exclusive per row and SQL Server treats NULLs as distinct, so one column would go unprotected with a single combined index). `tenant_tables.sql` updated with the same two indexes for new tenant DBs. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint + vite) compile-verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). The SQL migration is user-run per tenant DB (not load-bearing — the API-side OUTER APPLY fix already prevents the symptom even before the migration runs, but the migration is what actually removes existing duplicate rows and stops new ones at the DB level). **Follow-up (same day):** the user manually removed duplicate rows from `terms` directly (ahead of running the migration above) without repointing `bus_fee_structures.term_id` first, which orphaned the amounts that had been saved against the deleted duplicate term ids (grid showed 0 even though the row still existed in the DB) — see `Database/bus_fee_structures_orphaned_term_check.sql` (diagnostic + fill-in-the-blanks repair template + re-adds the FK if it had to be dropped to allow the delete). **Migration tool fix (same root class of bug, forward-looking):** `TermsMigrator`/`FeePeriodsMigrator` (both read `SAS_TermMonthData.AcdYear`) and `FeeStructuresMigrator` (reads `SAS_FeeMaster.AcdYear`) previously fell back to the **latest/default academic_year_id** whenever a row's `AcdYear` had a value that wasn't found in the destination `academic_years` — silently mis-attaching the term/period/fee-structure row to the wrong year. Changed all three to insert **NULL** in that specific case instead (the blank-`AcdYear` fallback-to-default behavior is unchanged); `academic_year_id` is nullable on `terms`, `fee_periods`, and `fee_structures` so this needs no schema change. In `FeeStructuresMigrator`, a NULL year also means the `term_id`/`fee_period_id` lookup (keyed `"{name}|{academicYearId}"`) correctly can't match anything, so those end up NULL too rather than matching the wrong year's term. **Checked and already correct (no fallback-to-latest, left unchanged):** `BusFeeStructuresMigrator` (`SAS_BusMaster.AcdmYear`) and `FeeReceiptsMigrator` (`SAS_FeeReceipts.AcdYear` — feeds both `fee_receipts` and `fee_receipt_items`) — both start `academicYearId` at `null` and only set it on a real match in the destination `academic_years`, never guessing with a fallback. `AscentMigration` (dotnet build, exit 0) rebuilt clean. Files: `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/TransportRepository.cs`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/transport/TransportPage.jsx`, `Database/bus_fee_structures_dedupe_migration.sql`, `Database/bus_fee_structures_orphaned_term_check.sql`, `Database/tenant_tables.sql`, `AscentMigration/Migrators/TermsMigrator.cs`, `AscentMigration/Migrators/FeePeriodsMigrator.cs`, `AscentMigration/Migrators/FeeStructuresMigrator.cs`.
Phase 122 ✅  Parent web app — sfsgwk-only policy links on the login screen (frontend-only) — sfsgwk (SFS School Gajuwaka) needed 4 external legal-page links visible on the login screen of `parent.sfsgwk.edu-care.in`, not shown for any other school. `LoginPage.jsx` gains a `POLICY_LINKS` map keyed by `VITE_SCHOOL_CODE` (currently one entry, `sfsgwk`, with Privacy Policy / Terms & Conditions / Cancellation Policy / Refund Policy → `https://sfsschoolgajuwaka.in/...`); rendered as a small centered row of links (`|`-separated, `target="_blank"`) below the login card, only when `import.meta.env.VITE_SCHOOL_CODE.toLowerCase()` matches a key in the map — same per-flavor-conditional pattern as the school app's `hs_char.png`/holyspiritjm check (Phase 118). To add another school's links later, add another entry to `POLICY_LINKS` — no other change needed. `npm run build:sfsgwk` clean (no eslint config in this app, unlike the school app). **DEPLOYED:** `deploy-parent.ps1 -School sfsgwk` only (other schools' parent apps untouched — their `parentRemote` config, where set, is unaffected since the change is gated by env at build time). Files: `ascent-parent-app/src/pages/auth/LoginPage.jsx`.
Phase 123 ✅  Fee Concession — investigated "Term dropdown shows terms from other academic years" (data repair, not a code bug) — a school reported the Term dropdown on Fees → Fee Concession showing terms that didn't belong to the selected academic year. **Verified the code is correct:** `FeeConcessionPage.jsx`'s `terms` state loads via `GET /school/master/terms?academicYearId={filterYear}` (re-fetched whenever the year filter changes; every Term dropdown on the page reads from this one filtered state, no unfiltered path exists), and `MasterDataRepository.GetTerms` filters `WHERE (@academicYearId IS NULL OR academic_year_id = @academicYearId)`. Confirmed the fix was actually LIVE (not a stale-deploy illusion) by pulling the deployed bundle (`https://demo.edu-care.in/assets/index-*.js`) and finding the `master/terms?academicYearId=${...}` call compiled in. **Real root cause: pre-existing bad data from BEFORE the Phase 121 migration-tool fix** — `TermsMigrator` used to silently default a term to the LATEST `academic_year_id` whenever its legacy `AcdYear` wasn't found in the destination, so terms that actually belonged to older years got stamped with the current year's id; selecting "the current year" then correctly shows everything tagged with that id — including those wrongly-migrated older terms. The Phase 121 code fix only prevents this for FUTURE migration runs — it doesn't retroactively correct data already migrated wrong. **The fix (data repair, not code):** `terms.year_name` is populated from the legacy `YearNam` column, which is SEPARATE from `AcdYear` (the one that drove the wrong `academic_year_id`) — so `year_name` still carries each term's true year label even on a mis-tagged row. New `Database/terms_academic_year_repair.sql`: Step 1 groups `terms` by `academic_year_id` and flags any year with more than one distinct `year_name` value (the symptom); Step 2 previews exactly which rows would be repointed and to what (matches `year_name` to an `academic_years` row, only when the match is UNAMBIGUOUS — a blank `year_name` or one matching more than one `academic_years` row is left untouched for manual review); Step 3 applies it inside a transaction (`COMMIT`/`ROLLBACK` left to the user after reviewing the affected-row count). **Safe by construction: only `terms.academic_year_id` on the SAME `term_id` row changes — `term_id` itself never changes**, so every FK pointing at a term (`fee_structures`, `bus_fee_structures`, `fee_concessions`, `fee_receipt_items`) keeps working exactly as before; only the term's own year classification is corrected. **`fee_periods` has the identical historical bug (`FeePeriodsMigrator` had the same fallback)** but no equivalent recoverable column — `year_no` is a CALENDAR year, not the academic-year label, and a Jan/Feb/Mar period's `year_no` legitimately belongs to the PREVIOUS academic year (school year spans the calendar-year boundary), so a blind "year_no starts the academic_year label" match would misclassify those months. Added a READ-ONLY diagnostic addendum to the same script (groups `fee_periods` by year, shows the `year_no` spread) so the user can check whether it's affected too, without attempting a not-fully-verified automatic repair. **`fee_structures` has NO recoverable breadcrumb at all** (no stored legacy year hint column) — flagged as a known gap; a targeted fix there would need a fresh cross-reference against the legacy source DB (same pattern as `Database/align_student_unique_id.sql`), not attempted here since it wasn't the reported symptom. No code change, no deploy — SQL only, user-run. Files: `Database/terms_academic_year_repair.sql`.
Phase 124 ✅  Reports → Transport Students Statement — "Invalid column name 'bus_route_id'" fixed — clicking Generate Report threw a SQL error. `ReportsRepository.GetTransportStudents` joined `LEFT JOIN bus_routes br ON br.bus_route_id = s.bus_route_id` — but `bus_routes`' primary key column is `route_id`, not `bus_route_id` (`bus_route_id` is only the name of the FK column on `students`, per decision — every other repository, e.g. `TransportRepository`, already joins `bus_routes` on `route_id` correctly; this one query had the wrong column name). Fixed to `br.route_id = s.bus_route_id`. Checked the rest of the API for the same mistake (grep for `bus_routes ... ON ...bus_route_id`) — no other occurrences. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) built + **deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`; `GET /version` → fresh `buildDate`). Files: `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/ReportsRepository.cs`.
Phase 125 ✅  Reports → Transport Students Statement — route dropdown snapped back to "All Routes" after selecting one (frontend, same root cause as Phase 124) — `TransportStudentsReport.jsx` built its route dropdown options as `{ label: r.routeName, value: r.busRouteId }`, but `GET /school/transport/routes` returns each route as `{ routeId, routeName, ... }` (the DTO field is `RouteId`, camelCased to `routeId` — `busRouteId` doesn't exist on that response; it's only the name of the FK column on `students`). Every option's `value` was therefore `undefined`, so picking any route set `busRouteId` state to `undefined`, which AntD's `Select` renders as empty/placeholder ("All Routes") — looked exactly like the dropdown refusing the selection. Fixed both the options map and the report-title lookup (`routes.find(r => r.routeId === busRouteId)`) to use `r.routeId`. Checked for the same mistake elsewhere in Reports — none found. eslint + vite build clean. **DEPLOYED:** `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). Files: `ascent-school-app/src/pages/reports/TransportStudentsReport.jsx`.
Phase 126 ✅  Student edit → Transport tab — Bus Route dropdown never populated (wrong API endpoints, same class of bug as Phase 124/125) — `StudentFormPage.jsx` loaded bus routes from `GET /school/master/bus-routes` and buses from `GET /school/master/buses` — **neither route exists**; bus routes and buses are served by `TransportController` at `/school/transport/routes` and `/school/transport/buses` (there is no bus/route CRUD in `MasterDataController` at all). Both calls 404'd and the `.catch(() => {})` on each swallowed the error silently, leaving `busRoutes`/`buses` permanently empty — so the Transport tab's Bus Route and Bus dropdowns had no options to select, on every student edit. Fixed both URLs; the downstream `routeOptions`/`busOptions` mapping (`r.routeId`/`b.busId`) was already correct, so no other change was needed. Checked the rest of the app for the same wrong paths — none found. eslint (2 pre-existing unrelated hook-dependency warnings only) + vite build clean. **DEPLOYED:** `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). **Follow-up (same day, user request):** Bus Route and Bus dropdowns now filter to Active-only (`status` Active / legacy `'Y'` / null — same convention as `languageOptions` a few lines above in this file), but always keep the student's CURRENTLY assigned route/bus even if it's since been made Inactive (`r.routeId === student?.busRouteId` / `b.busId === student?.busId` escape hatch), so opening an existing assignment can't silently blank it — same safety pattern as the Phase 120 language dropdowns. Redeployed (all 8 flavors). Files: `ascent-school-app/src/pages/students/StudentFormPage.jsx`.

Phase 129 ✅  Android — Exam Timetable (student + teacher) + web Hall Ticket report now auto-fills from Exam Master — analyzed first (user asked for a plan, not code): `exam_master` (Master Data → Exam Master) already captures a per-subject `exam_date` per class+exam type+year when staff set up an exam, but **nothing read it** — the web Marks grid/Marks Card only use its max-marks/activity/grade-type columns, and the Exam Hall Ticket report made clerks retype dates by hand every time into a disconnected free-text form. Decisions taken (user: "go with your recommendation"): **date-only** (no time/venue field — a schema change, skipped for v1), a **tab inside the existing Marks screen** (not a new home-screen tile, keeps exam-related things together), **teacher side included** in the same pass (near-free once the student endpoint exists, mirrors the Phase 127 Events precedent), and **fix the Hall Ticket report** to read real dates instead of manual entry. **Backend (no schema change — reads existing `exam_master.exam_date`):** `ExamTimetableGroupDto`/`ExamTimetableSubjectDto` added to the existing `Core/DTOs/Mobile/Data/MarksDto.cs` (not a new file — sidesteps the old-style-csproj registration gotcha [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]); `MobileDataRepository.GetExamTimetable(dbName, schoolId, classId, academicYearId)` — `exam_master` joined to `exam_types`+`subjects`, `WHERE exam_status='Active' AND exam_date IS NOT NULL` (a subject with no date simply doesn't appear — no "TBA" placeholder rows), grouped by exam type and **ordered by each group's earliest exam date** (not `exam_types.display_order`, which doesn't reliably track chronology when multiple exam types are active at once). New `GET /mobile/student/exam-timetable?academicYearId=` on `MobileStudentController` (mirrors `GetMarks`'s `academicYearId<=0` → resolve-latest-year pattern exactly; classId resolved from `Mobile.ClassName` the same way Announcements/Events already do). New `GET /mobile/teacher/exam-timetable?classId=` on `MobileTeacherController` (`classId` required, like Announcements — unlike Events, a timetable is inherently per-class; year resolved via the already-injected `_marks.GetCurrentAcademicYearId`, same as the other teacher marks endpoints). **Android:** `ExamTimetableGroupDto`/`ExamTimetableSubjectDto` Kotlin data classes in `ApiModels.kt`; `ApiService.getExamTimetable`/`getTeacherExamTimetable`; `StudentRepository`/`TeacherRepository` wrappers. New shared `ui/marks/ExamTimetableScreen.kt` (`ExamTimetableUiState` sealed class + composable, same shared-uiState pattern as the Phase 127 `EventsScreen` refactor) — one card per exam type, subject rows show `dd MMM yyyy` + day-of-week (`java.time.LocalDate`, already used elsewhere — Phase 107 confirmed core-library desugaring); empty state "Exam timetable not published yet" rather than blank/placeholder rows. **Parent:** `MarksViewModel` gains `timetableState` + `loadTimetable()` (loaded alongside `load()` on init and on year change — no per-tab-switch network call); `MarksScreen` gains a `TabRow` ("Results" | "Timetable", same `TabRow`/`Tab` pattern as `FeeScreen`'s category tabs) switching between the existing marks content and the new `ExamTimetableScreen`; `HomeScreen`'s Marks pull-to-refresh and the global refresh-icon handler both now reload the timetable too. **Teacher:** `TeacherViewModel` gains `examTimetableState`/`loadExamTimetable(classId)`; new `TeacherExamTimetableScreen.kt` (Scaffold+back arrow wrapper, same shape as `TeacherAnnouncementScreen`/`TeacherEventsScreen`) wrapping the shared `ExamTimetableScreen`; `TeacherHomeScreen` gains a 7th tile "Exam Timetable" (`Icons.Default.Schedule`, slate gradient `#334155→#64748B` — the one color not already used by the other 6 tiles) + matching list-mode `ActionCard`, both `enabled = cls != null` (needs a class, unlike Events which needs none) → `onExamTimetable(cls.classId)`; `MainActivity.kt` gains `TeacherScreen.ExamTimetable(classId: Int)` + routing. `versionCode` 44→**45**, `versionName` "4.4"→**"4.5"**. **Fix #4 — Hall Ticket report (`ExamHallTicketReport.jsx`, web, frontend-only):** `handleLoad` now also fetches `GET /school/exam-master?academicYearId=&examTypeId=&classId=` (already existed, Phase 106 — no backend change) alongside students/subjects, and pre-fills each schedule row's Exam Date (formatted `DD/MM/YYYY`, matching the field's existing placeholder) and Max Marks (from `subMaxMarks`) from the matching `exam_master` row; a subject with **no** matching row now starts **unchecked** (previously every subject started checked with blank fields, forcing the clerk to either fill in or manually uncheck every irrelevant one each time) — the clerk can still check it and type values by hand, so nothing is locked down, just pre-populated where real data exists. Time is still typed manually (matches the date-only decision above — `exam_master` has no time column). A toast nudges "no exam schedule found... set up Exam Master first" when the class/exam has nothing scheduled yet. Phase 130 ✅  Android — Teacher can message any student first (mobile) — analyzed before building: parent↔teacher messaging (Phase 92) only let the PARENT start a thread (`MobileMessagesController.SendMessage` → `GetOrCreateThread`); the teacher side (`MobileTeacherMessagesController.Reply`) required an existing `threadId` and 404'd otherwise, and `GetThreadsForTeacher` only ever showed threads with `last_message_at IS NOT NULL` — a teacher had no way to reach a parent who hadn't written first, and no roster to pick from at all. **Design evolved through several rounds of user clarification, landing on the simplest version:** started as "gate it behind a permission code, Principal-only, restricted to the teacher's own classes otherwise," but the user progressively simplified it — first to "every teacher can message any class's student," then explicitly "even if not `class_teacher_assignments`-assigned," and finally **"principal and teacher — no difference, they can message any [currently-enrolled] student."** End result: **no permission code, no role check, no `class_teacher_assignments` restriction on who can be messaged** — any staff member who can log into the mobile teacher app can start a conversation with any student in the school. **Backend (no schema change, no new tables):** `MessagingRepository` gains `GetAllClassesForMessaging` (all Active classes school-wide), `GetStudentsInClassForMessaging` (current-year Active students in a class, any section), and `GetParentIdForStudent` — resolves which registered parent account to message by looking up the student's CURRENT `student_id` in the tenant DB then querying `parent_children` in the **master** DB (parent_children lives there, not the tenant DB); matches on current `student_id` first, falls back to `admission_no` for a link gone stale because that parent hasn't opened the app since a promotion (mirrors the Phase 18 upsert-by-admission_no reasoning); returns `null` if no parent has ever registered for that student. **Access-model nuance the "no restriction" instruction didn't by itself resolve, so it was decided during implementation rather than asked as yet another question:** unrestricted INITIATION doesn't mean every teacher can read every OTHER teacher's ongoing conversations school-wide — `GetThreadsForTeacher` and `TeacherCanAccessThread` were broadened (EXISTS-based, not a JOIN, so no row fan-out / no DISTINCT needed) to ALSO include a thread if the calling teacher has personally sent a message in it, on top of the existing `class_teacher_assignments` condition — so a teacher who opens a conversation with an out-of-class student can keep finding and using it, without every other unrelated teacher suddenly seeing it too. **A real edge case this surfaced and fixed:** opening a brand-new thread and then immediately viewing it (`GetThread`) would 403 under that rule, because at that instant neither condition is true yet (no assignment, and no message sent by them yet) — added a third condition, `OR NOT EXISTS (... any message in this thread)`, so a just-created empty thread (nothing to leak) is viewable by whoever opened it, until the moment they send their first message, at which point the self-sent-message condition takes over. New `MobileTeacherMessagesController` endpoints: `GET mobile/teacher/messages/classes`, `GET mobile/teacher/messages/students?classId=`, `POST mobile/teacher/messages/open` (`{studentUniqueId}` → resolves parent, creates-or-finds the thread via the existing `GetOrCreateThread`, returns `{threadId}` — does NOT send a message itself; sending still goes through the existing `Reply` endpoint once the thread is open, so no new send-path logic was needed at all). New DTOs `MessagingClassDto`/`MessagingStudentDto`/`OpenConversationRequest` added to the existing `MessagingDtos.cs` (no new file). **Android — the actual chat UI needed ZERO changes:** `TeacherChatScreen` already works for any `threadId` including a freshly-created empty one. New `TeacherNewMessageScreen.kt` (Class dropdown → Student list, `ExposedDropdownMenuBox` matching `TeacherHomeScreen`'s existing pattern) — picking a student calls `openConversation`, then hands the resolved `threadId` straight to the **existing, unmodified** `TeacherChatScreen`. Entry point: a "New Message" `ExtendedFloatingActionButton` on `TeacherMessagesScreen` (new `onNewMessage` param). `TeacherViewModel` gains `messagingClasses`/`messagingStudents`/`isOpeningConversation` state + `loadMessagingClasses`/`loadMessagingStudents`/`openConversation`. `MainActivity.kt` gains `TeacherScreen.NewMessage` + routing. `versionCode` 45→**46**, `versionName` "4.5"→**"4.6"**. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + Android (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL, only the same pre-existing AutoMirrored-icon warning) verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1`; all three new routes confirmed live (`GET .../messages/classes`, `GET .../messages/students?classId=1`, `POST .../messages/open` → all 401 json = registered). **Android-only — not yet built into an APK / released**, rides with the other pending mobile work (see the consolidated pending-Android note). **Not built (out of scope for this pass, per the "no difference" simplification):** no separate "message any student" tier — if a school ever wants only SOME staff to have this and not others, that would need the permission-code approach discussed and explicitly declined during planning.
