Phase 131 ✅  School app — Fees → Transport Receipts (separate from Receipts) — Receipts showed every category of receipt mixed together with no way to see just transport. **Backend:** `GET /school/fees/receipts` gains an optional `category` param — currently only `"Transport"` is recognized, filtered via `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM fee_receipt_items fri WHERE fri.receipt_id = r.receipt_id AND fri.bus_route_id IS NOT NULL)` — the same structural signal `CollectFee` and `BulkImportReceipts` already stamp on transport line items (Phase 28/43), so it works for both web-collected and bulk-imported transport receipts with no new column and no per-receipt category flag needed. Omitted/unrecognized `category` = unfiltered, i.e. `Receipts` itself is completely unchanged. **Frontend:** `ReceiptsPage.jsx` parameterized with optional `category`/`title` props (defaults reproduce today's exact behavior — same pattern the 5 fee **collection** screens already use around `FeeCollectionBase`, just applied to the receipts list for the first time); when `category` is set it's added to the list query, the Card title, and the CSV/PDF export file names/PDF title all follow it. New thin wrapper `TransportReceiptsPage.jsx` (`<ReceiptsPage category="Transport" title="Transport Receipts" />`, mirrors `TransportFeePage.jsx`'s existing wrapper-around-shared-component shape) — new nav item "Transport Receipts" (Fees submenu, under Receipts) + route `/fees/receipts/transport`, gated by the same `FEE_VIEW` permission as Receipts. No DB migration, no APK. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (vite build clean — one pre-existing, unrelated `AppLayout.jsx` lint error confirmed via `git diff` to predate this change, left alone) verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`GET /school/fees/receipts?category=Transport` → 401 json = registered) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). Files: `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/School/FeeController.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/FeeRepository.cs`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/fee/ReceiptsPage.jsx`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/fee/TransportReceiptsPage.jsx` (new), `ascent-school-app/src/App.jsx`, `ascent-school-app/src/layouts/AppLayout.jsx`, `ascent-school-app/src/config/permissions.js`. **Follow-up (same day, user):** the `bus_route_id`-only check missed a real case — a school that set up "Transport Fee" as a regular `fee_type` (e.g. collected via School Fee) instead of the dedicated route-based Transport module never gets a `bus_route_id` on its receipt items. Broadened the `EXISTS` to also match via a `LEFT JOIN fee_types ft`: `ft.fee_type_name LIKE '%transport%'` OR `ft.description LIKE '%transport%'`/`'%bus%'` — the description patterns mirror the EXISTING `BuildCategoryFilter("transport")` branch (used for transport-outstanding classification, same file) so the two stay consistent; the `fee_type_name` check is new, added because that's literally what a school names the fee type. Backend-only; no frontend change needed. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`/school/fees/receipts?category=Transport` → 401 json = still registered).
Phase 132 ✅  Support Tickets — school staff raise Issue/Change tickets, Ascent manages status from the control app — planned first (user asked for a plan), then built exactly to the confirmed decisions: **open to every authenticated staff member** (no permission gate — same reasoning as the Phase 86 self-service Change Password: anyone hitting an issue should be able to report it without needing a grant), **branch-wide visibility** (every ticket for that school branch, not just the raiser's own, so colleagues can see if something was already reported), **any control role can manage tickets** (matches how most control endpoints already work), **no attachments, no notifications** (both explicitly declined by the user — flagged as future v2 work, not built). **New `ascent_master` table `support_tickets`** (master table 18) — deliberately a MASTER table, not a tenant table, since it must be visible across every school group from one control-app screen regardless of which tenant DB a ticket came from (same reasoning as `device_push_tokens`, Phase 91). `raised_by_user_id` has **no FK** — tenant `users.user_id` is a different DB per group, so `raised_by_name`/`raised_by_username` are snapshots (mirrors `messages.sender_name`, Phase 92) so a ticket stays meaningful even if that staff member later leaves. Columns: group_id/school_id (FKs), db_name, raised_by_*, ticket_type (Issue\|Change), priority (Low\|Medium\|High\|Critical), subject, description, module (free text — Fees/Attendance/etc., not DB-driven, same convention as Gender), status (Open\|InProgress\|Done\|Cancelled), assigned_to_control_user_id (FK → control_users), resolution_notes, created_at (IST default)/updated_at/resolved_at. `Database/master_tables.sql` updated + new idempotent `Database/support_tickets_migration.sql` (run ONCE on ascent_master, not per tenant — same rule as device_push_tokens). **Backend:** `SupportTicketDto`/`CreateSupportTicketRequest`/`UpdateSupportTicketStatusRequest` in a new `Core/DTOs/Control/SupportTickets/SupportTicketDtos.cs`; `SupportTicketRepository` (`Data/Repositories/Control/`) — dual-use shape, `GetMasterConnection()` throughout, instantiated from BOTH a school controller (create + branch-scoped list/detail) and a control controller (cross-group list with group/school/control-user-name joins + status update); `resolved_at` is set the moment status moves to Done/Cancelled and **cleared if reopened**, so it always reflects the CURRENT closed state rather than "closed at least once". Two new controllers: school-side `SupportTicketsController` (`school/support-tickets` — GET list, GET {id}, POST create) and control-side **`ControlSupportTicketsController`** (`control/support-tickets` — GET list w/ groupId/schoolId/status/ticketType/date/search filters, GET {id}, PUT {id}/status) — **deliberately NOT both named `SupportTicketsController`**, to avoid the exact Phase 87 trap (two controllers sharing a bare class name silently kill BOTH their routing under old-style Web API attribute routing); mirrors that phase's own fix naming (`SchoolUploadsController`/`ControlUploadsController`). All new files registered in the 3 old-style csprojs [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]. **Frontend — ascent-school-app:** new top-level nav item "Support Tickets" (after Conversations, before Settings) + `pages/support/SupportTicketsPage.jsx` — "Raise a Ticket" modal (Type/Priority/Module/Subject/Description) + a branch-wide list with a detail Drawer (shows resolution notes once closed); no `PATH_PERM` entry needed (an absent entry already means "always allowed", same as Dashboard). **Frontend — ascent-control-app:** the app's first-ever SECOND top-level nav item (it previously had only "School Groups") — `pages/support-tickets/SupportTicketsPage.jsx` — cross-group table (Group/School/Subject/Type/Priority/Status/RaisedBy/Created) with Group→School cascading filter dropdowns (School disabled until a Group is picked, matching the school app's own Class→Section cascading convention) + Status/Type/search filters, row click → Drawer with a Status select + resolution-notes textarea + Save (`PUT .../status`). **Gotcha caught before it shipped:** this control app's `api.get()` wrapper takes only a bare path string (no axios-style `{ params }` second argument, unlike the axios interface it otherwise mimics) — an initial draft passed `{ params }` which would have silently sent an unfiltered request; fixed to build the query string into the path with `URLSearchParams`, matching the school app's own established convention. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint clean, vite build clean) + control app (dependencies weren't installed yet in this environment — `npm install` run once, then eslint clean + vite build clean) all verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`school/support-tickets` + `control/support-tickets` → both 401 json = registered) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). **Control app NOT deployed from here** — there is still no `deploy-control.ps1` in this repo (confirmed, same gap noted in Phase 87); `ascent-control-app/dist/` is built and ready — needs the user's usual manual upload to `admin.edu-care.in`. **Not built (explicitly declined by the user, noted for a future pass):** file attachments (would need Ascent's own storage config — the existing R2 integration is per-school, awkward to reuse for an internal Ascent tool) and any notification on new-ticket/status-change (no SMTP integration exists anywhere in this codebase today — only SMS for OTP/parent comms). **SQL (user runs — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]]):** `Database/support_tickets_migration.sql` on `ascent_master`. Phase 133 ✅  Control app deployed to production + reusable `deploy-control.ps1` + a self-inflicted `/api` outage fixed same-session — closed the long-standing gap (flagged since Phase 87) that the control app had no deploy tooling and was "deployed manually." Investigated where it actually lives (no config anywhere named it): read-only FTP listing + fetching the live `https://edu-care.in/` HTML (`<title>ascent-control-app</title>`, matching Vite asset hashes) confirmed it's deployed at the **root** of `edu-care.in`'s `httpdocs/`, sibling to `/httpdocs/api` and the per-school privacy-policy folders — not a separate `admin.edu-care.in` subdomain (that doesn't exist). Added a `control` section to `deploy/deploy.config.json` (`appDir`, `apiUrl`, `remotePath:"/httpdocs"`) and `deploy/deploy-control.ps1` (build + `Send-FtpTree`, additive-only like every other deploy script). **Caught before shipping:** the control app has no `.env.production` — a plain `npm run build` silently falls back to `VITE_API_URL=http://localhost:62845`, so the script explicitly sets `$env:VITE_API_URL` before building. **Self-inflicted outage (caught and fixed the same session):** the control app's `public/web.config` SPA-fallback rewrite rule (`IsFile`/`IsDirectory` negate, standard pattern used by every other app in this repo per Phase 59) sits at the **docroot** here, and its `IsFile`/`IsDirectory` checks don't recognize the `/api` subfolder's own ASP.NET-routed (non-physical-file) URLs — so EVERY request under `https://edu-care.in/api/*` got silently rewritten to the control app's `index.html` (200 OK, HTML body) instead of reaching the .NET API, breaking control-app login and any other traffic through that shared hostname. Fixed by adding `<add input="{REQUEST_URI}" pattern="^/api(/|$)" negate="true" />` to the rule's conditions; redeployed; verified `/api/version`, `/api/branding`, `/api/control/auth/login` all return real JSON again. **Rule: any app sharing a docroot with `/api` (or another routed subfolder) must explicitly exclude that path from its own SPA-fallback rewrite — `IsFile`/`IsDirectory` alone does not detect a sibling application's extensionless routes.** No data was affected — purely an IIS routing bug, self-diagnosed and fixed within the same session before the user's next login attempt. Files: `deploy/deploy.config.json`, `deploy/deploy-control.ps1` (new), `ascent-control-app/public/web.config`.
Phase 134 ✅  Android — Exam Timetable given its own dedicated tile on the student/parent home screen (previously a "Timetable" sub-tab inside Marks, Phase 129) — user wanted parity with the teacher side, which already has Exam Timetable as its own tile. Added `Feature("Exam Timetable", Icons.Default.Schedule, 9, slate gradient)` to the single `features` list in `HomeScreen.kt` (Phase 101's single-source-of-truth list — this alone gives it both a Tiles-mode tile and a Tabs-mode bottom-nav entry); `FeatureContent(9)` renders the existing shared `ExamTimetableScreen` backed by `marksVm.timetableState`/`loadTimetable()` — no new ViewModel, no new API call, reuses exactly what Phase 129 already built. **Reverted `MarksScreen.kt`** back to a plain marks-only view (dropped the internal "Results/Timetable" `TabRow`) since the timetable now has its own dedicated access point — avoids showing the same feature in two places and matches how the teacher side already keeps Marks Entry and Exam Timetable as fully separate screens. `versionCode` 46→**47**, `versionName` "4.6"→**"4.7"**. Android-only, no API/DB change; `:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL (only the same pre-existing AutoMirrored-icon deprecation warnings). Rides in the same still-unreleased APK as everything else pending Play submission. Files: `AscentSchoolsMobile/app/src/main/java/com/ascentschools/mobile/ui/home/HomeScreen.kt`, `.../ui/marks/MarksScreen.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 135 ✅  Mobile App Adoption report (school app) + `parent_children.student_unique_id` — planned first (user asked for analysis only), then built to the confirmed approach. **The question:** which students' parents have the mobile app / never installed it. **Signal chosen:** a `parent_children` row (master DB) = that student's parent has completed OTP verification at least once — unambiguous, per-student, no notification-permission dependency (unlike `device_push_tokens`, which is per-*parent* not per-child and undercounts anyone who declined push permission — used only as a secondary "last active" hint, never as the primary signal). **The cross-DB join gap surfaced during planning:** `parent_children.student_id` is the tenant `students.student_id` at the time of first link — a year-specific IDENTITY that goes stale after a promotion the parent hasn't reopened the app since; `admission_no` isn't a safe fallback either since some schools renumber it on promotion (the exact Phase 76 bug). Fix: added **`parent_children.student_unique_id`** (the stable cross-year id already used everywhere else for this reason) — written going forward by the existing link create/refresh paths (`MobileParentAuthRepository.CreateChildLink`/`UpdateChildLink`, called from `MobileAuthController`'s `verify-otp` upsert loop and `ParentLinkChild`), sourced from `students.student_unique_id` which `MobileStudentAuthRepository.GetStudentsByParentMobile`/`GetStudentByAdmissionNo` now also select. **User raised a real concern before approving:** a freshly-added, all-NULL column would make the report show 0 installs until parents happened to re-log in. Resolved two ways: (1) the report's matching logic is a **fallback chain**, never dependent on the new column alone — primary match by `student_unique_id` when present, else by `admission_no` (identical to how it would work if the column didn't exist), so the report is correct from day one regardless of backfill timing; (2) new idempotent `Database/parent_children_student_unique_id_migration.sql` **backfills every existing link immediately** (not "wait for re-logins") via a cursor over `DISTINCT db_name` + dynamic cross-database `UPDATE` (three-part name via `QUOTENAME`, requires tenant DBs on the same SQL Server instance as `ascent_master` — true here; per-DB `TRY/CATCH` so one unreachable tenant DB doesn't abort the rest) matching by `student_id` first, else `admission_no+school_id` (latest year on a tie) — same priority the report itself would use. Purely additive; never overwrites an existing value; safely re-runnable. `Database/master_tables.sql`'s `parent_children` definition updated to match for new deployments. **Backend:** `ReportsRepository.GetMobileAppAdoption` — reads the tenant roster (Class/Section/AcademicYear filters, same shape as `GetDetainedStudents`) and separately reads master-DB `parent_children` + `device_push_tokens` (scoped to `group_id`+`school_id`) via the SAME `IConnectionFactory`'s `GetMasterConnection()` (`ReportsRepository` already had it — no new dependency), merging the two in C# (two-connections-merge-in-code, the same pattern `SupportTicketRepository`/`SchoolSettingsRepository` already use for master-DB access from a school controller — no attempt at a single cross-DB SQL query). New `GET /school/reports/mobile-app-adoption?academicYearId=&classId=&sectionId=` on `ReportsController`; new `MobileAppAdoptionRowDto` in `ReportDtos.cs`. **Frontend:** new `pages/reports/MobileAppAdoptionReport.jsx` (Class/Section/Year filters + a Status filter [All/Installed/Not installed], modeled directly on `DetainedStudentsReport.jsx`) — table with Has App tag, Linked On, and Last Active (approx, from the push-token hint, clearly separate from the primary signal) + an installed/total summary line + PDF/CSV export; added as a 21st entry (`MobileOutlined` icon) to the existing `REPORTS` array in `ReportsPage.jsx` — no new route or `PATH_PERM` entry needed, since Reports is one route with an internal sidebar switch, already gated by `REPORTS.VIEW`. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint clean, vite build clean) verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`GET /school/reports/mobile-app-adoption` → 401 json = registered) + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors). **SQL (user runs — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]]):** `Database/parent_children_student_unique_id_migration.sql` on `ascent_master` (run once, not per tenant) — the report works even before this runs (admission_no fallback), but accuracy for promoted students improves immediately once it does. Files: `API/AscentSchools.Core/DTOs/School/Reports/ReportDtos.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/ReportsRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/School/ReportsController.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/Mobile/MobileStudentAuthRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/Mobile/MobileParentAuthRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/Mobile/MobileAuthController.cs`, `Database/master_tables.sql`, `Database/parent_children_student_unique_id_migration.sql` (new), `ascent-school-app/src/pages/reports/MobileAppAdoptionReport.jsx` (new), `ascent-school-app/src/pages/reports/ReportsPage.jsx`.
Phase 136 ✅  Android — "Birthdays Today" (teacher tile) + birthday-wishes popup (parent/student app) — planned first (user asked for a plan, not code), then built to the confirmed approach. **Teacher side:** new `GET /mobile/teacher/birthdays?classId=` (classId **optional**, same "no restriction" pattern as Events/Messages/New Message — any teacher sees any student, no class-teacher-assignment gate) — `MobileDataRepository.GetBirthdaysToday(tenantDbName, schoolId, classId, month, day, includeFeb29)` filters the current-year roster (`students.status IN ('Active','Y')`, same current-academic-year subquery pattern used everywhere else, so Blocked students are excluded but Detained ones still show per the existing Block-vs-Detain convention) by `MONTH(date_of_birth)`/`DAY(date_of_birth)`. **`month`/`day` are computed in the CONTROLLER via `TimeHelper.IstToday()`, never SQL `GETDATE()`** — reinforces the Phase 98/103/105 rule (server runs US Eastern); note the repository itself COULDN'T call `TimeHelper` directly even if tempted to — `AscentSchools.Data` doesn't reference `AscentSchools.API`, so "today" has to be computed by the caller and passed in as plain ints, which is also the cleaner layering anyway. **Feb 29 handled (per the user's earlier-approved recommendation):** a new `includeFeb29` bool, true only when today is Feb 28 of a non-leap year, ORs in `MONTH=2 AND DAY=29` so a leap-day student isn't skipped in off years. New `BirthdayStudentDto` added to the existing `Core/DTOs/Mobile/Data/StudentProfileDto.cs` (no new file — sidesteps [[api-csproj-manual-registration]], same trick Phase 129 used). **Android:** new `ui/teacher/TeacherBirthdaysScreen.kt` — an in-screen Class dropdown defaulting to "All Classes" (`ExposedDropdownMenuBox`, same pattern as `TeacherNewMessageScreen`) over a list showing Name/Class·Section/Admission No + a "Turning N today 🎉" line computed client-side from the DOB year; `TeacherViewModel` gets a plain `birthdays`/`isLoadingBirthdays`/`birthdaysError` state trio (matches the Homework/Announcements list pattern, not the shared-sealed-class pattern Events/ExamTimetable use, since Birthdays has no parent-side equivalent screen to share). 8th tile on `TeacherHomeScreen` (cake icon, pink `#DB2777→#EC4899` — the one gradient not already used by the other 7 tiles), always enabled (no class required, like Events); `TeacherScreen.Birthdays` route in `MainActivity.kt`. **Parent/student side — needed ZERO new backend work:** `date_of_birth` was already returned by the existing `GET /mobile/student/profile` call (`StudentProfileDto.DateOfBirth`, present since before Phase 112 merely stopped *displaying* it) — the popup is pure client-side comparison against data already being fetched on every Home load. `HomeScreen.kt` (parent) collects `profileVm.uiState`, and on a `LaunchedEffect(profileState)` compares the DOB's month/day to `LocalDate.now()` (device-local — **deliberately NOT an IST-conversion case**, unlike the teacher-side server fix above: the phone is physically in India, so its own local calendar day is already correct, no server-timezone bug class applies here). **Once-per-day, not once-per-open (per the earlier-agreed design):** new `TokenStore.lastBirthdayPopupDate` (plain string pref, "yyyy-MM-dd") — the popup only fires when that date differs from today; set the moment the dialog is dismissed. **Deliberately NOT preserved across `clear()`** (unlike deviceId/branding/tilesView) — a re-login later the same day re-showing the popup once more is harmless, not worth the extra special-casing. **Known v1 limitation (flagged during planning, not built):** the popup only checks the *currently selected* child — a multi-child parent whose non-selected child's birthday is today won't see it unless they switch to that child; checking every linked child would need a new endpoint, offered and deferred. **A real ordering bug caught during implementation (not shipped):** the first draft referenced `profileVm` and the new `showBirthdayPopup` state in a `LaunchedEffect` placed BEFORE their `remember{}` declarations later in the same composable — a Kotlin forward-reference that would have failed to compile; fixed by moving the new state/effect to right after the existing `val profileVm = remember { ... }` block (after all the ViewModels, before the live-badges section) instead of splicing it into the earlier children/branding effect. `versionCode` 47→**48**, `versionName` "4.7"→**"4.8"**. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + Android (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL — only the same pre-existing AutoMirrored-icon/Divider deprecation warnings) verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`GET /mobile/teacher/birthdays` → 401 json = registered). **Android-only for the client half — not yet built into an APK / released**, rides with the other pending mobile work. Files: `API/AscentSchools.Core/DTOs/Mobile/Data/StudentProfileDto.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/Mobile/MobileDataRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/Mobile/MobileTeacherController.cs`, `AscentSchoolsMobile/app/src/main/java/com/ascentschools/mobile/data/api/ApiModels.kt`, `.../data/api/ApiService.kt`, `.../data/repository/TeacherRepository.kt`, `.../data/local/TokenStore.kt`, `.../ui/teacher/TeacherViewModel.kt`, `.../ui/teacher/TeacherHomeScreen.kt`, `.../ui/teacher/TeacherBirthdaysScreen.kt` (new), `.../ui/home/HomeScreen.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts`.
Phase 137 ✅  Daily Homework (and Homework List) — subject list wasn't in Class Subjects master's order — both pages load their subject dropdown/grid via `GET /school/class-subjects/for-class?classId=`, backed by `ClassSubjectRepository.GetSubjectsForClass`, which `ORDER BY sub.subject_name` (alphabetical) — ignoring `display_order`, the whole point of the "Order" column in Master Data → Class Subjects. Root cause: this query is intentionally **cross-year** (`DISTINCT` subjects mapped to the class across ALL academic years, no year filter — Daily Homework has no year picker), unlike `GetMapping` (the year-scoped Master Data tab query, which already correctly orders by `ISNULL(cs.display_order, 9999), sub.subject_name`) — being cross-year, a naive `ORDER BY cs.display_order` isn't well-defined when the same subject has a DIFFERENT display_order in different years' mappings. Fixed with a `ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY subject_id ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC)` picking each subject's **most recent year's** `display_order` (same "latest year wins on a cross-year collision" convention already used elsewhere in this codebase for subject/term lookups), then `ORDER BY ISNULL(display_order, 9999), subject_name`. Backend-only; no DTO/frontend change — both pages already just render whatever order the API returns. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) built + **deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`; `/version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-09-22 09:00 UTC`). Files: `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/ClassSubjectRepository.cs`.
Phase 138 ✅  Exam Master gains an exam TIME (in addition to the existing date), reflected in Exam Timetable (mobile student + teacher) and the Exam Hall Ticket print — new `exam_master.exam_time VARCHAR(5) NULL` ("HH:mm", 24-hour — a plain wall-clock time-of-day has no IST/server-timezone concerns, unlike a business timestamp, so no `TimeHelper` involvement needed here). **Backend:** `ExamMasterDto`/`CreateExamMasterRequest`/`UpdateExamMasterRequest`/`ExamMasterBulkRow` all gain `ExamTime`; `ExamMasterRepository` Get/Create/Update/BulkImport all carry it through (bulk import parses leniently via `DateTime.TryParse` + reformats to `HH:mm`, bad values silently dropped rather than failing the row — same tolerance as the other bulk-import fields in this file). `ExamMasterController` needed no change (passes the whole DTO through). **Exam Timetable (mobile):** `ExamTimetableSubjectDto` (`Core/DTOs/Mobile/Data/MarksDto.cs`) gains `ExamTime`; `MobileDataRepository.GetExamTimetable` selects `em.exam_time` and maps it through — both the student endpoint (`GET /mobile/student/exam-timetable`) and teacher endpoint (`GET /mobile/teacher/exam-timetable`) share this one repository method, so both picked it up with a single change. **Android:** `ExamTimetableSubjectDto.examTime: String?` + `ExamTimetableScreen.kt`'s `SubjectDateRow` now shows the time (`h:mm a`, via `java.time.LocalTime`) next to the day-of-week, e.g. "Monday · 10:00 AM" — shared by both the parent Marks-screen tab and the teacher's own screen, so one change covers both. **Exam Hall Ticket print (`ExamHallTicketReport.jsx`):** the Time column — previously always blank/manual per the Phase 129 note "not captured in exam_master" — now pre-fills from `exam_master.examTime` (formatted `h:mm A`) the same way Date and Max Marks already did, while staying free-text/editable so staff can still extend it into a range (e.g. "10:00 AM – 12:00 PM") if they want one printed. **Frontend (`ExamMasterTab.jsx`):** new `TimePicker` (`HH:mm`, 5-min steps) next to the Date field in the Add/Edit form, a Time column in the list, and `ExamTime` added to the bulk-import CSV template/example/preview/column-reference text. **Bonus fix while in this file (not asked, but the schema addition made it possible and it was silently losing data):** `AscentMigration/Migrators/ExamMasterMigrator.cs` — legacy `SAS_ExamNam.ExamDatTim` is a full DATETIME (date **and** time), but the migrator only ever copied it into `exam_date` (a DATE column), silently discarding the time-of-day on every migrated school. Now also extracts `exam_time` from the same source value (`00:00:00` treated as "no time entered," not midnight — a real exam is never scheduled at midnight, so this avoids inventing a fake time for old rows that never had one). No other report/screen touches `exam_date` (checked — Marks Card and the toppers/failed-students reports don't reference it). API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + AscentMigration (`dotnet build`, exit 0) + school app (eslint clean, vite build clean) + Android (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin` BUILD SUCCESSFUL) all verified. `versionCode` 48→**49**, `versionName` "4.8"→**"4.9"**. **DEPLOYED: full `deploy-api.ps1` + `deploy-school.ps1 -School all` (all 8 flavors).** **⚠️ LOAD-BEARING — SQL MUST BE RUN NOW:** the API was deployed selecting/inserting/updating a column (`exam_master.exam_time`) that does not yet exist on any tenant DB until `Database/exam_master_time_migration.sql` is run — until then, **every** Master Data → Exam Master action (list/add/edit/bulk-import) AND the mobile Exam Timetable (both student and teacher) will 500 with "Invalid column name 'exam_time'" on every school. This is the reverse of the usual "migration before API" ordering (caught too late this time, not before deploying) — run the migration on every tenant DB immediately to restore these features; nothing else on the site is affected (Hall Ticket, Marks Card, and everything else that doesn't touch `exam_master` directly is fine in the meantime). Android is compile-verified only — not yet in a released APK, rides with the rest of the pending mobile work. Phase 139 ✅  Migration tools — legacy transport "Yes"/"Y" flag now maps to "Bus" — investigated first (user asked to check, not implement): both `AscentMigration/Migrators/StudentsMigrator.cs` and `AscentSyncTool/Mappers/Mappers.cs` copied legacy `StuTransportTyp` straight through with only `.Trim()`, no value translation. The school app's Transport Type dropdown (`StudentFormPage.jsx`) only recognizes `Bus`/`Walking`/`Van`/`Other`, so a legacy "Yes"/"Y" flag landed as literal unmatched text — AntD's `Select` shows the field blank on that student's edit form even though the raw DB value is still sitting there. Fixed in both tools (kept as two copies since neither tool references the other's code — `AscentMigration` and `AscentSyncTool` are separate standalone apps): new `MapTransportType(legacy)` helper (mirrors the existing `MapStatus`-style helper already in each file) — `"Yes"`/`"Y"` (case-insensitive) → `"Bus"`; anything else (blank, "No", or an already-sensible value) passes through unchanged, per the narrow scope actually requested — no attempt to also map "No"/blank or guess at other legacy conventions not confirmed. Both `AscentMigration` (`dotnet build`, exit 0) and `AscentSyncTool` (MSBuild Debug, exit 0) compile-verified. **Not deployed/redistributed** — these are standalone desktop tools the user runs locally; `AscentSyncTool.exe` still needs redistributing to school machines per the usual process (same as every other AscentSyncTool phase) before this reaches an actual sync run, and `AscentMigration` is run ad-hoc per new-school onboarding. Files: `AscentMigration/Migrators/StudentsMigrator.cs`, `AscentSyncTool/Mappers/Mappers.cs`.
Phase 140 ✅  Migration + Sync Tool — bus assignment wired up (both `bus_route_id` AND `bus_id`, both tools) — investigated first (user asked to check `BusID` handling, found it unused everywhere), then implemented on explicit go-ahead. **AscentMigration (`StudentsMigrator.cs`) — `BusID` → `students.bus_id`:** added the missing second half of transport migration, mirroring the EXISTING `StuBusRoute`→`bus_route_id` two-step lookup exactly (`LoadLegacyBusNameMap` reads legacy `SAS_BussData.BusID→BusNam`; `LoadDestBusIdMap` reads dest `buses.bus_name→bus_id`, Active-row-wins on a duplicate name — same convention as the route lookup); `bus_id` added to the INSERT column list + params. A miss at either step logs a warning and leaves `bus_id` NULL (row still migrates) — same non-fatal posture as the route lookup, never the transport-type migration. **AscentSyncTool — route AND bus now flow through Export Students (previously neither did):** the tool's own `LegacyStudent`/`GetStudents` had NO bus columns at all (not even selected from the legacy DB) — fixed by giving `GetStudents`' query a table alias plus two `LEFT JOIN`s directly in SQL (`SAS_BusRoutes br ON br.RouteID = s.StuBusRoute`, `SAS_BussData bd ON bd.BusID = s.BusID`), so the query itself resolves straight to **names** (`RouteName`, `BusName`) — no separate C# lookup-map step needed in the sync tool, since the legacy IDs and their name tables live in the exact same local legacy DB it already queries. **API side (`StudentBulkRow`, shared by BOTH the sync tool AND the web CSV import since they hit the same `POST /school/students/bulk` endpoint):** gained `RouteName`/`BusName` — matched by name server-side in `StudentRepository.BulkCreate`, same "send the name, resolve to an id server-side" convention already used for `ClassName`/`SectionName`/`FeeCategory` on this same DTO. New `routeMap`/`busMap` built once per import (Active-row-wins on a duplicate name — extracted into a shared `AddActiveWins` helper, since both `classMap`/`catMap`-style exact lookups AND this Active-wins style now coexist in the same method). **Deliberately lenient, not an error:** an unmatched route/bus name leaves `bus_route_id`/`bus_id` NULL rather than failing the whole row — a typo'd route/bus name shouldn't block an otherwise-good student import, matching how the optional date fields already behave in this same method. Wired into **both** the INSERT and the Upsert UPDATE path (transport assignment can change over time, same as `transport_type` which was already updatable on upsert despite that block's stale "left untouched" comment). No DB migration needed — `students.bus_route_id`/`bus_id` already existed in the schema; this phase is pure wiring. `AscentMigration` (`dotnet build`, exit 0), `AscentSyncTool` (MSBuild Debug, exit 0), and the API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) all compile-verified. **DEPLOYED:** full `deploy-api.ps1` (`/version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-09-26 13:42 UTC`). **Not redistributed:** `AscentSyncTool.exe` needs its usual rebuild/redistribution to school machines before a real sync run picks this up; `AscentMigration` is run ad-hoc per school onboarding, whenever next used. **Not touched (out of scope, web CSV import):** `StudentsImportPage.jsx`'s column-reference table/template doesn't mention `RouteName`/`BusName` — the capability exists on the shared endpoint now, but exposing it in the web bulk-import UI wasn't asked and wasn't added. Files: `AscentMigration/Migrators/StudentsMigrator.cs`, `AscentSyncTool/Data/LegacyDb.cs`, `AscentSyncTool/Models/LegacyModels.cs`, `AscentSyncTool/Models/ApiModels.cs`, `AscentSyncTool/Mappers/Mappers.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Core/DTOs/School/Students/BulkImportDtos.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/StudentRepository.cs`.
Phase 141 ✅  Daily Homework → SMS to parents on save (setting-gated, automatic) — new per-branch **School Settings → Notifications → "Send SMS when Daily Homework is saved"** toggle (`school_settings.homework_sms_enabled BIT`, default OFF); when ON, saving Daily Homework fires one SMS per parent of every student in the section(s) the homework was saved for — no per-save confirmation prompt, unlike the existing Attendance→Absentee SMS flow (Phase 64), which is a manual per-action confirm; this one is fully automatic once the setting is on, closer in spirit to the existing push-notification trigger (Phase 91) that already fires on the same save. **Reuses the entire per-school SMS infrastructure from Phase 55/60 as-is** — a new template key, `HOMEWORK`, is manageable through the EXISTING Settings → SMS Gateway page with zero code change there (that page's template CRUD was already fully generic/key-agnostic, confirmed before building — "Other keys are for future use" was already in its own hint text); the school enters the actual message text + DLT template id there once Ascent gives them the copy (the user's own next step — "I'll provide SMS details once this step is done"). Reuses the SAME placeholder set already established (`{name}`/`{class}`/`{admissionNo}`/`{date}`) — no new placeholder needed. **Backend:** new `SmsRepository.GetHomeworkRecipients(dbName, schoolId, classId, sectionId)` (mirrors `GetAbsentRecipients`'s shape, filtered by class+section instead of absence; **added current-academic-year scoping this query didn't otherwise have** — same class of "stale prior-year student" gap Phase 66/118 fixed elsewhere, proactively avoided here rather than repeating it); new `Helpers/HomeworkSmsNotifier.cs` (new file, registered in the csproj per [[api-csproj-manual-registration]]) — mirrors `PushNotifier`'s fire-and-forget `Task.Run` convention exactly (primitives captured on the request thread, never touches `TenantContext` inside the task — Phase 61 rule; wraps the whole body in try/catch so an SMS failure can never affect a homework save that's already succeeded): checks the setting → loads the school's SMS account + the `HOMEWORK` template (silent no-op if either is missing/inactive/no DLT id, matching the same "unconfigured = never breaks a create" posture as push) → for each of `CreateBatchHomework`'s **already-resolved** target sections (`BatchHomeworkResult.Sections` — the same list the push-notification loop right above it already iterates, so a class-wide save correctly sends once per real section, not once for a fake "whole class" recipient), loads recipients and sends+logs each one via the existing `SmsHelper.SendSms`/`SmsRepository.LogSms` (new `SmsType = "Homework"` log value — plain string column, no schema constraint to touch). Wired into `HomeworkController.CreateBatchHomework` right after the existing push-notification block. **Deliberately sequential, not `Task.WhenAll`-parallel** (unlike `SmsController.Send`'s user-initiated bulk send) — this trigger is already fire-and-forget from the caller's perspective (the homework save already returned), so there's no HTTP-timeout pressure driving the parallel-dispatch need that motivated Phase 12's original design. `SchoolSettingsDto`/`UpdateSchoolSettingsRequest` gain `HomeworkSmsEnabled`; `SchoolSettingsRepository.Get`/`Upsert` (master DB) carry it through. **Frontend:** new "Notifications" card on `SchoolSettingsPage.jsx` (a `Switch`, matches the existing per-group-card layout); one-line addition to `SmsGatewayPage.jsx`'s hint paragraph naming `HOMEWORK` as an automatic key. API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + school app (eslint clean — 2 pre-existing unrelated hook-dependency warnings only — vite build clean) verified. **NOT YET DEPLOYED — user chose to wait for the migration first** (asked via AskUserQuestion rather than deploying and risking `GET/PUT /school/settings` + every Daily Homework save 500ing on "Invalid column name 'homework_sms_enabled'" in the gap): `Database/school_settings_homework_sms_migration.sql` must be run on `ascent_master` (NOT per tenant — `school_settings` is a master-DB table, one row per branch) BEFORE the next `deploy-api.ps1`. **SQL (user runs — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]]):** `Database/school_settings_homework_sms_migration.sql` on `ascent_master`. Files: `Database/master_tables.sql`, `Database/school_settings_homework_sms_migration.sql` (new), `API/AscentSchools.Core/DTOs/School/Settings/SchoolSettingsDtos.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/SchoolSettingsRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/SmsRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Helpers/HomeworkSmsNotifier.cs` (new), `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/School/HomeworkController.cs`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/settings/SchoolSettingsPage.jsx`, `ascent-school-app/src/pages/settings/SmsGatewayPage.jsx`.
Phase 142 ✅  Homework SMS — sfsgwk's rich per-subject DLT format (built on Phase 141) — the user configured a template keyed `HOMEWORKNEW` with a real DLT-registered pattern: a fixed 9-subject-slot layout (`Dear Parent, {#var#}` header, then `Eng: {#var#}{#var#}{#var#}` / `Tel: ...` / `Hin:` / `Mat:` / `Sci:` / `Soc:` / `EVS:` / `Bio:` / `IIT:`, each subject getting 3×30-char variable slots, ending `Principal-SFS`) — structurally nothing like the simple single-token `HOMEWORK` template Phase 141 built, so it needed real code rather than a configurable string. **Two rounds of clarification resolved the ambiguous parts before writing anything** (user explicitly asked for a plan + doubts first): header composition ("remove 'Today', drop the word 'Class', use ordinal class short-forms — Nursery→Nur/LKG/UKG/1st..10th — to fit ≤30 chars"), chunking rule (**blind 30-char character slice, not word-boundary wrapping** — confirmed, simpler and matches what was asked), and the school-gating mechanism (**not a hardcoded subdomain check** — `TenantContext` has no subdomain field and adding one just for this felt like the wrong lever; instead gates on **whether the school has an active `HOMEWORKNEW` template with a DLT id configured** — functionally restricts it to sfsgwk today since only they will ever create that key, but doesn't require touching auth/JWT, and lets another school opt in later just by adding the same template key). `HOMEWORKNEW` takes priority over the simple `HOMEWORK` key when both are configured; the master **on/off toggle from Phase 141 (`homework_sms_enabled`) still gates both paths equally** — it decides IF an SMS goes out at all, the template-key check decides WHICH shape it takes. **`HomeworkSmsNotifier.NotifyClass` signature changed** (Phase 141→142): now takes the raw `DateTime assignedDate` (was a pre-formatted `yyyy-MM-dd` string — the rich path needs a *different* format, `d-MMM` e.g. "26-Sep", so formatting moved inside the notifier, per-path) and the original `List<BatchHomeworkItem>` from the save request (subject_id+description pairs — **read directly from the already-in-memory request, no new DB query for homework content**; Daily Homework applies the same `Items` uniformly to every section in one save, so the subject-text map is built exactly once regardless of how many sections are targets). New `SmsRepository.GetSubjectShortNames(dbName, subjectIds)` — the one genuinely new query, resolving each saved item's `subject_id` to its `short_name` so it can be matched (case-insensitive) against the fixed slot list. **`MapClassOrdinal(className)`** — robust by design rather than a literal string lookup (deliberately NOT dependent on knowing sfsgwk's exact stored `class_name` values, which weren't queried — [[user-handles-all-sql-execution]] means no live DB check): keyword-matches `nursery`/`lkg`/`ukg` case-insensitively, else regex-extracts the first run of digits and applies the standard English ordinal-suffix rule (handles "8", "8 Class", "Class 8" etc. identically), else falls back to the raw class name untouched rather than ever blanking it. **`Chunk30`** — plain `Substring(i*30, ≤30)` ×3, no word-boundary logic, rest of the text past 90 chars silently dropped (confirmed, not a bug). The composed header is blind-truncated to 30 chars the same way (worked out to ~26 for the example section name — comfortable margin, per-section length still varies). A section with zero SMS-eligible recipients (no father_mobile on any student) is skipped entirely — no empty send attempted. **A subject in the day's homework whose `short_name` isn't one of the 9 fixed slots (com/G.K/M.S/abacus/Physics) has no home in this message and is silently omitted** — confirmed acceptable (the homework itself still saves normally either way). API (MSBuild Release, exit 0) verified. **Real-world caveat flagged, not yet verified:** `SmsHelper.SendSms` URL-escapes the whole message including embedded `\n\n` line breaks before sending, which is the correct way to pass them — but whether smslogin.mobi's gateway preserves those breaks in the delivered SMS is provider behavior that needs a live test message to confirm, not something verifiable from code alone. **NOT YET DEPLOYED — same pending item as Phase 141:** `Database/school_settings_homework_sms_migration.sql` still needs to run on `ascent_master` before either homework-SMS path can go live (the API already references the `homework_sms_enabled` column). Files: `API/AscentSchools.Data/Repositories/School/SmsRepository.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Helpers/HomeworkSmsNotifier.cs`, `API/AscentSchools.API/Controllers/School/HomeworkController.cs`.
Phase 114 ✅  Android targetSdk 36 (Android 16) — Play compliance + toolchain bump + a real Gradle wrapper — Play Console flagged the app: **from 31 Aug 2026 an app whose target API is not within one year of the latest Android release can no longer be updated**, and the highest non-compliant target was API 35. **`compileSdk`/`targetSdk` 35 → 36**, `versionCode` 41→**42**, `versionName` "4.1"→**"4.2"**. compileSdk 36 needs **AGP ≥ 8.9.1** (8.5.2 was in use and is only tested to compileSdk 34), so the toolchain moved with it: **AGP 8.5.2 → 8.9.1**, **Gradle 8.7 → 8.11.1** (AGP 8.9 requires 8.11.1), **Kotlin 2.0.0 → 2.0.21**. **Compose BOM deliberately left at 2024.08.00** — `ui/components/PullToRefresh.kt` uses the Material3 **1.2.x** experimental `PullToRefreshContainer`/`rememberPullToRefreshState().nestedScrollConnection` API (Phase 65 explicitly picked it because BOM 2024.08 has no `PullToRefreshBox`); that API was REMOVED in M3 1.3+, so bumping the BOM would break the build and force a rewrite of the pull-to-refresh wrapper. Kotlin stayed on the 2.0 line for the same reason (its Compose compiler matches the 1.6.x runtime the BOM pins). **Gradle wrapper ADDED** — the repo had `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties` but no `gradlew`/`gradlew.bat`/`gradle-wrapper.jar`, so every build had to go through Android Studio (many phase notes say "no gradlew wrapper in repo" — now stale). Bootstrapped with the cached Gradle 8.9 distribution (`~/.gradle/wrapper/dists/gradle-8.9-bin/*/gradle-8.9/bin/gradle wrapper --gradle-version 8.11.1`) **before** bumping AGP — running it after would have failed, since AGP 8.9.1 refuses to configure under Gradle 8.9. CLI builds now work with `JAVA_HOME="C:/Program Files/Android/Android Studio/jbr"` (JBR 21) + `./gradlew :app:bundle{Flavor}Release`. **SDK note:** only `platforms/android-36.1` was installed locally (API 36.1, no plain `android-36`); the SDK licences are accepted, so AGP auto-downloaded the missing platform + build-tools during the first build — no manual SDK Manager step was needed. **Verified:** `:app:assembleDemoDebug` BUILD SUCCESSFUL, then **signed release AABs for all 5 published flavors** (`ascent`/CHAK IN, `stannsasf`, `holyspiritjm`, `depaulemyv`, `Demo`) — `minifyWithR8` + `lintVital` pass (R8 is where a toolchain bump usually bites — cf. the Phase 23 Tink strip), all 5 `jarsigner -verify` → **jar verified**, and the merged release manifest reads `targetSdkVersion="36" versionCode="42" versionName="4.2"`. Only pre-existing deprecation warnings (AutoMirrored icons, `Divider`→`HorizontalDivider`) — cosmetic, untouched. AAB verification tip: `aapt2 dump xmltree` **cannot read an .aab** ("could not identify format of APK" — the manifest inside a bundle is protobuf, not binary XML); read `app/build/intermediates/merged_manifest/{flavor}Release/.../AndroidManifest.xml` instead, which is plain text. **16 KB page size (the other Play requirement that lands at the same time): NOT an issue** — scanned all 113 cached AARs, **zero** ship a `.so`, so there is no native code to re-align (Razorpay checkout 1.6.40, Firebase, Play app-update, Coil, OkHttp and Tink/security-crypto are all pure JVM). **Behaviour changes reviewed against the manifest:** (a) **predictive back is ON by default at targetSdk 36** and the manifest sets no `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback` — the app's Compose `BackHandler`s (tiles full-screen back, teacher chat) route through androidx.activity's `OnBackPressedDispatcher` which supports it on activity-compose 1.9.1, but back navigation is the one thing worth smoke-testing on device; the escape hatch if anything misbehaves is `android:enableOnBackInvokedCallback="false"` on `<application>`; (b) Android 16 **ignores orientation/resizability restrictions on ≥600dp displays** — no impact, the manifest sets neither `screenOrientation` nor `resizeableActivity`; (c) edge-to-edge was already enforced at API 35 and the app already calls `enableEdgeToEdge()` + `imePadding()` (Phase 92). Android-only; no API, DB, or web change. **Not yet submitted to Play** — the signed AABs are built and waiting. Files: `AscentSchoolsMobile/app/build.gradle.kts`, `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties` (+ new `gradlew`, `gradlew.bat`, `gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar`).
Phase 113 ✅  Partial fee payment in the school web app + term-order enforcement (frontend-only) — clerks can now collect part of a fee line instead of the whole outstanding, but only in strict term order. **The backend already supported this and needed no change:** outstanding is recomputed on every read as `StructureAmount − PaidAmount − ConcessionAmount` where `PaidAmount = SUM(fee_receipt_items.net_amount)` over Active receipts (`FeeRepository.GetCrossYearFeeSummary`), and `CollectFee` stores whatever `Amount`/`ConcessionAmount` the client sends with **no validation against the fee structure**. The only thing blocking partial payment was the UI hardcoding the amount to the full outstanding. The Razorpay path needed nothing either — `CreatePaymentOrder` persists the item payload and replays it through `CollectFee` on verify/webhook/reconcile. **Mobile + parent web are untouched by construction** (they build items from `outstanding` and have no editable amount field), so partial stays web-only with zero mobile/parent work — and no APK. All five collection screens (Admission/School/Transport/Hostel/Other) share `FeeCollectionBase.jsx`, so it is a **one-file change**. **Partial amounts:** new `payAmounts` state (lineKey → amount); the read-only "Pay Now" cell became an `InputNumber` (`min 0`, `max = li.outstanding`, precision 2) enabled only on selected rows, plus a new **Balance (₹)** column showing what would remain (`Clears` tag when 0, `Partial` tag otherwise). `initSelection` seeds every owed line at its full outstanding, so **the default flow is identical to before** — a clerk who changes nothing collects exactly what they used to. **Term order (user decision — Term 1 must clear before Term 2):** `uniqueTerms` (sorted by `order_no` / period sequence, stable-sorted so ties keep the server's ORDER BY) is walked in order; the first term that is not *settled* stays payable — that is where a partial payment lands — and **every term after it is locked**. A term is settled when every still-owed line in it is both selected AND paid in full; an already-cleared term has no owed lines, so `.every` on the empty set makes it settled by definition. Locked terms get a `Locked` tag + tooltip on the term checkbox and the row checkbox (both `Tooltip`-wrapped in a `<span>` — a disabled input swallows hover), and their items are excluded from `collectibleItems`, so they can never reach the payload. **Lines with no term AND no period** (`T_0`, e.g. a one-off admission fee) sit **outside** the sequence — never locking, never locked — otherwise they sort last (orderNo 999) and would be unreachable until every term was paid. Every selection/amount change routes through a single `applySelection(keys, amounts)` that recomputes the lock chain against the *candidate* state and **prunes** anything that has fallen behind (lower a Term 1 amount while Term 2 is selected → Term 2 drops out immediately) — done in the handlers, not a `useEffect`, to avoid a render loop. **Concession handling (the subtle part):** the old formula sent `Amount = outstanding + concession, ConcessionAmount = concession` on every payment, which is correct exactly once but would **re-print the same concession on every instalment**. `buildItems` now uses that convention only when the payment *clears* the line in one shot (`li.paidAmount <= 0 && payNow >= li.outstanding`); a partial or follow-up payment sends `Amount = payNow, ConcessionAmount = 0` — the cash actually received. Money is unaffected either way: `net_amount = Amount − Concession = payNow`, and outstanding reads the concession from **`fee_concessions`**, never from the receipt item — `fee_receipt_items.concession_amount` is display-only. Amounts are clamped with `Math.min(payValue, li.outstanding)` in `payNowOf` (AntD only clamps `max` on blur, so an over-typed value could otherwise reach the payload), and zero-amount lines are filtered out of the payload. **Deliberately NOT done:** cross-**year** ordering is unchanged (an older year need not be cleared before a newer one — the year is picked explicitly); no server-side amount validation was added (user decision — `/school/fees/collect` and `/mobile/fees/payment-orders` still trust the client's amounts, a pre-existing gap that would let a tampered mobile request under- or over-pay); no per-school on/off setting (user decision — always on in the web app). No DB migration, no API change, no APK. eslint clean + `npm run build:demo` clean. **Runtime-untested** (compile-verified only) — worth one manual pass on a demo student with 3 terms. Files: `ascent-school-app/src/pages/fee/FeeCollectionBase.jsx` (only).
Phase 102 ✅  CHAK IN launcher label fix — generic app showed "Ascent Schools" text under the CHAK IN icon — the on-device launcher label is `android:label="@string/app_name"` (AndroidManifest, on `<application>`), and each flavor sets `app_name` via `resValue("string","app_name",…)` in `app/build.gradle.kts` — which **overrides** the per-flavor `src/{flavor}/res/values/strings.xml` (those "Ascent Schools" entries are dead/leftover) and `src/main`. The generic **`ascent`** flavor (the published CHAK IN app, `applicationId=in.educare.app`) had its `resValue` app_name still set to **"Ascent Schools"**; the CHAK IN **icon** was updated (Phase 48) but this **label string** never was. **The Play Store *listing* name ("CHAK IN", Phase 49) does NOT change the on-device launcher label — only `app_name` does.** Fix: `ascent` flavor `resValue("string","app_name",…)` "Ascent Schools" → **"CHAK IN"** (one line). Baked per-school flavors unaffected (each has its own resValue). In-app screens unaffected (the generic app shows the *selected* school's API branding name; app_name is just the launcher label + pre-selection fallback). Takes effect on the next build/install of the `ascent` flavor. `versionCode` 34→**35**, `versionName` "3.4"→**"3.5"** (rides with the tile-dashboard build). Android-only, no API/DB change. **Rule: the launcher label is `resValue app_name` per flavor in build.gradle.kts — the Play listing name and the flavor `strings.xml` do NOT control it.**
Phase 101 ✅  Mobile tile dashboard — switchable Tiles/Tabs navigation (Android; reference-styled) — clients wanted a launcher-style tile home instead of the bottom tabs; added a **switchable** view (default stays Tabs) modeled on a client reference screenshot (colored header band + logo, red "News" ticker, 3-col icon grid on a branding-tinted background, footer). **Reuse (key point):** the navigation ENGINE is untouched — the 8 parent destinations live in one `features` list (single source of truth) consumed by BOTH the bottom `NavigationBar` and the tile grid, and the feature→screen wiring is one shared `FeatureContent(index)` `when` in `HomeScreen.kt`; only the *presentation* differs. **Toggle:** a "Tile view / Tab view" item in the existing ⋮ overflow menu (parent + teacher), persisted in new `TokenStore.tilesView` (a boolean, default false = Tabs) which `clear()` **preserves** across logout (like deviceId/school). Tiles mode has no bottom bar: tapping a tile opens that feature full-screen with a back arrow (`BackHandler` returns to the grid). **Reference layout (parent, `HomeScreen.kt`):** (1) **Logo band** — full-width band in the school's branding **primary color** carrying the logo (falls back to `getApplicationIcon()`) + name — "reuse the logo on a colored band", no photo (user decision); (2) **News ticker** — a red "News" chip + `Modifier.basicMarquee()` auto-scroll of the latest announcement titles (from the already-loaded `AnnouncementsViewModel`), tap → opens Notices; (3) **3-column `IconTile` grid** — each tile a rounded gradient square + white glyph with the label below, on a page tint **derived from the branding primary color** (user decision), with **live badges** (Fees "₹X due" red, Homework/Notices/Events counts) computed from the collected VM states — no extra network calls; (4) **Footer** "Powered by Ascent Info Solutions" pinned at the bottom. The app bar also adopts the branding color in tiles mode. **Teacher home** (`TeacherHomeScreen.kt`) got the same toggle + `IconTile` style (2×2, gated on class/section), no hero/ticker. **Branding color plumbing (Android-only — `/branding` already returns `PrimaryColor`, the mobile DTO just didn't map it):** `BrandingDto.primaryColor` added, `AuthRepository.loadBranding` stores `tokenStore.brandingPrimaryColor` (preserved across logout), `HomeScreen` calls `loadBranding()` on mount (works for baked flavors too — `/branding` resolves from the school code) and holds color/logo in state so the UI recomposes when it arrives; `parseBrandColor(hex, fallback=NavyBlue)` in `IconTile.kt` converts the hex. **Removed (superseded):** the earlier 2-col image-card `FeatureTile.kt` and its `TILE_IMAGES.md` unDraw-drop-in guide — the new tiles are solid gradient squares + Material icons, not background illustrations. `basicMarquee` compiles clean on Compose BOM 2024.08 (foundation 1.6). `versionCode` 32→**34**, `versionName` "3.2"→**"3.4"** (33 was an interim unreleased build of the first card-tile version). **Android-only — no API/DB/backend change.** Compile-verified (`:app:compileDemoDebugKotlin`, fresh run, BUILD SUCCESSFUL). Not yet built into an APK / released. Notes: Fees badge reflects only the **School** fee category (what the Fees screen loads by default); if a school has no branding primary color set, band/tint fall back to the app navy.
Phase 100 ✅  Push notification school-scoping fix — a parent got a demo-school announcement on the "CHAK IN" app that was logged into a DIFFERENT school (server-only) — with all flavor apps installed and logged in as the same parent, a single announcement fanned out to EVERY app install (observed 5 notifications on one device; 1 for a single-app school). **Root cause:** `DevicePushTokenRepository` resolved parent push targets by joining `device_push_tokens` to `parent_children` **on `parent_id` only**, ignoring the token's own `school_id`/`group_id`/`db_name`. Because `parent_accounts` live in the **master DB and are shared across all schools** (one master `parent_id` per mobile number, linked to children in many schools via `parent_children`), a demo-school announcement reached every app install/token that same master parent had ever registered — including the generic CHAK IN app currently viewing another school, and every baked per-school flavor on the device. Registration was already correct (`MobileNotificationsController` stores the currently-selected school's `GroupId/SchoolId/DbName` on the token via `UpsertParentToken`, refreshed after child-selection on login/cold-start/child-switch, deleted on logout/change-school); only the **resolution queries** didn't use it. **Fix:** scoped all three parent-token resolvers to the target school — `GetSchoolParentTokens` and `GetParentTokensForStudents` (gained a `schoolId` param) now add `AND dpt.db_name=@dbName AND dpt.group_id=@groupId AND dpt.school_id=@schoolId` to the token join, and `GetTokensForParent` (teacher→parent message reply — same latent leak) gained `dbName`/`schoolId` params + the same filter (`PushNotifier.NotifyClass`/`NotifyParent` + `MobileTeacherMessagesController` pass them). Now a device only receives pushes for the school its app is currently logged into. **Inherent (not a bug):** one CHAK IN install = one FCM token = one *current* school, so a parent with children in two schools using a single generic-app install only gets the currently-selected school's pushes until they switch children. **Diagnostic that confirmed it was correct behavior:** tapping the notification opened the *baked demo app* (each flavor's `FirebaseMessagingService` posts its own notification and Android routes the tap to the posting app), proving the push went to demo's own app, not CHAK IN. **Server-only** — built (MSBuild Release, exit 0) + deployed (full `deploy-api.ps1`, 35 files); `GET /version` → fresh `buildDate 2026-07-25 08:44 UTC`. No APK/DB change; existing token rows self-correct on the next registration (login/cold-start). Files: `Repositories/Mobile/DevicePushTokenRepository.cs`, `Helpers/PushNotifier.cs`, `Controllers/Mobile/MobileTeacherMessagesController.cs`.
Phase 95 ✅  Deployed Phases 90–94 to production + reusable Play Store release guide — **API** (full `deploy-api.ps1`), **all 5 school web apps** (`deploy-school.ps1 -School all`), and the **4 per-school privacy-policy pages** are live. Verified: `GET /version` returns a fresh build; new endpoints (`school/class-teachers`, `school/message-reports`, `mobile/messages`, `mobile/teacher/messages`) return **401** (registered, not 404); each privacy page serves the new "Messaging and Reported Content" section + FCM-token disclosure. **Deploy-time fix (would have silently killed prod push):** the API publish failed on a stale `<Content Include="ascent-schools-firebase-adminsdk-fbsvc-….json" />` in `AscentSchools.API.csproj` — the raw Firebase download name at the PROJECT ROOT, which no longer existed (the file had been moved to `App_Data/firebase-service-account.json` but the csproj reference wasn't updated, AND the real file wasn't referenced as Content at all → even a good publish wouldn't have deployed the secret → `FcmPushService.IsConfigured=false` in prod → push silently no-ops). Fixed the reference to `App_Data\firebase-service-account.json`; publish now bundles the secret (IIS blocks HTTP access to App_Data). Combined with the FCM IAM role grant (decision #75), production push is functional. **New deploy script:** `deploy/deploy-privacy.ps1` — uploads the static per-school `{folder}/privacy-policy.html` to `/httpdocs/{folder}/` (docroot derived from `api.remotePath` minus `/api`); reuses `_common.ps1` FTP helpers; folders `chakin`/`stannsasf`/`holyspiritjm`/`depaul`; `-IncludeAccountDeletion` to also push those. **The per-school privacy pages ARE the live ones** (confirmed by user), served at `https://edu-care.in/{chakin|stannsasf|holyspiritjm|depaul}/privacy-policy.html` — NOT the root `privacy-policy.html`. **STILL PENDING — mobile:** the Android APK (messaging + due-date removal + push, `versionCode` 29) has NOT been built/submitted to Play. **Play Store release guide (REUSE for every future Play release — new schools, next CHAK IN version):** an interactive step-by-step artifact — build/sign AABs per flavor, Data safety (FCM token → "Device or other IDs"; Messages → "Other in-app messages"; both **Collected, not Shared**, App functionality, linked-to-user), the full Content-rating questionnaire **question→answer key** (educational=Yes, users-interact=Yes, UGC-primary=No, nudity/violence=No, block/report/chat-moderation=Yes, invited-friends-only=Yes), App access reviewer login (**parent 9999999999 / OTP 123456**, CHAK IN school code **1000**; the bypass lives in `MobileAuthController` `ReviewMobile`/`ReviewOtp`), UGC moderation attestation, submit + staged rollout. **Guide URL:** https://claude.ai/code/artifact/6769f494-9ad8-42a3-be28-6c12066454a2 . **Reviewer-test setup reminder:** for messaging to be testable, the demo school needs a student with `father_mobile=9999999999` AND a class teacher assigned to that child's class (else the Messages tab shows "Messaging isn't set up"). Only the mobile app is on Play — the school web app needs no reviewer account.
Phase 91b ✅  Phase 91 push never actually built — csproj registration gap (found 2026-07-17) — the whole API solution failed to compile: `PushNotifier`/`FcmPushService`/`MobileNotificationsController` existed on disk with correct namespaces but were **never added to `AscentSchools.API.csproj`**. The API projects are **old-style (non-SDK) csproj**, so a new `.cs` file is invisible to the build unless listed as `<Compile Include>`. Phase 91's Core DTO (`PushDtos.cs`) and Data repo (`DevicePushTokenRepository.cs`) *were* registered, which is exactly why it looked finished and was recorded as "compile-verified". Had the csproj not been fixed, push would have deployed as dead code (and nothing else would have built either). Fixed by adding the three `<Compile Include>` lines. **Rule: after creating ANY new API .cs file, add it to the csproj and build `API/AscentSchools.sln` (NOT the repo root — there is no solution there) with MSBuild Release, and confirm exit code 0.**
Phase 86 ✅  Self-service Change Password (school app) — before this, only an admin (`USER_MGMT.MANAGE`) could reset a user's password via User Management; regular staff had no way to change their own. Added a self-service flow for **any authenticated school user**. **Backend:** `POST /school/auth/change-password` (`SchoolAuthController`, plain `ApiController` — action tagged `[SchoolAuth]` so `TenantContext.Current` is populated/non-null) → verifies `CurrentPassword` against the stored hash (`SchoolAuthRepository.GetById().PasswordHash` vs `JwtHelper.HashRefreshToken(current)`), rejects with 400 if wrong, enforces min 6 chars, then `SchoolAuthRepository.UpdatePassword(userId, newHash)` sets `password_hash` **and revokes all active refresh tokens** (forces re-login everywhere). `ChangePasswordRequest` DTO added to `Core/DTOs/Auth/LoginRequest.cs`. **Frontend:** `AppLayout` header user dropdown gains a **"Change Password"** item (above Logout) → a Modal (current / new / confirm, with match validation) → `POST /school/auth/change-password` → on success shows a message and **logs the user out** (`logout()` + navigate to `/login`) since the tokens were revoked. API + all 5 school apps deployed. (Note: like the admin reset-password endpoint, this is protected by `[SchoolAuth]` = any authenticated user — which is correct here since it only changes the caller's own password from `TenantContext.UserId`.)
Phase 85 ✅  Cloudflare R2 storage — Phase B (3 upload buttons on homework/announcements/events + mobile) — replaces the manual youtube/image/attachment URL text fields with **up to 3 R2 file uploads** per item; parents see them in the app. **DB:** new tenant table `media_uploads` (generic: entity_type [homework|announcement|event], entity_id, file_name, file_url [R2 public URL], file_type [image|doc|audio|video], file_size_kb, school_id) — `tenant_tables.sql` (table 48) + `Database/media_uploads_migration.sql` (also **relaxes `school_events.media_url` to NULL** — it now holds only the optional YouTube URL; R2 uploads live in `media_uploads`). **Backend:** `MediaUploadsRepository` (Insert/GetByEntity/GetForEntities/Delete/CountByEntity/GetCurrentAcademicYear); `UploadsController.Presign` extended for `homework` (key `homeworks/{academicYear}/{id}/…` — year folders → delete old years to save space), `announcement` (`announcements/{id}/…`), `event` (`events/{id}/…`), with a longer presign window (1 h) for `video/*` (1 GB); new `POST /school/media/attach`, `GET /school/media?entityType=&entityId=`, `DELETE /school/media/{id}`; `SchoolEventsController` no longer requires `MediaUrl` (optional YouTube); mobile `HomeworkDto`/`AnnouncementDto`/`SchoolEventDto` gain a `Media` list, populated in `MobileDataRepository` (`LoadMedia` per entity). **School app:** reusable `components/MediaUploader.jsx` (3 upload buttons → presign → direct R2 PUT → `media/attach`; lists with delete; size/type caps enforced client-side — image 1 MB, PDF 2 MB, audio 10 MB, video 1 GB); wired into `HomeworkPage`/`AnnouncementsPage`/`EventsPage` (manual URL fields removed; **events keep an optional YouTube URL field** + image/video uploads; the uploader shows in **edit mode**, and after **Create** the modal now **stays open in edit mode** carrying the new id from the `Created(id,…)` response — so you attach files immediately without reopening). **Mobile (needs new APK):** `ApiModels.kt` `MediaUploadDto` + `media` on the 3 DTOs; new `ui/components/MediaAttachments.kt` (images inline via Coil, others as tappable buttons → `ACTION_VIEW`) wired into Homework/Announcements/Events screens; `versionCode` 20→**21**, `versionName` "1.9"→**"2.0"**. **Go-live per school:** run `media_uploads_migration.sql` (before using the new UI — events save with no YouTube URL needs the nullable media_url). API + all 5 school apps deployed; mobile pending APK build.
Phase 84 ✅  Cloudflare R2 storage — Phase A (config page + presigned uploads + student photo→R2) — moving file storage to per-school Cloudflare R2 (S3-compatible). **Approach:** browser uploads **directly to R2 via SigV4 presigned PUT URLs** (no file bytes through our API/IIS — needed for the 1 GB event videos coming in Phase B); our API only signs the URL and records the resulting public URL. **DB:** new tenant table `r2_configs` (per school, `UNIQUE(school_id)`: account_id, access_key_id, secret_access_key [never returned], bucket_name, public_base_url, is_enabled) — in `tenant_tables.sql` (table 47) + idempotent `Database/r2_config_migration.sql`. **Backend:** `R2ConfigRepository` (Get [safe, `hasSecretKey` flag] / GetInternal [with secret] / Upsert [blank secret keeps existing]); `R2Service` (manual SigV4 presign — `PresignPut` host-only signed header, UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD, region `auto`; `PublicUrl`; **no AWS SDK dependency**); `UploadsController` `GET/PUT /school/settings/r2` + `POST /school/uploads/presign` (Phase A handles `purpose=student-photo` → key `student-images/{AdmissionNo}_{academicYear}.{ext}`; homework/announcement/event added in Phase B); `StudentsController` gains `PUT /school/students/{id}/photo` (JSON `{photoUrl}`) to save the R2 URL after upload. New files registered in the old-style `.csproj`s. **Frontend:** `pages/settings/R2StoragePage.jsx` (Settings → "R2 Storage", `SETTINGS.MANAGE`); `api/r2Upload.js` helper (presign → raw `fetch` PUT to R2 → return public URL; size/type caps: 1 MB images, 2 MB docs, 1 GB video, jpg/png/webp/pdf/mp3/mp4); `StudentFormPage` photo now uploads to R2 (validates ≤1 MB image) and saves via the new PUT; student `photo_path` is now a full R2 URL, so `Avatar` rendering handles absolute vs legacy-relative (`/^https?:\/\//` check) in `StudentFormPage` + `StudentsPage`. **Go-live per school:** run `r2_config_migration.sql`; set up R2 (public access via r2.dev/custom domain, an Object-Read&Write API token, and a CORS rule allowing PUT from the school site); enter creds in the R2 Storage page. Until configured, presign returns 400 and the student still saves (photo skipped with a warning). API + all 5 school apps deployed. **Phase B (next):** 3 upload buttons on homework/announcements/events → `media_uploads` table, remove the manual youtube/image/attachment URL fields (keep YouTube field on events), mobile app renders the attachment list (new APK).
Phase 83 ✅  API version/deploy-date in the school-app footer — the footer already showed UI version + build date (Phase 66); added the **live API build info** next to it so you can tell which API is deployed. **API:** new public `GET /version` (`PublicController`, AllowAnonymous) → `{ version, buildDate }` — `version` = executing assembly version, `buildDate` = last-write time of `~/bin/AscentSchools.API.dll` via `HostingEnvironment.MapPath` (the actual FTP-deploy time; more accurate than the shadow-copied `asm.Location`), formatted `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm`, best-effort (null on error). **Frontend:** `AppLayout.jsx` fetches `/version` on mount (`useEffect`) and the footer now reads `Powered by … · UI v{__APP_VERSION__} ({__BUILD_DATE__}) · API v{version} ({buildDate})`. API (full `deploy-api.ps1`) + all 5 school apps deployed. **API version is now a real bumpable number:** `AssemblyVersion`/`AssemblyFileVersion` in `API/AscentSchools.API/Properties/AssemblyInfo.cs` set to **`1.1.0.0`** (was default `1.0.0.0`) — `GET /version` reads `AssemblyVersion` via `asm.GetName().Version`. **Bump both lines per API release.** Verified live: `/version` → `{"version":"1.1.0.0","buildDate":"2026-07-06 01:45"}`. (`__APP_VERSION__` UI version is still `1.0.0` from `vite.config` — bump per release; `__BUILD_DATE__` auto-updates each build.) **UTC fix:** `buildDate` was read with `File.GetLastWriteTime` (server LOCAL tz — the box is US Eastern ~UTC-4, so a 07:00 UTC deploy displayed as 03:00) → switched to `GetLastWriteTimeUtc(...) + " UTC"`. The UI `__BUILD_DATE__` (`new Date().toISOString()`) was already UTC — just added a `" UTC"` label so both footer times read unambiguously and match.
Phase 82 ✅  OTP request timeout fix (mobile + API) — Android login sometimes showed "timeout" after entering the mobile number because `/mobile/auth/parent/request-otp` **synchronously calls the smslogin.mobi gateway**, which is sometimes slow; the app's 30s `readTimeout` fired before the server responded (OTP often still sent, late). Coordinated fix so **app timeout > API gateway timeout**: **Android** (`RetrofitClient.kt`, both `okHttpClient` + `refreshClient`) `readTimeout` 30→**60s**, added `writeTimeout` 30s + `callTimeout` 75s (main client); **API** (`SmsHelper.SendSms`) replaced the plain `WebClient` (which hangs up to the 100s default `HttpWebRequest` timeout) with a `TimedWebClient : WebClient` overriding `GetWebRequest` to set `request.Timeout = 45000` (45s) — so the server returns a real result/error within the app's 60s window instead of the app timing out on its own. Applies to both OTP (global account) and SMS Center (per-school account) since both go through `SendSms`. **API deployed** (full `deploy-api.ps1`). **Android change needs an APK rebuild** (Android Studio; no gradlew in repo) + redistribution — compile-safe timeout bump only, no logic change. `app/build.gradle.kts` `versionCode` 19→**20**, `versionName` "1.8"→**"1.9"**.
Phase 81 ✅  Dashboard "Recent Receipts" ordered by payment date — the dashboard's recent-5 receipts (`DashboardRepository`, `SELECT TOP 5 … FROM fee_receipts`) ordered by `created_at DESC` (row-insert time), so back-dated/legacy-imported receipts sorted by when they were entered, not when paid. Changed to `ORDER BY fr.payment_date DESC, fr.created_at DESC` (payment date first, insert time as same-day tie-breaker). API-only (`DashboardPage.jsx` renders in backend order, no client-side sorter). **Deploy gotcha (learned):** `deploy-api.ps1 -SkipBuild` skips the FolderProfile **publish** step and re-uploads whatever is already in `bin/Release/Publish` — a manual `msbuild /t:Build` writes to `bin/Release/`, NOT the publish folder, so `-SkipBuild` uploaded a STALE publish (change didn't reach the server). **Rule: after ANY API source change, run the full `deploy-api.ps1` (no `-SkipBuild`)** — it publishes fresh then uploads. `-SkipBuild` is only for re-uploading an already-published build unchanged. Redeployed full; live. **Follow-up:** the recent-receipts table also gained a **Type** column (payment mode — Cash/Cheque/Online): `DashboardRepository` `LEFT JOIN payment_modes` → `RecentReceiptDto.PaymentMode`; `DashboardPage.jsx` renders it as a Tag between Date and Status. Deployed.
Phase 80 ✅  Mobile long-lived sessions (365-day refresh) — cut SMS re-OTP cost — parents were logged out after 7 days of not opening the app (both the DB refresh token AND the `parentRefreshToken` cookie were 7-day), forcing an SMS OTP re-login (a real cost to the school). Extended the **mobile** refresh window to **365 days** (sliding — every use rolls it forward), while **leaving web apps at 7 days** (control/school use passwords, no SMS cost; long web sessions are a security downgrade). `JwtHelper` gains `MobileRefreshDays` (reads `Jwt:MobileRefreshTokenDays`, **defaults to 365 when the key is absent** — so it works even if Web.config isn't re-uploaded) + `MobileRefreshTokenExpiry()`. Applied ONLY in the mobile controllers: `MobileAuthController` (parent + student — its shared `SetCookie` cookie `Expires` + the 4 `CreateRefreshToken` expiry calls) and `MobileTeacherAuthController` (`SetCookie` + 2 calls). `ControlAuthController`/`SchoolAuthController` untouched (still `RefreshTokenExpiry()`=7d + `AddDays(7)` cookie). `Web.config` adds `Jwt:MobileRefreshTokenDays=365`. **No APK change** — this is server-side; combined with the existing PersistentCookieJar (Phase 34/70) + cold-start silent refresh (Phase 34/71) + Path=/ cookie (Phase 56), a parent on a current APK who reopens the app stays logged in without OTP unless they go a full year without opening it, reinstall, or switch phones. **One-time transition:** existing users keep their old 7-day cookie until their next login; after that single OTP they get the 365-day cookie. API deployed (mobile sessions live at 365d via the code default). Security note: longer refresh window widens the lost-phone exposure, mitigated by device binding (one device/parent), token rotation on each refresh, HttpOnly + SHA-256-hashed-in-DB tokens, and server-side revocability.
Phase 79 ✅  Students audit trail via SQL Server temporal (system-versioned) table — the `audit_logs` tenant table is event-level only (no old/new values) and unused; instead of instrumenting the ~10 student write paths, made `students` a **system-versioned temporal table** so EVERY change (web form, bulk import, sync tool, promote, block/detain, change-section, transport/hostel update) is snapshotted automatically into `dbo.students_history` — **zero application code**. Added hidden `valid_from`/`valid_to` `DATETIME2 GENERATED ALWAYS AS ROW START/END` PERIOD columns + `SYSTEM_VERSIONING = ON (HISTORY_TABLE = dbo.students_history)`. The "who/when" comes from `students.updated_by`/`updated_at` (Phase 78) carried on every history row, plus the system period. **Files:** `tenant_tables.sql` (two `ALTER TABLE students` after the table def — for new groups) + idempotent `Database/students_temporal_migration.sql` (guarded by `sys.periods` / `sys.tables.temporal_type=2`; for existing tenant DBs). **Requires SQL Server 2016+** (any edition). Query history: `SELECT * FROM students FOR SYSTEM_TIME ALL WHERE student_id=@id ORDER BY valid_from`. Period cols are HIDDEN so `SELECT *`/existing queries and the explicit-column INSERT/UPDATE paths are unaffected (GENERATED ALWAYS cols are auto-populated, never in the insert list). Caveats: future `ALTER TABLE students` MUST wrap with `SYSTEM_VERSIONING = OFF` … `ON` (see decision #74); DROP requires it off. **Viewer:** `GET /school/students/{id}/history` (`StudentsController.GetStudentHistory` → `StudentRepository.GetHistory` querying `students FOR SYSTEM_TIME ALL AS s`, newest-first, joined to classes/sections/academic_years for display names) → `StudentHistoryDto` (valid_from/valid_to + created_by/updated_by/updated_at + key snapshot fields); `StudentFormPage.jsx` edit view gained a **"View change history"** button → a Drawer with a table (one row per saved version, newest first, UTC times). **Migrations run + API and all 5 school apps deployed** (Phases 76–79 shipped together in this deploy).
