Phase 78 ✅  Student create/update audit — `created_by` on web create + new `updated_by`/`updated_at` — the web "Register Student" form (`StudentRepository.Create`) never wrote `created_by` (only the Promote path did; bulk got it in Phase 77), and there was no update-audit trail at all. **created_by:** `Create(...)` gains a `createdBy` param + `created_by` in the INSERT; `StudentsController.CreateStudent` passes `Tenant.FullName`. **updated_by/updated_at:** NEW `students` columns `updated_by VARCHAR(25) NULL` + `updated_at DATETIME NULL` (added to `tenant_tables.sql` after `created_at`; idempotent migration `Database/students_updated_audit_migration.sql` for existing tenant DBs); `Update(...)` gains an `updatedBy` param and sets `updated_by = @updatedBy, updated_at = GETDATE()`; `StudentsController.UpdateStudent` passes `Tenant.FullName`. **UI:** `GetById` SELECT + `StudentDto` now return `CreatedBy/CreatedAt/UpdatedBy/UpdatedAt`; `StudentFormPage.jsx` shows a read-only audit footer on edit ("Created by X on … · Last updated by Y on …", `dayjs`-formatted). Both bulk-create paths keep the Phase-77 behavior (bulk sets `created_by`; upsert never touches it). API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) compile-verified. **Deployed** (`students_updated_audit_migration.sql` run per tenant DB; API + all 5 school apps live).
Phase 77 ✅  Sync tool — extended student fields + created_by='synctool' — the sync tool's Export Students carried only ~20 columns; extended it to cover 25 more legacy→new fields (mappings taken from `AscentMigration.StudentsMigrator`, the authoritative source) plus `created_by`. **New fields** (legacy `SAS_StudentMaster` col → `students` col): `StuJoinTyp`→join_type, `StuFatherOccup`→father_occupation, `StuFatherEmpTyp`→father_employment_type, `StuMotherOccup`→mother_occupation, `StuDOJ`→date_of_joining, `StuNationlity`→nationality, `StuDoorNo`→door_no, `StuAddrArea`→address_area, `StuAddrCity`→address_city, `StuAddrState`→address_state, `StuAddrPerminent`→permanent_address, `StuMailID`→email, `StuAnnualIncome`→annual_income, `StuFamilyChildres`(varchar→int)→family_children_count, `StuDOBStat`→dob_proof_submitted, `StuCasteStat`→caste_cert_submitted, `StuTransportTyp`→transport_type, `AdminDate`→admission_date, `StudentType`→student_type, `BloodGrp`→blood_group, `JoinTerm`→join_term, `StuFirstLang`→first_language, `StuThirdLang`→third_language, `StuUdiseNo`→udise_no (mother_tongue`←MothrLang` was already mapped). **fee_category_id:** resolved server-side to the school's **'General'** category (`SELECT TOP 1 fee_category_id FROM fee_categories WHERE category_name='General'`), **NULL if none** — applied only for sync inserts (gated on `createdBy=='synctool'`), so web CSV import behaviour is unchanged. **created_by:** `BulkStudentImportRequest.CreatedBy` (new) — sync tool sends `"synctool"`; `StudentsController.BulkImport` computes `createdBy = request.CreatedBy ?? Tenant.FullName` and passes it to `StudentRepository.BulkCreate(..., string createdBy)`; INSERT now writes `created_by` (previously never set on the bulk/single-create paths — only Promote did). **Upsert:** per user decision, the UPDATE path now refreshes **all** these fields including enrollment-specific (`fee_category_id`, `join_type`, `date_of_joining`, `admission_date`, `transport_type`, `student_type`, `join_term`) — but **never** `created_by`, and still leaves class/section/roll/status/academic_year untouched. Dates `date_of_joining`/`admission_date` parsed leniently (blank/bad → NULL, row not failed) via a new `TryParseDate` helper. **Files:** AscentSyncTool `LegacyDb.GetStudents` (SELECT), `LegacyStudent` model, `StudentBulkRow`+`BulkStudentImportRequest`(`CreatedBy="synctool"`) in `ApiModels.cs`, `Mappers.ToBulkStudent`; API Core `StudentBulkRow`+`BulkStudentImportRequest.CreatedBy`, `StudentsController.BulkImport`, `StudentRepository.BulkCreate`. All 27 target columns verified present in `tenant_tables.sql` students table. No DB schema change. API (MSBuild Release) + AscentSyncTool (MSBuild) compile-verified. **API deployed**; redistribute the rebuilt `AscentSyncTool.exe` for the extended fields to flow on the next sync.
Phase 76 ✅  Sync tool — export legacy StuUnqID + match students by student_unique_id (fixes promotion duplicates) — legacy VB6 changes a student's `admission_no` on promotion (UKG→1st etc.) while `StuUnqID` stays stable; the sync's upsert matched existing students by `admission_no + academic_year_id`, so a changed admission_no wasn't found → INSERT → **duplicate**. Fix: carry `StuUnqID → students.student_unique_id` and match on the **stable** `student_unique_id + academic_year_id`, updating `admission_no` on match. **AscentSyncTool:** `LegacyDb.GetStudents` SELECT adds `StuUnqID`; `LegacyStudent` gains `double? StuUnqID` (legacy float); `StudentBulkRow` (`Models/ApiModels.cs`) gains `int? StudentUniqueId`; `Mappers.ToBulkStudent` maps `StuUnqID`(float)→`StudentUniqueId`(int). Also moved the **Export Students** tab to first place in `MainForm.cs`. **API:** Core `StudentBulkRow` (`BulkImportDtos.cs`) gains `int? StudentUniqueId`; `StudentRepository.BulkCreate` upsert path now: if `StudentUniqueId` is supplied → look up existing by `student_unique_id + academic_year_id + school_id`, else fall back to `admission_no + academic_year_id` (so the **web CSV import**, which sends no unique id + `Upsert=false`, is unaffected); the UPDATE now also sets `admission_no = @admissionNo` (writes the promoted/changed number) alongside the personal/contact fields (class/section/roll/status/fee_category/year still untouched); the INSERT uses the supplied `StudentUniqueId` when present (`ISNULL(@providedUniqueId, <reuse-by-admission_no else MAX+1>)`), else the prior generation logic. No DB schema change; no new endpoint. **Migrated-via-AscentMigration schools already align** (StudentsMigrator stored `student_unique_id = StuUnqID`); only students created by the **sync tool before this change** have a web-generated unique_id ≠ StuUnqID. For those, one-time `Database/align_student_unique_id.sql` (cross-DB, tenant + legacy on the same instance; preview-first, transaction-wrapped, ROLLBACK-by-default; matches `admission_no + academic_year` → sets `student_unique_id = StuUnqID`; includes a same-year duplicate report) — run per tenant DB before relying on the new matching, then redeploy the API. Both API (MSBuild Release) + AscentSyncTool (MSBuild) compile-verified. **API deployed**; redistribute the rebuilt `AscentSyncTool.exe`.
Phase 75 ✅  School users created from staff records (`users.employee_id → staff.staff_id`) — school-app **User Management** now creates a login only from an existing **staff** member (except the admin, which is still created via the Control app and has `employee_id = NULL`). Leverages the pre-existing unused `users.employee_id INT NULL` column — **no structural DB change**, only constraints. **Decisions (user):** staff selection **mandatory**; **one login per staff** (enforced); username **auto-suggested + editable**; **email & mobile read-only** (locked to the staff record); full name prefilled + editable. **Backend:** `CreateTenantUserRequest.EmployeeId` (int?) + `TenantUserRepository.Create` writes `employee_id`; `TenantUserDto` gains `EmployeeId`/`StaffName`/`Designation` and `GetAll` `LEFT JOIN staff` for the grid; new `TenantUserRepository.GetAssignableStaff(schoolId)` (Active staff of the branch whose `staff_id NOT IN (SELECT employee_id FROM users WHERE employee_id IS NOT NULL)` — returns `StaffDto`, reused, no new file) + `IsStaffAvailableForLogin(schoolId, staffId)`; new endpoint `GET /school/users/staff`; `SchoolUsersController.CreateUser` now requires `EmployeeId` and validates the staff is Active + unlinked (400 otherwise) before the existing username-taken check. The **control-app admin path** (`SchoolGroupsController`, same `CreateTenantUserRequest`) simply omits `EmployeeId` → NULL, so it's unaffected (the school-app "required" check lives only in `SchoolUsersController`). **Frontend (`ascent-school-app/src/pages/rbac/UsersPage.jsx`):** loads assignable staff on mount; Add-User modal gains a mandatory searchable **Staff Member** select → `onStaffSelect` auto-fills Full Name/Email/Mobile + suggests a username (`employeeCode` else normalized name via `suggestUsername`); **Email & Mobile inputs are `readOnly`**; grid gains a **Staff · Designation** column (admin row → "—"); **Add User** is disabled with an info alert when no assignable staff remain (all linked or none exist); after create, `fetchAll()` refreshes so the just-linked staff drops off the dropdown. **DB:** new idempotent `Database/users_staff_link_migration.sql` adds `FK_users_staff (employee_id → staff.staff_id)` + filtered `UNIQUE INDEX UQ_users_employee ON users(employee_id) WHERE employee_id IS NOT NULL` (one login per staff; multiple NULLs allowed for admin); `tenant_tables.sql` adds the same **after** the `staff` table (staff is table 35, created after `users` table 4 — the FK can't be inline on `users`). Both API (MSBuild Release) + school app (vite) compile-verified. **Deployed** (API + all 5 school apps). **Pending go-live step:** run `users_staff_link_migration.sql` on each tenant DB for the DB-level FK + one-login-per-staff enforcement — the app already works without it (column/join pre-exist; the dropdown hides already-linked staff), so the migration only adds the hard DB guarantee.
Phase 74 ✅  Delete attendance for a date (school app) + Half Day save-validation fix — (1) **Delete feature:** new **"Delete Attendance"** tab in `ascent-school-app` `pages/attendance/AttendancePage.jsx` (third tab beside Mark Attendance / Monthly Summary). Class + Date required, **Section optional** ("All Sections (whole class)") → **Load** (read-only preview of the marked statuses via the existing `GET /school/attendance` grid, with a "N marked" / "Not marked" tag + a Section column) → **Delete Attendance** button behind a `Popconfirm` (disabled when nothing is marked; the confirm text says "this section" vs "the whole class (all sections)"). Hard delete (rows removed) so the day can be re-marked cleanly. **Backend:** `DELETE /school/attendance?classId=&sectionId=&date=` (`AttendanceController.DeleteAttendance`) → `AttendanceRepository.DeleteAttendance` hard-`DELETE`s `student_attendance` rows for **current-academic-year** students of that class on that date (same year-scoping subquery the mark grid uses, so prior-year stray rows aren't touched), returns the deleted row count. **`sectionId` is optional (`int?`)** on both `DELETE` and `GET /school/attendance` — when omitted, `WHERE (@sectionId IS NULL OR section_id = @sectionId)` covers the whole class across all sections. `GetAttendanceGrid` gained a nullable `sectionId`: null → roster of all sections (ordered by section then name), `ClassName` shows just the class name, and `AttendanceStudentDto.SectionName` (new, `LEFT JOIN sections`) lets the class-level preview show each student's section. The Mark tab always passes a section, so it's unaffected (backward-compatible). No DB change. **Web API gotcha (fixed):** a `[FromUri] int? sectionId` param is still treated as REQUIRED for action selection unless it has a **default value** — without `= null`, `GET /school/attendance?classId=&date=` (no sectionId) matched no GET action → 405 `"The requested resource does not support http method 'GET'."` Fix: `[FromUri] int? sectionId = null` (and move it last, C# requires optional params after required ones) on BOTH `GetAttendance` and `DeleteAttendance`. Nullable alone is not enough — the default value is what makes it optional for the URI action selector. **Belt-and-suspenders:** the frontend also always sends `sectionId=${delSection || 0}` (0 = whole class; server maps `sectionId > 0 ? sectionId : null`), so the query param is always present regardless. Verified live by probing the prod endpoint: no-section GET and `sectionId=0` GET/DELETE all return **401** (auth reached = action selected) instead of **405**. Deploy lesson: a `deploy-api.ps1 -SkipBuild` push did NOT pick up the change (running DLL not recycled) — a full `deploy-api.ps1` (rebuild + upload) was needed before the `=null` took effect. (2) **Half Day save-validation fix (completes Phase 73):** the `validStatuses` allow-list in **both** `AttendanceController.SaveAttendance` AND `MobileTeacherController` (the two attendance save paths) was hardcoded `{Present, Absent, Late, Holiday}` and **rejected `HalfDay` with 400** — Phase 73 added Half Day to the UIs but saving it would have failed. Added `"HalfDay"` to both allow-lists (and their error messages). Deploy the API with this before relying on Half Day end-to-end. Both API (MSBuild Release) and school app (vite) compile-verified.
Phase 73 ✅  Student attendance — "Half Day" status added everywhere — extends student attendance from Present/Absent/Late to include **`HalfDay`** (stored value, no space — matches the staff-attendance convention and the parent mobile calendar which already had a `HalfDay` day-cell color). **No DB change** — `student_attendance.status` is `VARCHAR(10)` with no CHECK constraint, so `HalfDay` (7 chars) just stores; save path was already status-agnostic (`AttendanceRepository.SaveAttendance` MERGE). **Decisions (user):** value = `HalfDay`; counts as **0.5 day present** in every attendance %; half-day students do **NOT** get the Absent SMS (only `Absent` triggers it — unchanged). **Backend (.NET):** `AttendanceMonthlySummaryDto.HalfDayDays` + `AttendanceRepository.GetMonthlySummary` SUM; mobile `AttendanceSummaryDto.HalfDayDays` + `MobileDataRepository.GetAttendance` count; `DashboardDto.TodayHalfDay` + `DashboardRepository` (SUM + `AttendanceRow.TodayHalfDay`; attendance % numerator now `Present+Late+0.5×HalfDay`); `ReportDtos` (`AttendanceSheetRowDto.HalfDay`, `DailyAttendanceSummaryRowDto.HalfDay`, `MonthlyAttendanceRowDto.TotalHalfDay`, `MonthCounts.HalfDay`, `AttendanceRegisterRowDto.HalfDay`) + the 4 `ReportsRepository` attendance methods (`GetAttendanceSheet`/`GetAttendanceRegister`/`GetMonthlyAttendanceSheet`/`GetDailyAttendanceSummary`) now count `HalfDay` and map the day-cell to `"H"`. No new endpoints/migration. **School web app:** `AttendancePage.jsx` — `HalfDay` added to `STATUS_OPTIONS`/`STATUS_COLOR` (blue) with a `STATUS_LABEL` map rendering "Half Day"; mark-grid radio, header count tag (`HD:`), and Monthly Summary tab get a Half Day column + the 0.5-weighted %. `DashboardPage.jsx` — half-day in the inline P/A/L line + a Half Day progress row. All 4 attendance report pages (`AttendanceSheetReport`/`AttendanceRegisterReport`/`MonthlyAttendanceSheetReport`/`DailyAttendanceSummaryReport`) get an `H`/`HD` legend+color (blue `#1677ff`), a Half Day count column, grand-total cell, and PDF/CSV export column; their % formulas use `(present + 0.5×halfDay) / total`. **Android:** `ApiModels.kt` `AttendanceSummaryDto.halfDayDays` (default 0); parent `AttendanceScreen.kt` — Half Day `StatPill` + legend entry + % includes `0.5×halfDay` (DayCell already handled `"HalfDay"`); teacher marking — `AttendanceStatus` enum gains `HalfDay` (every exhaustive `when` updated: load-mapping `"halfday"`, quick-tap toggle → resets to Present like Late, status-color/icon in `TeacherAttendanceScreen` [HalfDay = blue, reuses Schedule icon], bottom-sheet status list, summary `HD` chip, badge label "HALF DAY"); long-press status sheet is how Late/HalfDay are set (quick tap stays Present↔Absent). `status.name` = `"HalfDay"` posts the correct value. **Build:** API compiles (MSBuild Release); school app builds (vite, demo mode). Android edits are compile-ready (all exhaustive `when`s covered; DTO field has a default so JSON parse is safe) but need an Android Studio/Gradle build + versionCode bump for the APK (no gradlew wrapper in repo). Deploy: API first (old clients ignore the new status), then school app, then mobile. Not yet deployed.
Phase 72 ✅  Serial-number columns (school app, frontend-only) + migrator TC status — (1) **S.No columns:** added a leading `S.No` column to five `ascent-school-app` grids that previously had none: Students list (`pages/students/StudentsPage.jsx`), Fee Receipts (`pages/fee/ReceiptsPage.jsx`), and three Reports — Class-wise Students (`pages/reports/ClassStudentsReport.jsx`), Absents Statement (`pages/reports/AbsentsReport.jsx`), Attendance Sheet (`pages/reports/AttendanceSheetReport.jsx`). For **paginated** grids (Students, Receipts, Absents) the numbering is continuous across pages — pagination was made controlled (`page`/`pageSize` state + `onChange`) and the cell renders `(page - 1) * pageSize + index + 1`; for the **non-paginated** report grids (Class-wise Students, Attendance Sheet — `pagination={false}`) it renders `index + 1`. Attendance Sheet's S.No is `fixed:'left'` alongside its existing fixed Adm No/Name columns. Frontend-only; the PDF/CSV exports on the report pages were **not** changed (still omit S.No). Deployed to all 5 schools via `deploy/deploy-school.ps1 -School all`. (2) **Migrator TC status:** `AscentMigration/Migrators/StudentsMigrator.cs` `MapStatus` gained `case "TC": return "TC";` (between `D→Inactive` and `R→R`) so VB6 `SAS_StudentMaster.StuStatus='TC'` migrates as the `TC` status the student form recognizes ("TC Issued"). Existing mappings unchanged (`A→Active`, `D→Inactive`, `R→R`, else→null). Note: `switch` is case-sensitive — expects source value exactly `TC` (uppercase). The student-form status dropdown (`StudentFormPage.jsx` `STATUS_OPTIONS`) remains a hardcoded 3-option list (Active/Inactive/TC); the Students-list filter additionally includes Blocked (managed via Block/Unblock actions, not the form dropdown). (3) **New migrator — exam_types** (`SAS_ExamNam` → `exam_types`): `AscentMigration/Migrators/ExamTypesMigrator.cs` + `Models/Legacy/LegacyExamType.cs` (POCO: `ExamNam`/`acdyear`/`examremarks`/`examstatus`). Source `SELECT DISTINCT ExamNam, acdyear, examremarks, examstatus FROM SAS_ExamNam`. Mapping: `ExamNam`→`exam_type_name` (trimmed; row skipped if blank), `acdyear`→`academic_year_id` (lookup `academic_years.academic_year=acdyear` school-scoped, **fallback** to `ResolveDefaultAcademicYearIdAsync` on miss — same pattern as Terms/Subjects), `examremarks`(varchar)→`display_order` via `int.TryParse` (NULL when unparseable; column is `INT NULL`), `examstatus` A→Active/D→Inactive/else→Active (column is `VARCHAR(10)`, not int despite the brief's "status int"); `school_id`=`Config.SchoolId`, `created_by`="migration", `created_at`=`DateTime.Now`. **Dedup is per-year** — composite skip key `exam_type_name|academic_year_id` (so the same exam name across two years → two rows, matching how the app filters exam types by year); a second source row with the same name+year but different remarks/status is counted as Skipped (first wins), guarded both against existing dest rows (Skip mode) and within the same batch. Registered in `MigrationRunner.BuildMigrators` after `SubjectsMigrator` (only dep is academic_years). Added to `MigrationRunner.CleanupAsync` (after fee_receipts — nothing in the migrated set references it; `student_marks` does but isn't migrated) and to `Database/cleanup_migration_data.sql`. Truncate/Skip modes + `exam_types.force` re-run supported like the rest. Compile-verified. (4) **New table + migrator — grade_types** (`SAS_MarksGrade` → `grade_types`): NEW tenant table `grade_types` (table 45) — `id` IDENTITY PK, `grade_name` VARCHAR(200), `subject_id` INT NULL (FK → subjects), `max_marks`/`min_marks` FLOAT, `grade` VARCHAR(100), `remarks` VARCHAR(200), `status` VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'Active', `created_date` DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE() (note: `created_date`, not `created_at`), `created_by` VARCHAR(200), `school_id` INT. Added to `Database/tenant_tables.sql` (new groups) + new idempotent `Database/grade_types_migration.sql` (`IF NOT EXISTS` guard — run per existing tenant DB). Migrator `AscentMigration/Migrators/GradeTypesMigrator.cs` + `Models/Legacy/LegacyMarksGrade.cs`. Mapping: `GradName`→`grade_name`, `MaxMrks`→`max_marks`, `MinMrks`→`min_marks`, `Grad`→`grade`, `Rrmrks`→`remarks`, `TraStatus` A→Active/D→Inactive/else→Active; `created_date`=`DateTime.Now`, `created_by`="migration", `school_id`=`Config.SchoolId`. **`SubjID`→`subject_id` is a two-step lookup** (like FeeStructures' category): step 1 legacy `SAS_MarksGrade.SubjID` → `SAS_Subjects.SubjectNam` (source map `SubJectID`→`SubjectNam`); step 2 `subject_name` → dest `subjects.subject_id`. Since dest `subjects` are per-academic-year (same name repeats), the dest map is ordered `academic_year_id DESC` so a name collision maps to the **latest year's** subject_id (warned); a miss at either step sets `subject_id=NULL` (row still inserted, error logged — grade not lost). Skip-mode composite key `grade_name|subject_id|grade|min_marks|max_marks`. Registered in `BuildMigrators` after `ExamTypesMigrator` (dep: subjects, migrated earlier); cleanup deletes `grade_types` **before** `subjects` (FK) — added to `CleanupAsync` and `cleanup_migration_data.sql` near the top. Truncate/Skip + `grade_types.force` supported. Compile-verified. (Note: `grade_types` is migrate-only — no school-app UI/API consumes it yet.) (5) **New table + migrator — exam_master** (`SAS_ExamNam` (full rows) → `exam_master`): NEW tenant table `exam_master` (table 46) — `id` IDENTITY PK, `exam_type_id` INT NULL (FK → exam_types), `class_id` INT NULL (FK → classes), `exam_total_marks`/`exam_min_marks`/`subject_min_marks`/`sub_max_marks` INT, `exam_remarks` VARCHAR(300), `academic_year_id` INT NULL (FK → academic_years), `subject_id` INT NULL (FK → subjects), `exam_status` VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT 'Active', `created_by` VARCHAR(200), `created_date` DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE(), `school_id` INT, `exam_category` VARCHAR(200), `exam_date` DATE, `grade_type_id` INT NULL (FK → grade_types). Added to `tenant_tables.sql` + new idempotent `Database/exam_master_migration.sql`. Migrator `AscentMigration/Migrators/ExamMasterMigrator.cs` + `Models/Legacy/LegacyExamMaster.cs`. **Source is the SAME `SAS_ExamNam` table that feeds ExamTypesMigrator** — but read in full (`SELECT *`, one exam_master row per legacy row), whereas ExamTypesMigrator reads only DISTINCT names. Direct maps: `ExamTotalMarks`→`exam_total_marks`, `ExamMinMarks`→`exam_min_marks`, `SubMinMrks`→`subject_min_marks`, `SubMaxMrks`→`sub_max_marks`, `ExamRemarks`→`exam_remarks`, `ExamCatgry`→`exam_category`, `ExamDatTim`(datetime)→`exam_date`(date), `ExamStatus` A→Active/D→Inactive/else→Active; `created_date`=now, `created_by`="migration", `school_id`=`Config.SchoolId`. **Lookups:** `AcdYear`→`academic_year_id` (academic_years map, fallback default year); `ExamNam`→`exam_type_id` via exam_types keyed `name|academic_year_id` (per-year; falls back to name-only/latest-year, NULL+error on miss); `ClasId`→`class_id` two-step (legacy `SAS_ClassNames.ClassID→ClassNam` then dest `classes.class_name→class_id`); `SubjectID`→`subject_id` two-step (legacy `SAS_Subjects.SubJectID→SubjectNam` then dest subjects keyed `name|academic_year_id`, fallback name-latest); `GradeTyp`→`grade_type_id` — legacy `SAS_MarksGrade.MrksGradId→GradName`, then dest `grade_types` matched by **`grade_name|subject_name`** (per user decision: grade_types has NO class_id, so the class condition in the original spec is dropped; subject matched by NAME via `grade_types LEFT JOIN subjects` to decouple from per-year subject_id). Every lookup miss → that FK column NULL + a per-row error logged (row still inserted; nothing lost). Skip-mode composite key `exam_type_id|class_id|subject_id|academic_year_id|exam_category`. Registered in `BuildMigrators` after `GradeTypesMigrator` (all 5 parent tables migrated earlier); cleanup deletes `exam_master` **first** (it's the most-child table) — added at the TOP of `CleanupAsync` and `cleanup_migration_data.sql`. Note: `LoadExamTypeMap`/`LoadSubjectMap` are SYNC (not async) because they use `out` params (C# forbids `out` on async) — they call sync `dest.Query`. Truncate/Skip + `exam_master.force` supported. Compile-verified. Migrate-only (no UI/API yet).
Phase 70 ✅  Daily (multi-subject) homework entry — legacy-style "enter all subjects' homework for a class on one date" page, alongside the existing single-entry Homework List. **Backend:** `POST /school/homework/batch` (`HomeworkController.CreateBatchHomework`) takes `BatchHomeworkRequest` (`ClassId`, `SectionId`, `AssignedDate`, `Items: [{SubjectId, Description}]`; new DTOs in `HomeworkDto.cs`); validates ≥1 filled subject. `HomeworkRepository.CreateBatchHomework` runs in a transaction: resolves subject names in one query (row title = `"{subjectName} Homework"`), **soft-cancels (`status='Cancelled'`) that day's existing Active rows for the same Class+Section** (matched with `ISNULL(class_id,0)`/`ISNULL(section_id,0)`), then inserts one `homework` row per filled subject with `due_date = assigned_date` (legacy form: H.W. date IS the due date) — so re-saving the same Class+Section+Date acts as create-or-edit. Returns the count of subjects saved. **Frontend:** `ascent-school-app/src/pages/homework/DailyHomeworkPage.jsx` (Class/Section/Date pickers → grid of subjects with a Description field each → Save). Homework nav restructured into a parent menu with two leaves: **"Daily Homework"** (`/homework/daily`) and **"Homework List"** (`/homework`). Both gated by the `HOMEWORK.MANAGE` permission (Phase 50). The mobile homework feed (`section_id IS NULL OR = @sectionId`, Phase 19) reads these rows unchanged.
Phase 69 ✅  Pending online-payment reconciliation — recovers gateway payments where the parent paid but left the app/browser before the JS success callback ran AND no webhook is configured (the order stays `Pending` forever, parent is debited, no receipt). New admin page **"Pending Online Payments"** under Fees (`/fees/pending-payments`, gated by `STUDENT_FEE` perms). Uses the gateway API keys already in `gateway_configs` — **no Razorpay dashboard access needed**. **Backend (`FeeController` + `GatewayRepository` + `IGatewayService`/`RazorpayGatewayService`):** new `IGatewayService.FetchOrderPayment(keyId, keySecret, externalOrderId)` → `OrderPaymentStatus` (Found/PaymentId/Status/AmountInRupees/Method); Razorpay impl calls `GET orders/{id}/payments` (Basic auth), prefers a `captured` payment else the latest attempt. Endpoints: `GET /school/fees/payment-orders/pending?createdAfter=&createdBefore=` (lists Pending orders joined to student → `PendingPaymentOrderDto`), `GET /school/fees/payment-orders/{id}/status` (live single-order status → `PaymentOrderStatusDto`), `GET /school/fees/payment-orders/scan?...&onlyReconcilable=true` (checks every pending order at the gateway in one pass → `ScannedPaymentOrderDto[]`, default returns only captured/reconcilable rows so the admin needn't click each), `POST /school/fees/payment-orders/{id}/reconcile` (creates the receipt for a genuinely-captured order). A shared private `ReconcileOrderToReceipt(...)` is now the single receipt-creation path for the verify callback, the webhook, AND the manual reconcile (each tags receipt remarks with its source: `""`/` via webhook`/` via reconcile`) — replaces the duplicated collect-request reconstruction. `EvaluateStatus(...)` centralizes the reconcilable decision (captured + amount matches within ₹0.01 + not already Paid) and its human-readable note, shared by single-status and scan. Reconcile is idempotent (a late webhook that landed first short-circuits to the existing receipt) and refuses on non-captured/amount-mismatch. `GatewayRepository.GetPendingOrders` + `GatewayOrderListItem` (data-layer row shape) added. **Frontend:** `ascent-school-app/src/pages/fee/PendingPaymentsPage.jsx` (date-range filter → list pending orders, per-row "Check status", bulk "Scan", "Reconcile" on captured rows). No DB change.
Phase 67 ✅  Attendance current-year scope — more spots — extending the Phase 66 attendance fix to the remaining attendance reads that lacked the current-academic-year filter (multi-year student model: one Active row per student per year, classes/sections shared across years → prior-year/promoted students leaked in / counts inflated). Fixed: (1) `ReportsRepository.GetAttendanceSheet` (the only attendance report missing it — the others already take `@academicYearId`; this one has no year picker so it uses the current-year subquery); (2) `DashboardRepository` "today's attendance" — now JOINs `students` + filters current year (was a raw `student_attendance` COUNT with no student join, so stray marks on prior-year student rows inflated the dashboard count); (3) `ReportsRepository.GetDailyAttendanceSummary` — added the current-year filter to the grouped query. All use the same `(SELECT TOP 1 academic_year_id FROM academic_years WHERE school_id=@schoolId AND status='Active' ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC)` subquery. API-only. Note: attendance rows previously SAVED against prior-year student_ids (via the old all-years grid) still exist in `student_attendance` — now excluded from counts but not deleted; a cleanup query can remove them if desired.
Phase 66 ✅  School-app footer version stamp (frontend-only) — the `ascent-school-app` footer ("Powered by Ascent Info Solutions") now also shows `· v{version} ({build date})` so you can tell which build is live. `vite.config.js` reads `package.json` and injects two build-time globals via `define`: `__APP_VERSION__` (from `package.json.version`, set to `1.0.0` — bump per release) and `__BUILD_DATE__` (`new Date()` at build, `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm`, auto-updates every build so a fresh deploy is distinguishable even without a version bump). `AppLayout.jsx` renders them (with a top-of-file `/* global __APP_VERSION__, __BUILD_DATE__ */` for lint). Each white-label flavor stamps its own build time. Frontend-only; school app only (parent/control not changed). Also fixed attendance: `AttendanceRepository` (grid + existing-marks subquery + monthly summary) now filters students by the current academic year (`academic_year_id = (SELECT TOP 1 ... FROM academic_years WHERE school_id=@schoolId AND status='Active' ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC)`) — the multi-year student model previously returned prior-year/promoted students for a class/section (classes & sections are shared across years), so the roster was wrong; API-only, redeploy.
Phase 65 ✅  Android session-keepalive + refresh (Android-only) — (1) **Stay logged in:** the app had NO in-session token refresh (only a cold-start `silentRefresh()`), so a mid-session 401 (access token expired during inactivity) surfaced "session expired" and forced re-login — unlike the web apps whose HTTP layer refreshes-and-retries on 401. Added an **OkHttp `Authenticator`** in `RetrofitClient.kt`: on 401 it calls the parent/teacher refresh endpoint via a separate cookie-sharing client (no recursion; skips `/auth/` calls; caps retries via `responseCount`; thread-safe via `refreshLock`), saves the new access token to `TokenStore`, and retries the original request → app stays logged in for the full 7-day sliding window. **Prereqs (same as web):** API deployed with Phase 56 cookie `Path=/` + 480-min token, and ONE re-login to replace the old `/mobile/auth/parent`-path cookie (which never reaches `/api/...`). (2) **Data refresh** (staying logged in does NOT auto-refresh screen data — each tab loads once on its ViewModel init): added **pull-to-refresh per tab** via a reusable `ui/components/PullToRefresh.kt` wrapper (Material3 1.2.x experimental `PullToRefreshContainer` — BOM 2024.08 lacks `PullToRefreshBox`), applied in `HomeScreen`'s `when(tab)` around each screen (no per-screen edits — LazyColumn nested-scroll feeds the gesture; brief spinner then the tab's own load runs); plus a **global Refresh icon** in the `TopAppBar` that calls every tab VM's `load()` / `feeVm.loadFees()` (all VMs are created upfront in HomeScreen). Android-only; compile-verified. (3) **Refresh button also re-checks app version:** since long-lived sessions may never cold-start, the global Refresh button now also re-runs the Phase 57 update gate — `MainActivity` exposes a `recheckUpdate` callback (re-runs `appConfigRepo.checkVersion()`, resets `softDismissed`) passed to `HomeScreen` as `onCheckUpdate`; the refresh button invokes it, so Forced → `ForceUpdateScreen` (blocking) / Soft → `UpdateAvailableDialog`, reusing the existing UI (no new API/DB; `/mobile/app/config` + `app_config`).
Phase 64 ✅  Attendance → SMS absentees on save (frontend-only) — after Attendance "Save" succeeds, if any student is marked Absent, `AttendancePage.jsx` shows a `modal.confirm` "Send SMS to absentees?" ; on Yes it reuses the SMS Center ABSENT flow: `GET /school/sms/recipients?smsType=Absent&date=&classId=&sectionId=` (server recomputes the just-saved absentees WITH parent mobile + class + admissionNo) then `POST /school/sms/send` with `smsType:'Absent'`. Uses the school's configured ABSENT template/gateway + `{name}/{class}/{admissionNo}/{date}` substitution (Phase 60). Recipients are read from the server (not the grid) because the attendance grid lacks parent mobile/class; the recipients endpoint also auto-excludes absentees without a mobile, so the toast reports actual sent/failed (may be fewer than the popup's grid count). No API/DB change — reuses existing endpoints; deploy the school app only. Prerequisite: SMS gateway + active ABSENT template configured (Phase 55/60) and API deployed with the Phase 61 Task.Run fix, else the send returns a clear error (attendance still saves).
Phase 63 ✅  School-app UI tweaks (frontend-only) — (1) **Full-width layout:** `ascent-school-app/src/index.css` `#root` was locked to a fixed centered column (`width:1126px; margin:0 auto; border-inline; text-align:center`) left over from the Vite starter, causing left/right empty space — changed to `width:100%` (removed the fixed width, centering, side border, inherited center-align; AntD handles its own layout). The rest of `index.css`/`App.css` are still unused starter styles (`.counter`/`.hero`/`#social`/big `h1`) — harmless. The parent (`ascent-parent-app`) and control (`ascent-control-app`) apps likely share the same starter `#root` and would need the same fix if their gap matters. (2) **Attendance serial number:** added a `#` column (row index + 1) as the first column of the mark-attendance table (`markColumns` in `AttendancePage.jsx`); the Monthly Summary table doesn't have it. Both are school-app-only; no API/DB change.
Phase 62 ✅  SMS Center "SMS Type" dropdown labels from template Titles (frontend-only) — the Send-tab "SMS Type" dropdown was hardcoded (`SMS_TYPES` = Absent/FeeDue/Custom, fixed labels). Now the **labels** are loaded from each school's templates: on mount `SMSPage.jsx` (SendTab) calls `GET /school/sms/config`, maps each fixed type to its template via `SMS_TYPE_KEY` (`Absent→ABSENT`, `FeeDue→FEE_DUE`, `Custom→CUSTOM`), and uses that template's `title` as the label (falls back to the hardcoded label when a template/title is missing). So renaming a template's Title in Settings → SMS Gateway renames the dropdown. **Still only the 3 fixed types** — the dropdown `value`s and their per-type recipient logic (date vs year filters, `GetRecipients` queries) are unchanged; a brand-new template key does NOT create a new type (that needs code in ~4 places: `SMS_TYPES`/dropdown, `GetRecipients`+repo query, the type's filter UI, and `TemplateKeyFor`). The History-tab type filter still uses the static labels. No API/DB change; deploy the school app only.
Phase 61 ✅  SMS Center send crash fix — `TenantContext.Current` is null inside `Task.Run` — sending from SMS Center returned 500 "An error has occurred / Object reference not set" (shown as "Network Error" per recipient in the grid), while the SMS still went out. Cause: `SmsController.Send` dispatches each recipient on a parallel `Task.Run` thread and called `_repo.LogSms(Tenant.TenantDbName, Tenant.SchoolId, ...)` inside it; **`TenantContext.Current` reads `HttpContext.Current.Items`, and worker threads have no `HttpContext`** → `Tenant` is null → NRE at the LogSms call (after the SMS was already sent). Latent since the Phase 12 parallel dispatch; surfaced now that SMS Center is used end-to-end. Fix: hoist `var dbName = Tenant.TenantDbName; var schoolId = Tenant.SchoolId;` (and `sentBy`) into locals **before** the `Task.Run` loop and use those inside the lambda — never touch `Tenant`/`TenantContext.Current`/`HttpContext.Current` inside `Task.Run`/`Task.WhenAll` worker threads. API-only fix; redeploy required. (Companion content note: a template whose Message Text still contains the DLT `{#var#}` syntax sends literally — the server only substitutes the named tokens `{name}/{class}/{admissionNo}/{date}/{amount}`, so put those in Message Text, not `{#var#}`.)
Phase 60 ✅  SMS Center placeholders extended — `{class}` + `{admissionNo}` — the SMS template substitution (`SmsController.BuildMessage`) previously only filled `{name}`/`{date}`/`{amount}`, and the send payload didn't even carry class/admission-no to the server. Added so schools can use real DLT templates like `Dear Parent, Your ward {name} of {class}, Admin No : {admissionNo} is Absent on {date}.ST.ANNE'S`. **Full token set now:** `{name}` (student name), `{class}` (class name), `{admissionNo}` (admission no), `{date}` (Absent date, `yyyy-MM-dd`), `{amount}` (Fee Due outstanding). Changes: `SmsRecipient` DTO (`SmsDtos.cs`) gains `AdmissionNo` + `ClassName`; `BuildMessage` adds `.Replace("{class}",...)` + `.Replace("{admissionNo}",...)`; `SMSPage.jsx` send payload now includes `admissionNo`/`className` per recipient (`GetRecipients` already returned them — they were dropped before); `SmsGatewayPage.jsx` hint text lists the full set. Tokens go in the template's **Message Text** field; the "Placeholders (hint)" field is cosmetic only (never read by code). **Both API + school app must be deployed together** — until then `{class}`/`{admissionNo}` render literally. No DB change. **UI discoverability (frontend-only follow-up):** `SmsGatewayPage.jsx` Add/Edit-Template modal now shows the available placeholders as **clickable chips** (click inserts the token into Message Text) + a legend `{name} = Student name · {class} = Class name · …`, driven by a `PLACEHOLDERS` constant that mirrors `BuildMessage` (keep in sync when adding server-side tokens); the "Placeholders (hint)" field is labelled optional/for-reference-only. The stored message_text's static text + variable order must match the school's DLT-approved template exactly or the operator rejects it. (Date currently sent as `yyyy-MM-dd`; format in `BuildMessage` if the DLT template needs `dd-mm-yyyy`.)
Phase 59 ✅  One-click deploy scripts + build fixes — replaced the manual "build each school -> overwrite dist -> copy to Plesk -> repeat" workflow with PowerShell deploy scripts under `deploy/` (native `System.Net.FtpWebRequest`, no WinSCP). **Files:** `deploy/deploy-school.ps1`, `deploy/deploy-parent.ps1` (`-School <name>|all`, `-SkipBuild`), `deploy/deploy-api.ps1` (`-SkipBuild`), `deploy/_common.ps1` (FTP upload + config loader + `Invoke-ViteBuild`), `deploy/deploy.config.example.json` (committed template) + `deploy/deploy.config.json` (**gitignored** — FTP creds + per-school remote paths), `deploy/README.md`. Each script **builds then FTP-uploads**: React apps build each flavor into its **own** `dist/<name>` via `npm run build:<mode> -- --outDir dist/<name> --emptyOutDir` (fixes the shared-`dist` clobber), API publishes via MSBuild FolderProfile (`bin/Release/Publish`). **API safety rules:** `Uploads/` is never uploaded (`api.excludeDirs`, preserves server media); `web.config` uploaded only if `uploadWebConfig=true` AND it does NOT still contain the placeholder `CHANGE_THIS_TO_A_STRONG_SECRET` (guard against pushing template secrets to prod). Native FTP has no diff-sync → full upload each run, additive (nothing deleted). **Build fixes made along the way:** (1) `AscentSchools.API.csproj` web-publish import was hardcoded to `v15.0` (`...\VisualStudio\v15.0\WebApplications\Microsoft.WebApplication.targets`) which fails on machines with only newer VS (e.g. VS18) — replaced with the **version-agnostic** `$(VSToolsPath)` pattern (`VisualStudioVersion`/`VSToolsPath` defaults + `Import ... Condition=Exists(...)`); `deploy-api.ps1` locates the installed `Microsoft.WebApplication.targets` via `vswhere` and passes `/p:VSToolsPath=...`. Works under any VS, CLI or IDE. (2) Parent app `package.json` build scripts realigned to its `.env.*` files (added `build:depaulemyv`/`build:holyspiritjm`, removed orphan `build:srividya`). (3) SPA fallback: `.htaccess` + `web.config` (URL-rewrite all non-file paths → `/index.html`) live in each web app's `public/` so a browser refresh on a deep route (e.g. `/students`) loads the app instead of a host 404. Run scripts from repo root: `.\deploy\deploy-api.ps1`, `.\deploy\deploy-school.ps1 -School stannsasf`. Only `stannsasf` + API path (`/httpdocs/api`) confirmed; other school/parent docroots in the config are pattern-guessed — verify in Plesk. **Remote-path model:** the scripts upload to the **literal** `schoolRemote` / `parentRemote` string per school — there is NO prefix/convention logic in code (the `parent.<sub>` defaults are just placeholder guesses; real Plesk dirs vary, e.g. demo's parent is `/p.demo.edu-care.in/`). `deploy-school.ps1` uses `schoolRemote`; `deploy-parent.ps1` builds from `parentApp.appDir` and uploads to `parentRemote`. A school whose `parentRemote` is `""`/`null` is skipped by `deploy-parent.ps1`. Edit each path in the gitignored `deploy.config.json` to the exact Plesk Document Root.
Phase 59b ✅  Android debug cleartext fix — testing the `stannsasf` flavor in DEBUG against the local API (`http://localhost:62845/`) threw "CLEARTEXT communication to localhost not permitted". Root cause: the manifest sets `usesCleartextTraffic="true"` but ALSO `networkSecurityConfig="@xml/network_security_config"`, and **when a network security config is present, `usesCleartextTraffic` is ignored**; the `stannsasf` (and `depaulemyv`) flavor ship their own `res/xml/network_security_config.xml` with `cleartextTrafficPermitted="false"` (prod-locked), overriding `main`'s `true`. Fix: added **`app/src/debug/res/xml/network_security_config.xml`** with `cleartextTrafficPermitted="true"` — in Android resource merging the **build-type (`debug`) source set overrides the flavor**, so ALL `*Debug` builds permit cleartext while every release build keeps its flavor's secure (https-only) config. Verified via `mergeStannsasfDebugResources` → packaged_res resolves to `true`. (Note: a variant source set like `src/stannsasfDebug` also works but the single build-type file covers every flavor.) Reminder for physical-device debug: `localhost` is the phone itself, so run `adb reverse tcp:62845 tcp:62845` once per adb session so the phone reaches the host's IIS Express; rebuild/reinstall the debug app to pick up the new config.
Phase 58 ✅  In-app receipt print (Android + parent web) + fee-collection fixes — (A) **Concession double-count fix:** `li.outstanding` already nets out concession (structure − paid − concession), but all three payment clients also sent `ConcessionAmount` separately, so the server subtracted it twice → online order failed with "Total amount must be greater than zero" (and offline made negative receipts). Fixed so each item sends `Amount = outstanding + concession`, `ConcessionAmount = concession` → server stores `net_amount = outstanding` (correct paid total) while the receipt still shows the concession line (`FeeCollectionBase.jsx` school, `FeeHomePage.jsx` parent web, `FeeViewModel.kt` Android). (B) **Mobile popup "₹0" fix:** Android `MobilePaymentResultDto.amount` didn't match the server's `totalAmount` JSON → always 0.0; renamed field to `totalAmount` (the receipt + money were always correct — display only). (C) **Receipt "Collected by":** Android sends header `X-Client-App: mobile` (RetrofitClient); `MobileFeesController.ResolveClientSource()` → `"Mobile App"` for the app, defaults to `"Parent Portal"` for the web (no header). Stored as `created_by` on the order (so the Razorpay webhook fallback inherits it) and the receipt. (D) **Print receipt:** new `GET /mobile/fees/receipts/{id}` (MobileFeesController, validated to the child by `student_unique_id` via `FeeRepository.ReceiptBelongsToUniqueId`); `GetCrossYearFeeSummary` line items gained `ReceiptId` + `CreatedBy` (correlated subqueries in school/transport/hostel branches). **Android:** `MobileFeeLineItemDto.canPrint` = paid AND receiptId != null AND `createdBy == "Mobile App"`; a Print icon on paid rows + a "View / Print Receipt" button on the success dialog; `ReceiptPrinter.kt` builds branded HTML → offscreen WebView → `PrintManager` (Print or Save-as-PDF, no library). **Parent web:** Print button on paid rows where `createdBy == "Parent Portal"`; fetches the receipt and reuses `PaymentSuccessPage` (with a `reprint` flag → header shows "Fee Receipt"). Tapping a paid term prints the whole receipt it belongs to (may include other terms paid together). OTP and the school web app receipt print (Phase 42) unchanged.
Phase 57 ✅  Mobile app update gate (server-driven force/soft update) — lets you force every installed app onto a safe version by flipping a DB flag, with no app redeploy. **DB (master):** `app_config` table (`application_id`, `platform`, `min_supported_version_code`, `latest_version_code`, `update_message`, `store_url`; UNIQUE(application_id, platform)); migration `Database/app_config_migration.sql` seeds one row per published applicationId (`in.educare.app`/`stannsasf`/`holyspiritjm`/`depaulemyv`/`demo`) + a `'*'` fallback, armed but forcing nobody (`min=1, latest=12`); added to `master_tables.sql` (table 16). **Backend:** `GET /mobile/app/config?applicationId=&platform=android&versionCode=` (`MobileAppConfigController`, AllowAnonymous — called on cold start before login); `AppConfigRepository.GetConfig` (exact app row, falls back to `'*'`); computes `updateRequired` (versionCode < min) / `updateAvailable` (< latest); **fails open** (returns "no update" when no config row); DTOs `AppConfigDto`/`AppVersionStatusDto` in `Core/DTOs/Mobile/AppConfig/AppConfigDtos.cs`. **Android:** `AppConfigRepository.checkVersion()` (fail-open) returns `UpdateStatus` (Ok/Soft/Forced); `MainActivity` checks on launch → `ForceUpdateScreen` (full-screen, back-disabled) when required, dismissible `UpdateAvailableDialog` when optional, else proceeds; `ui/update/UpdateScreens.kt` + `openPlayStore()` (uses `store_url` or `market://details?id=<pkg>`). To force later: `UPDATE app_config SET min_supported_version_code=<N> WHERE application_id='...'`. Must be shipped in v1 so it's in the field before a breaking bug (zero installs at build time = clean). iOS-ready via the `platform` column. **OPERATIONAL GOTCHAS (learned the hard way):** (1) `min_supported_version_code` / `latest_version_code` are Gradle **`versionCode` INTEGERS** (e.g. 13, 16) — NOT `versionName` strings ("1.5"). Putting "1.5" in the INT column stores `1`/`2` and breaks the gate. (2) **Never set `min` above the versionCode actually LIVE in Play Production** — doing so force-loops every user (the live app is below min, and there's no newer version to update to). Sequence: promote the new versionCode to **Production** (not just a beta/closed-testing track — a beta build is NOT what normal installs get) → confirm it's rolled out and a device reports it (the school-app/login footer shows `(build N)`; or `adb shell dumpsys package <appId> | findstr versionCode`) → THEN raise `min`/`latest`. (3) Android **Settings → Apps shows versionName, not versionCode** — to read the real installed versionCode use the login-screen footer `(build N)` (Phase 65) or `adb dumpsys`. (4) Unblock a force-loop instantly (no redeploy) by lowering `min_supported_version_code` to ≤ the live versionCode.
Phase 56 ✅  Session/refresh fix — "logged out after idle" — two parts. (1) **Root-cause fix:** silent token refresh always failed in production, so users were kicked to the login page the moment the 30-min access token expired (only noticeable after idle, since within 30 min the in-memory access token covered everything; tabs left open felt fine until idle). Cause: every refresh cookie was scoped to its route path (`schoolRefreshToken` → `Path=/school/auth`, `controlRefreshToken` → `/control/auth`, `parentRefreshToken` → `/mobile/auth/parent`, `studentRefreshToken` → `/mobile/auth/student`, `teacherRefreshToken` → `/mobile/auth/teacher`), but the API is hosted under `/api` in production (`https://edu-care.in/api`), so the real refresh URLs are `/api/school/auth/refresh` etc. — the browser/OkHttp never sent a `/school/auth`-scoped cookie to a `/api/school/auth/refresh` request (path-prefix mismatch) → refresh 401 → logout. Worked in local dev only because dev has no `/api` prefix (`http://localhost:62845/school/auth/refresh` matched). Fix: set `Path=/` on ALL refresh cookies (set + clear) in `SchoolAuthController`, `ControlAuthController`, `MobileAuthController` (parent + student), `MobileTeacherAuthController`. `/` is a strict superset so it's safe regardless of deployment topology (subdomain vs path, with/without `/api`). Existing orphaned cookies (old path) are harmless and expire in 7 days; a fresh login re-issues at `/`. (2) **Mitigation:** bumped `Jwt:AccessTokenMins` 30 → 480 (8 h) in Web.config so an idle user stays active a full school day even before a refresh is needed (Web.config edit auto-restarts the app pool, no rebuild). Note: cookies still `Secure=false` (left as-is so local http dev works; same-site Lax default sends them fine on https — revisit if ever served cross-site, which needs `SameSite=None; Secure`). Both web apps' page-reload session restore was separately fixed earlier via the `authChecked` render gate (don't redirect to /login until the mount silent-refresh resolves).
Phase 55 ✅  Per-school SMS gateway config — the SMS Center previously sent via a single hardcoded smslogin.mobi account in `SmsHelper.cs` (username `mushkin` / sender `ASNTNF`) with **placeholder** DLT template ids (`PLACEHOLDER_ABSENT_TPL_ID` etc.), so Absent/FeeDue/Custom SMS could never actually send. Made the gateway account + DLT templates **per-school, DB-driven** (normalized two-table design so new SMS types are data, not schema). **DB (tenant):** `sms_configs` (one row per school — provider, api_url, username, api_key, sender_id, is_enabled) + `sms_templates` (one row per school per `template_key` — template_id, message_text with `{name}/{date}/{amount}` placeholders, placeholders hint, is_active; UNIQUE(school_id, template_key)); migration `Database/sms_config_migration.sql` (seeds existing schools from DISTINCT `students.school_id` with the old shared account + 3 default templates `ABSENT`/`FEE_DUE`/`CUSTOM`, template_id blank); added to `tenant_tables.sql` (structure) + `seed_tenant_data.sql` (per-`@SchoolId` seed). **Backend:** `SmsHelper.SendSms` gained a per-account overload `(apiUrl, username, apiKey, senderId, mobile, message, templateId)`; old global overload kept for OTP; the 3 placeholder template consts removed. `SmsRepository` gained `GetSmsAccount` (incl. api_key, send path), `GetSmsConfig` (api_key omitted → `HasApiKey` flag, includes templates), `UpsertSmsConfig` (blank api_key keeps existing), `GetTemplates`/`GetTemplate`/`UpsertTemplate`/`DeleteTemplate`. `SmsController` `Send` now loads the school's account + template by key (Absent→ABSENT, FeeDue→FEE_DUE, Custom→CUSTOM), errors clearly if not configured/disabled/no DLT id, fills placeholders from recipient/context (Custom uses the typed message); new endpoints `GET/PUT /school/sms/config`, `POST /school/sms/templates`, `DELETE /school/sms/templates/{key}`; DTOs `SmsConfigDto`/`UpdateSmsConfigRequest`/`SmsTemplateDto`/`SaveSmsTemplateRequest`/`SmsAccount` added to `SmsDtos.cs`. **Frontend:** new `pages/settings/SmsGatewayPage.jsx` (gateway-account card with write-only API key + a templates table with add/edit/delete modal), under **Settings → SMS Gateway** (`/settings/sms-gateway`), gated by `SETTINGS.MANAGE`. **OTP is intentionally unchanged** — parent app + mobile login OTP still use the hardcoded global account + `OtpTemplateId` in `SmsHelper.cs`. Go-live: run `sms_config_migration.sql` on each tenant DB, deploy API + school app, then each school sets its own sender id + DLT template ids in the new page (Send fails with a clear message until configured).
Phase 54 ✅  Exam Types management UI — the Marks Entry "Exam Type" dropdown was always empty because exam types are per-academic-year rows in the `exam_types` tenant table, the backend CRUD existed (`GET/POST/PUT /school/marks/exam-types` in `MarksController`/`MarksRepository`) but **no frontend screen ever created them** (only `MarksEntryPage` + the 3 exam report pages read them). Added `ascent-school-app/src/pages/master/ExamTypesTab.jsx` (modeled on `FeePeriodsTab`) and wired it into `MasterDataPage.jsx` as a new "Exam Types" tab between Subjects and Payment Modes: academic-year filter (auto-selects current year via `?activeOnly=true` + `isCurrent`), table (Order / Exam Type / Status / edit), add/edit modal (Academic Year required, Exam Type Name required max 50, Display Order). Uses the existing endpoints — DTO `SaveExamTypeRequest` = `ExamTypeName` + `AcademicYearId` + `DisplayOrder`; no backend change. Notes: backend `GetExamTypes` returns Active-only and create/update expose no status field (no delete/deactivate endpoint yet), so the form omits status (shows a green "Active" tag); exam types must be created per academic year (Marks Entry filters by year). Gated by the `MASTER_DATA` permission like the rest of Master Data (Phase 50). Quick SQL unblock without the UI: `INSERT INTO exam_types (exam_type_name, academic_year_id, display_order, school_id, created_by, status) VALUES ('FA 1', @YearId, 1, @SchoolId, 'manual', 'Active'), ...`.
Phase 53 ✅  RBAC fix — role→permission mapping matched by name, not `school_id IS NULL` — after Phase 50 (page gating), an admin logged in and saw ONLY the Dashboard. Root cause: `GetUserPermissions` (`SchoolAuthRepository`) returned ZERO permissions because the role→permission seed/migration mapped permissions with `WHERE r.role_name = '...' AND r.school_id IS NULL`. In some tenant DBs (e.g. migrated ones) the default roles (`Principal`, `Admin Clerk`, ...) are created with a **non-NULL `school_id`** (observed: Principal role_id=1 had `school_id=5`), so the `school_id IS NULL` filter matched nothing and NO `role_permissions` rows were ever inserted — so every JWT came back with an empty `perm[]` and the gated UI hid everything except Dashboard (the one route with no permission requirement). This was a latent bug from day one; Phase 50 surfaced it because nothing consumed the permissions array before. The login `schoolId` resolution itself was fine (`users.school_id` if set, else first `user_roles.school_id`) and matched the assignment — the gap was purely the missing `role_permissions` rows. Fix: both `Database/seed_tenant_data.sql` and `Database/admin_permissions_migration.sql` now match roles by `role_name` ONLY (dropped `AND r.school_id IS NULL`), so every same-named role row gets mapped regardless of its `school_id`. `admin_permissions_migration.sql` was also upgraded from a new-perms-only patch into a **complete role-permission repair**: Principal → ALL active permissions (CROSS JOIN), and Admin Clerk / Fee Clerk / Class Teacher / Librarian → their **full intended sets (original 24 subset + new Phase-50 codes)**, all idempotent (`NOT EXISTS` guard) — so re-running it repairs an existing broken DB in one pass. Diagnostic that confirmed it: `SELECT ... FROM user_roles ur JOIN role_permissions rp ... WHERE ur.user_id=@uid AND ur.school_id=@schoolId` returned no rows while `user_roles` showed Principal at school_id=5. Per-DB hotfix (no redeploy): `INSERT INTO role_permissions SELECT DISTINCT ur.role_id, p.permission_id FROM user_roles ur CROSS JOIN permissions p WHERE ur.user_id=(SELECT user_id FROM users WHERE username='admin') AND p.status='Active' AND NOT EXISTS(...)`, then re-login. Note: the `permissions.status='Active'` filter in `GetUserPermissions` is fine — `permissions.status` defaults to `'Active'`, so seeded/migrated perms (which don't set status) are Active.
Phase 52 ✅  Migration tool — duplicate-key crash fix + academic_years status mapping — (1) **Duplicate-key crash:** `TermsMigrator`, `FeePeriodsMigrator`, and `SubjectsMigrator` built their `academic_years` lookup (`academic_year` string → `academic_year_id`) with LINQ `.ToDictionary(...)`, which throws `System.ArgumentException: An item with the same key has already been added` when the dest `academic_years` table has duplicate `academic_year` values for the school (caused by earlier re-runs without cleanup). Because the dictionary is built BEFORE any source rows are read, the migrator crashed even when the source had zero rows (e.g. `subjects` failing despite `SAS_Subjects`/`SAS_StuSubjects` being empty). Fixed by replacing the `ToDictionary` in all three with the same defensive `new Dictionary<string,int>(OrdinalIgnoreCase)` + `foreach` + `ContainsKey` loop already used by the larger migrators (`FeeStructuresMigrator`, `StudentsMigrator`, `FeeReceiptsMigrator`, `BusFeeStructuresMigrator`) — keeps the FIRST id on a duplicate and logs a `Warning: duplicate academic_year '...'` instead of throwing. Note: this only makes the lookup tolerant; duplicate `academic_year` rows remain a data problem (downstream FKs become ambiguous) — the real reset is `Cleanup=true` / `Database/cleanup_migration_data.sql` then re-migrate. Detect with `SELECT academic_year, COUNT(*) FROM academic_years WHERE school_id=@id GROUP BY academic_year HAVING COUNT(*)>1`. (2) **academic_years status mapping:** `AcademicYearsMigrator.MapStatus` maps legacy `SAS_AcdYear.AcdStatus` A→`Active`, D→`Inactive` (unchanged), but the default/unknown case changed from `Inactive` to `Active` (a year with a missing/odd status is now treated as Active rather than silently hidden). (3) Also fixed (Phase 51 follow-up) stray unfiltered `delete from homework`/`delete from subjects` lines in `cleanup_migration_data.sql` → now filter by `@SchoolId` with aligned `@@ROWCOUNT` prints.
Phase 51 ✅  Migration tool — fee_periods migrator — added `FeePeriodsMigrator` to `AscentMigration` to migrate monthly fee periods from the legacy VB6 DB. Source: `SELECT * FROM SAS_TermMonthData WHERE Descrpt = 'Monthly'` (the same legacy table `TermsMigrator` reads with `LIKE '%Term%'` — monthly rows are the `Descrpt='Monthly'` slice). Dest: `fee_periods` (tenant table 17). Reuses the existing `LegacyTerm` POCO (columns identical — no new model). Column mapping: `TrmMnthData`→`period_label`; `month_no` extracted from the label (`"October-2026"`→10; supports full month name "MMMM", 3-letter "MMM", and numeric, via `TryExtractMonthNo` splitting on `-`/`/`/space and `DateTime.TryParseExact`); `YearNam`→`year_no` (smallint; `"2026-27"`→2026 by taking the part before `-`, **fallback** to the trailing year embedded in the label via `TryParseYearNo`); `OrdrNo`→`sequence_no`; `TraStatus` A→`Active`/D→`Deleted`/else→`Active` (note: differs from `TermsMigrator` which maps D→Inactive — intentional, per the supplied mapping); `AcdYear`→`academic_year_id` via `academic_years.academic_year` lookup (school-scoped), **fallback** to `ResolveDefaultAcademicYearIdAsync` on miss (NOT null, so monthly fee structures stay linkable) — same pattern as `TermsMigrator`; `school_id`=`Config.SchoolId`, `created_by`="migration", `created_at`=`DateTime.Now`. Truncate mode deletes existing `fee_periods` for the school; Skip mode dedups on composite key `period_label|academic_year_id`. Rows with empty label / unrecognizable month / undeterminable year are recorded as per-row errors and skipped (non-fatal). Registered in `MigrationRunner.BuildMigrators` **after `TermsMigrator`, before `FeeStructuresMigrator`** (academic_years already migrated; runs before anything that could reference `fee_period_id`). Cleanup: `fee_periods` added to `MigrationRunner.CleanupAsync` AND `Database/cleanup_migration_data.sql` right after `fee_structures` (deleted after both `fee_receipt_items` and `fee_structures`, which carry `fee_period_id` FKs, so the reverse-FK-order transaction won't violate a constraint). Also fixed stray unfiltered `delete from homework`/`delete from subjects` lines in `cleanup_migration_data.sql` to filter by `@SchoolId` with proper `@@ROWCOUNT` prints. Idempotent via `_migration_log`; force re-run with `<add key="fee_periods.force" value="true" />`.
Phase 50 ✅  Role-based page access — Phase A (frontend UX gating) only — RBAC data model, JWT `perm[]` claims, and the `RequirePermissionAttribute` filter all already existed but were never wired up: every authenticated school user could reach every route and see every nav item, and **no backend endpoint enforces any permission**. Phase A adds frontend page/nav gating; **Phase B (backend endpoint enforcement via `[RequirePermission]` on controller actions) is NOT yet implemented — to be done later**. Added 15 new permission codes across 8 new modules (the original 24 codes covered only Student/Fee/Attendance/Marks/Library/Transport/Hostel, leaving Master Data, Homework, Announcements, Events, Staff, Reports, SMS, Settings, and User Management ungated): `MASTER_DATA.VIEW/MANAGE`, `HOMEWORK.VIEW/MANAGE`, `ANNOUNCEMENT.VIEW/MANAGE`, `EVENTS.VIEW/MANAGE`, `STAFF.VIEW/MANAGE`, `REPORTS.VIEW`, `SMS.SEND`, `SETTINGS.MANAGE`, `USER_MGMT.MANAGE`. **Frontend (`ascent-school-app`):** new `src/config/permissions.js` is the single source of truth — `P` (all permission-code constants, mirrors backend `PermissionCodes.cs`) + `PATH_PERM` (every absolute route path → required permission; `null` = always allowed, e.g. Dashboard) + `canAccessPath()` helper; new `src/pages/NoAccess.jsx` (AntD `Result` 403 + "Back to Dashboard"); `App.jsx` gains a `Protected({path, children})` wrapper that looks up `PATH_PERM[path]` and renders `<NoAccess/>` when `hasPermission()` is false — all 32 protected routes wrapped (Dashboard `index` left open so users with few permissions still land somewhere); `AppLayout.jsx` gains `filterNav(items, hasPermission)` — hides nav leaves the user lacks and drops parent menus (Students/Fees/Staff/Settings) when all children are filtered out; `Menu items` + `openKeys` now use the filtered `navItems`. The `hasPermission()` helper in `authStore.js` (previously dead code) is now the basis for both the route guard and nav filter. **Backend constants:** `PermissionCodes.cs` gains nested classes `MasterData/Homework/Announcement/Events/Staff/Reports/Sms/Settings/UserManagement` (ready for Phase B; not yet referenced by any controller). **DB:** `tenant_tables.sql` seeds the 15 new permissions for new onboarding; `seed_tenant_data.sql` maps them to default roles (Principal auto-gets all via existing `CROSS JOIN permissions`; Admin Clerk gains master-data/content/reports/SMS/staff-view; Fee Clerk gains reports; Class Teacher gains homework/announcements/events/master/reports); new idempotent migration `Database/admin_permissions_migration.sql` inserts the perms + maps them to the 4 default roles on existing tenant DBs. **Go-live actions:** (1) run `admin_permissions_migration.sql` on every existing `ascent_group_{N}` tenant DB — WITHOUT it the new pages gate on codes nobody holds and vanish for everyone including admins; (2) users must LOG OUT + LOG IN so the new perms land in their JWT; (3) rebuild/deploy the school app. **Important:** frontend gating is UX only, NOT a security boundary — the API is still open to any authenticated token until Phase B decorates the controllers. Custom roles created via the Roles UI need the new perms assigned by an admin (they appear automatically in the role permission drawer after the migration). VB6 API-key integration unaffected (granted all permissions in `JwtAuthenticationFilter`).
Phase 49 ✅  CHAK IN legal pages (generic app) — the generic single-app build is published on the Play Store as **CHAK IN** (applicationId `in.educare.app`) and needs its own Play-required Privacy Policy + Account Deletion URLs, separate from the per-flavor pages. Created a `chakin/` folder at repo root with `privacy-policy.html` + `account-deletion.html`, mirroring the existing per-flavor pattern (e.g. `stannsasf/`, `holyspiritjm/`, `depaul/` — each school folder has both files served at `https://edu-care.in/{folder}/...`, cross-linked). Kept consistent with the existing flavor pages: same HTML/CSS, developer "Sesh Rao", contact `support@edu-care.in`, Razorpay + smslogin.mobi third-party sections, 90-day retention, page cross-links. Adapted for the multi-school nature of CHAK IN (vs single-school flavors): app name "CHAK IN" throughout; single-school references generalized to "your school"; added a "School code" item to data-collected list + a "connect you to your school using the school code" usage point; account-deletion form gained a "School name" field (included in the generated mailto body). URLs after upload: `https://edu-care.in/chakin/privacy-policy.html` + `https://edu-care.in/chakin/account-deletion.html` — set these in the CHAK IN Play Console listing (Privacy Policy URL + Data safety deletion link). Note: per-flavor pages use developer "Sesh Rao" while the root `privacy-policy.html` ("Ascent Schools") uses company "Ascent Info Solutions"; CHAK IN pages follow the "Sesh Rao" per-flavor convention (revisit if the Play listing is under the company name).
Phase 48 ✅  Android generic-flavor logo on school-code + login screens — for the `ascent` (generic) flavor, a CHAK logo was added via Android Studio Image Asset, which generated launcher icons (`ic_launcher`/`ic_launcher_round`/`ic_launcher_foreground` + adaptive-icon XML) under `app/src/ascent/res/mipmap-*` — overriding only the generic app (baked flavors stannsasf/depaulemyv/holyspiritjm untouched) and replacing the placeholder indigo "A" from Phase 45. **Login screen (`SmsAuthScreen`)** already displayed a logo (school branding logo from API `tokenStore.brandingLogoUrl`, falling back to `getApplicationIcon()`), so the new flavor icon automatically became its fallback — no change needed. **School-code screen (`SchoolCodeScreen`)** had no logo (it appears before any school is selected, so only a generic app-bundled logo is possible there); added an `AsyncImage` at the top of the card showing `context.packageManager.getApplicationIcon(context.packageName)` (Coil), clipped to a `CircleShape` at 96dp, with `Spacer(16.dp)` before the "CHAK IN" title (new imports: `CircleShape`, `clip`, `LocalContext`, `coil.compose.AsyncImage`). Used `getApplicationIcon()` deliberately, NOT `painterResource(R.mipmap.ic_launcher_round)` — Image Asset now generates an adaptive-icon XML for `ic_launcher_round` too, so `painterResource` on it would crash on API 26+ (extends decisions #48/#49: the safe way to show the launcher icon inside Compose is Coil + `getApplicationIcon()`). Net: school-code screen always shows the generic CHAK logo; login screen shows the selected school's branding logo (CHAK fallback). Caveat: CHAK is a square text logo so the circle clip may crop edges slightly — switch to no-clip/rounded-rect if it looks tight.
Phase 47 ✅  Android Gradle release signing — release builds were never wired to a signing config, so the *Generate Signed Bundle / APK* wizard showed all `*Debug` variants greyed out (debuggable builds can't be release-signed — that's by design; pick a `*Release` variant) and required manually re-entering the keystore each time. A keystore already existed at repo root `keystore/ascent-keystore.jks` (alias `key0`, store+key password identical, valid until 2051) but wasn't connected to Gradle. Added `AscentSchoolsMobile/keystore.properties` (storeFile/storePassword/keyAlias/keyPassword; **gitignored** — added `keystore.properties` + `**/keystore.properties` to root `.gitignore`); `app/build.gradle.kts` loads it via `rootProject.file("keystore.properties")` + `Properties().load(FileInputStream(...))` (imports `java.io.FileInputStream` + `java.util.Properties` at file top), adds a `signingConfigs { create("release") { ... } }` block, and attaches it in `buildTypes.release` — all guarded by `if (keystorePropertiesFile.exists())` so a fresh clone without the file still builds unsigned and the wizard still works. Result: `gradlew bundleAscentRelease` (AAB) / `assembleAscentRelease` (APK) now produce a **signed** artifact automatically (verified `jar verified` on `app-ascent-release.aab`), and Android Studio's **Build → Build Bundle(s)/APK(s)** signs without the wizard. Same for other flavors: `bundle{Stannsasf,Depaulemyv,Holyspiritjm,Demo}Release`. **Security caveat (not yet addressed — user's call):** `keystore/ascent-keystore.jks` AND `keystore/strong pwd.txt` are committed to git; recommend `git rm --cached` both + gitignore `keystore/` and keep a private backup (losing the key = can never update the Play Store listing).
Phase 46 ✅  Android "Change School" on home page — the generic single-app build previously only exposed "Change School" on the login screen (`SmsAuthScreen`, pre-login); after login the home `⋮` menu had just "Switch Child" + "Logout", so switching school post-login required a manual Logout first. Added a **"Change School"** `DropdownMenuItem` (School icon) to the `HomeScreen` overflow menu, between "Switch Child" and "Logout". `HomeScreen.kt` gains an `onChangeSchool: (() -> Unit)? = null` param; the menu item renders only when non-null. `MainActivity.kt` wires it **only for the generic flavor** (`isGenericApp`; null for baked flavors so it never shows there — their school is fixed). Because the home page is post-login (session is scoped to the current school's group), the callback ends the session: `authRepo.logoutParent()` (revokes refresh token) + `tokenStore.clear()` (session) + `tokenStore.clearSchool()` (school code + cached branding) + reset `feeViewModel`/`isLoggedIn`/`userType` + `needsSchool = true` → routes back to `SchoolCodeScreen`. No confirmation dialog (per decision). Contrast with login-screen "Change School" which runs pre-login (no session) and only does `clearSchool()` + `needsSchool=true`.

(2) **Wrong-logo fix:** the generic `ascent` flavor (applicationId `in.educare.app`, SCHOOL_CODE blank) inherits icons from `src/main`, and `src/main` still held DePaul's "OPES" launcher icons (`ic_launcher*.webp` + adaptive foreground) — so the generic "Ascent Schools" app showed the DePaul logo on the home screen AND on `SmsAuthScreen` (baked flavors use `getApplicationIcon()` as the login logo when no API branding is loaded yet). The Demo flavor was likewise wrongly baked with St. Ann's "Light of Life" logo. The 3 published flavors (stannsasf, depaulemyv, holyspiritjm) keep their own correct logos untouched. **Local debugging note:** debug build's `API_BASE_URL = http://localhost:62845/`; on a physical device `localhost`/`127.0.0.1` is the phone itself, so run `adb reverse tcp:62845 tcp:62845` (cleared on unplug/reboot/adb restart — re-run each session) to tunnel to the host's IIS Express. Hitting `http://localhost:62845/` in a browser returns 403 Forbidden — that's normal (no route at `/`); test a real endpoint like `/branding` instead. Placeholder icon only — replace via Android Studio Image Asset (target `main`) when a real Ascent logo exists. No gradlew wrapper in repo; build with Studio JDK + the cached Gradle 8.7 dist, or from Android Studio (`Ctrl+F9` Make, `Shift+F10` Run; pick variant in Build Variants panel).

the legacy insert also fills `ClassNam` (from receipt API ClassName) and resolves `FeeMasterID`/`FeeID` via inline subqueries (FeeMasterID ← `SAS_FeeMaster` by ClassNam+NofPayments[=term]+FeeTyp+AcdYear, FeeMasterStat='A'; FeeID ← `SAS_FeeTypes.FeeTypID` by FeeTyp, FeeTypStatus='A'); files: `MainForm.cs`, `SyncTab.cs`, `Config/AppSettings.cs`, `Api/AscentApiClient.cs`, `Data/LegacyDb.cs`, `Models/` (standalone copied POCOs), `Mappers/Mappers.cs` (legacy↔bulk; receipt-detail item→legacy); **backend changes:** (1) `BulkStudentImportRequest.Upsert` (default false — web UI keeps strict insert-only) + `BulkImportResult.Updated` counter; `StudentRepository.BulkCreate` upsert path matches existing rows by `admission_no + academic_year_id + school_id`, UPDATEs personal/contact fields only (student_name, gender, date_of_birth, father/mother name, father/mother mobile, aadhar_no, caste, caste_code, religion, mother_tongue — NOT class/section/roll/fee_category/status/academic_year which are promotion/lifecycle-managed), inserts otherwise reusing the student's existing student_unique_id (subquery: latest-year unique_id for that admission_no, else MAX+1) so cross-year linkage holds; no DB column needed; `MasterDataController.GetAcademicYears` accepts `[FromUri] bool activeOnly = false`; all 24 frontend pages that use academic year dropdowns updated: call `?activeOnly=true` and auto-select the year with `isCurrent === true` on mount; exam-related pages (`ExamHallTicketReport`, `ExamToppersReport`, `FailedStudentsReport`) and `FeePeriodsTab` call their `onYearChange` handler on auto-select so dependent dropdowns (exam types, fee periods) populate automatically;
