Android mobile unchanged (no year picker UI); `StudentsPage` year dropdown has no "All Years" option (removed — current year is always pre-selected); fee collection student search (`FeeCollectionBase.jsx`) passes `?latestOnly=true` to `GET /school/students` so multi-year students appear only once (latest academic year row returned via ROW_NUMBER partition); dashboard `TotalActiveStudents` query scoped to current academic year via subquery `SELECT TOP 1 academic_year_id FROM academic_years WHERE status='Active' ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC` (same Option A logic — counts only active students in the most recently created Active year); `FeeRepository.GetCrossYearFeeSummary` student-rows query extended with `AND ISNULL(ay.status, 'Active') = 'Active'` — mobile and parent portal outstanding now only shows Active academic years (Inactive years silently excluded; `ISNULL` guard retains rows with no matching academic year record)
```
Phase 37 ✅  VB6 legacy data migration tool — `AscentMigration/` standalone .NET 4.8 console app that reads from a legacy VB6 SQL Server DB (source) and writes into an `ascent_group_{N}` tenant DB (dest); configured entirely via `App.config` (Source/Dest connection strings, SchoolId, DefaultAcademicYearId, Modules=all|comma-list, DryRun, BatchSize, Cleanup, per-table mode and force flags); `DefaultAcademicYearId` is the fallback academic_year_id for migrators whose legacy source has no academic-year column (fee_categories, fee_types — used unconditionally) or whose AcdYear lookup misses (terms, fee_structures — used as fallback); `0` (default) = AUTO-resolve at runtime via `BaseMigrator.ResolveDefaultAcademicYearIdAsync` (`SELECT TOP 1 academic_year_id FROM academic_years WHERE school_id ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC` — the latest year AcademicYearsMigrator just inserted), `>0` = manual override; auto-resolve means a `Cleanup=true` re-migration that re-inserts academic_years with new IDENTITY values picks up the correct new ID without editing App.config; `MigrationRunner` runs migrators in FK-dependency order, auto-creates `_migration_log` table in dest, is fully idempotent (skips already-completed migrators, re-runs if `{name}.force=true` in App.config); each migrator inherits `BaseMigrator` (OpenSource/OpenDest, Log, LogProgress); two modes per table: `Truncate` (delete existing school rows then re-insert all) or `Skip` (composite-key dedup — skip rows already present); 14 migrators in order: `AcademicYearsMigrator` (SAS_AcdYear → academic_years), `ClassGroupsMigrator` (SAS_ClassGrps → class_groups), `FeeCategoriesMigrator` (SAS_FeeCategory → fee_categories), `ClassesMigrator` (SAS_ClassNames → classes; maps class_group via SAS_ClassGrps prefix lookup), `FeeTypesMigrator` (SAS_FeeTypes → fee_types), `TermsMigrator` (SAS_TermMonthData → terms; composite key `term_name|academic_year_id`), `FeeStructuresMigrator` (SAS_FeeMaster → fee_structures; two-step category lookup: FeaCatgID→name via SAS_FeeCategory, name→fee_category_id via dest; term key `"termName|academicYearId"`; maps status A→Active/D→Inactive), `BusRoutesMigrator` (SAS_BusRoutes → bus_routes; skip key: route_name; status A→Active/D→Inactive), `BusFeeStructuresMigrator` (SAS_BusMaster → bus_fee_structures; two-step route lookup: legacy RouteID→RouteNam via SAS_BusRoutes, RouteNam→route_id via dest bus_routes with Active-wins-on-duplicate; term key `"termName|academicYearId"`; skip key: route_id|term_id|academic_year_id), `SectionsMigrator` (no legacy sections table — reads `SELECT DISTINCT StuSect FROM SAS_StudentMaster`; inserts one row per section × per class; sequence_no resets 1..n per class; skip key: class_id|section_name), `StudentsMigrator` (SAS_StudentMaster → students; 5 preloaded lookup maps: acadYearMap, classMap, sectionMap `"section_name|class_id"`, legacyRouteNameMap RouteID→RouteNam, destRouteIdMap route_name→route_id Active-wins; ~50 mapped columns; StuFamilyChildres varchar→int via TryParse; StuUnqID float→int cast; status A→Active/D→Inactive/TC→TC/R→R/else→NULL; student_name NOT NULL — row skipped if blank; is_detained defaults to 0; StuMobile1/StuMobile2 may contain multiple numbers separated by `/` or `,` — `TakeFirstMobile` splits and takes first token capped at 10 digits for father_mobile/mother_mobile; full raw StuMobile1→spare_field_1, StuMobile2→spare_field_2; skip key: admission_no|academic_year_id), `BusesMigrator` (SAS_BussData → buses; no FK deps; BusCapacity varchar→int TryParse; skip key: bus_name), `PaymentModesMigrator` (SAS_PaymentMods → payment_modes; is_online=0 for legacy rows; appends fixed Online row mode_name='Online'/is_online=1 after legacy rows; skip key: mode_name), `FeeReceiptsMigrator` (SAS_FeeReceipts → fee_receipts + fee_receipt_items; academic year scope controlled by `fee_receipts.academicYear` App.config key: `latest`/omitted=`SELECT TOP 1 AcademicYear FROM SAS_AcdYear ORDER BY AcademicYear DESC`, `all`=no filter, or a specific year string e.g. `"2024-25"`; groups filtered rows by FeeReciptID in memory ordered by Id_new; header fields from first row, total_amount=SUM(FeeAmt) across group; 5 lookup maps: acadYearMap, studentMap `"admission_no|academic_year_id"` → StudentId+StudentUniqueId, paymentModeMap, feeTypeMap, termMap; each receipt group runs in a single transaction with rollback on error; fee_receipt_items: fee_type_id from FeeTyp, term_id from PaymentTyp+AcdYear composite key, amount=net_amount=FeeAmt, concession_amount=0; Truncate deletes items via JOIN before receipts; skip key: receipt_no); `MigrationLogger` tracks completion per migrator name + timestamps (+ `ClearAllAsync` to wipe the log); **Cleanup phase** — `<add key="Cleanup" value="true" />` in App.config triggers `MigrationRunner.CleanupAsync` before migration: deletes all migrated tables for the configured `SchoolId` in REVERSE FK-dependency order (children first: fee_receipt_items → fee_receipts → students → sections → bus_fee_structures → fee_structures → buses → bus_routes → terms → fee_types → classes → fee_categories → class_groups → academic_years → payment_modes), then clears `_migration_log` so every migrator re-runs; respects `DryRun` (prints planned deletes without executing); aborts loudly if a non-migrated table (e.g. student_attendance/marks from real usage) still references a row via FK — signals the school has live data and must not be wiped; solves the "re-migration fails on FK constraints" problem (each migrator's own forward-order Truncate can't delete a parent while children still reference it); `Database/cleanup_migration_data.sql` — manual SSMS alternative: same reverse-order deletes wrapped in `SET XACT_ABORT ON` + transaction (all-or-nothing rollback), per-table `@@ROWCOUNT` prints, single `@SchoolId` variable; `GET /school/master/terms` extended with optional `?academicYearId` filter (needed for term lookup accuracy); `Database/legacy_classdata_migration.sql` — SQL script to seed class groups (Pre-Primary/Primary/High School/General with IDENTITY_INSERT for predictable IDs) and 26 classes for a specific school (one-time manual alternative to running ClassesMigrator)
```

---

## Migration Tool Structure
```
AscentMigration/
├── AscentMigration.sln / .csproj   ← .NET 4.8 console app; Dapper + System.Data.SqlClient
├── Program.cs                       ← entry point; loads config, prompts Enter, runs MigrationRunner
├── MigrationRunner.cs               ← runs migrators in FK-dependency order; auto-creates _migration_log; idempotent (skip already-done, force re-run via App.config flag)
├── MigrationLogger.cs               ← async; EnsureLogTable / IsCompleted / WriteAsync / ClearAsync / PrintLog against dest DB
├── MigrationResult.cs               ← Total / Migrated / Skipped / ErrorCount / Errors list; Print() method
├── Config/
│   ├── MigrationConfig.cs           ← loads App.config: Source/Dest CS, SchoolId, DefaultAcademicYearId, Modules, DryRun, BatchSize; ShouldRun/IsForced/GetTableMode/GetOptionalSetting helpers
│   └── MigrationMode.cs             ← enum Truncate | Skip
├── Models/Legacy/                   ← POCO classes matching VB6 DB columns (LegacyAcademicYear, LegacyClass, LegacyClassGroup, LegacyFeeCategory, LegacyFeeMaster, LegacyFeeType, LegacyTerm, LegacyBusRoute, LegacyBusMaster, LegacyStudent, LegacyBus, LegacyPaymentMode, LegacyFeeReceipt, LegacyExamType, LegacyMarksGrade, LegacyExamMaster)
└── Migrators/
    ├── IMigrator.cs                  ← interface: Name + RunAsync()
    ├── BaseMigrator.cs               ← abstract; OpenSource/OpenDest; Log/LogProgress helpers
    ├── AcademicYearsMigrator.cs      ← SAS_AcdYear → academic_years (no FK deps — runs first)
    ├── ClassGroupsMigrator.cs        ← SAS_ClassGrps → class_groups (no FK deps)
    ├── FeeCategoriesMigrator.cs      ← SAS_FeeCategory → fee_categories
    ├── ClassesMigrator.cs            ← SAS_ClassNames → classes (depends on class_groups)
    ├── FeeTypesMigrator.cs           ← SAS_FeeTypes → fee_types (depends on academic_years)
    ├── TermsMigrator.cs              ← SAS_TermMonthData → terms (depends on academic_years; composite key term_name|academic_year_id)
    ├── ExamTypesMigrator.cs          ← SAS_ExamNam → exam_types (depends on academic_years; runs after SubjectsMigrator); DISTINCT ExamNam/acdyear/examremarks/examstatus; ExamNam→exam_type_name, acdyear lookup (fallback default year)→academic_year_id, examremarks→display_order (int.TryParse, NULL if bad), examstatus A→Active/D→Inactive/else→Active; composite skip key exam_type_name|academic_year_id (per-year dedup)
    ├── GradeTypesMigrator.cs         ← SAS_MarksGrade → grade_types (depends on subjects; runs after ExamTypesMigrator); two-step subject lookup (legacy SubjID→SAS_Subjects.SubjectNam→dest subjects.subject_id, latest-year on name collision, NULL on miss); GradName→grade_name, MaxMrks/MinMrks→max_marks/min_marks, Grad→grade, Rrmrks→remarks, TraStatus A→Active/D→Inactive/else→Active; composite skip key grade_name|subject_id|grade|min_marks|max_marks
    ├── ExamMasterMigrator.cs         ← SAS_ExamNam (full rows) → exam_master (depends on exam_types, classes, academic_years, subjects, grade_types; runs after GradeTypesMigrator); lookups: ExamNam→exam_type_id (name|year), ClasId→class_id (2-step via SAS_ClassNames), AcdYear→academic_year_id, SubjectID→subject_id (2-step via SAS_Subjects, name|year), GradeTyp→grade_type_id (MrksGradId→GradName, matched grade_name|subject_name — grade_types has no class_id); marks/remarks/category direct, ExamDatTim→exam_date, ExamStatus A→Active/D→Inactive; NULL+error on any lookup miss; composite skip key exam_type_id|class_id|subject_id|academic_year_id|exam_category
    ├── FeePeriodsMigrator.cs         ← SAS_TermMonthData (Descrpt='Monthly') → fee_periods (depends on academic_years; runs after TermsMigrator, before FeeStructuresMigrator); TrmMnthData→period_label, month extracted from label ("October-2026"→10) →month_no, YearNam (fallback label year)→year_no, OrdrNo→sequence_no, TraStatus A→Active/D→Deleted→status, AcdYear lookup (fallback default year)→academic_year_id; composite skip key period_label|academic_year_id
    ├── FeeStructuresMigrator.cs      ← SAS_FeeMaster → fee_structures (all deps); two-step category lookup; status A→Active/D→Inactive
    ├── BusRoutesMigrator.cs          ← SAS_BusRoutes → bus_routes (no FK deps); skip key: route_name; status A→Active/D→Inactive
    ├── BusFeeStructuresMigrator.cs   ← SAS_BusMaster → bus_fee_structures (depends on bus_routes, academic_years, terms); two-step route lookup (RouteID→RouteNam via source, RouteNam→route_id via dest, Active wins on dup); skip key: route_id|term_id|academic_year_id
    ├── SectionsMigrator.cs           ← no legacy sections table; reads DISTINCT StuSect from SAS_StudentMaster; inserts section × class cross-product; sequence_no resets 1..n per class; skip key: class_id|section_name
    ├── StudentsMigrator.cs           ← SAS_StudentMaster → students (depends on academic_years, classes, sections, bus_routes); 5 lookup maps; ~50 mapped columns; StuFamilyChildres varchar→int TryParse; StuUnqID float→int; status A→Active/D→Inactive/TC→TC/R→R/else→NULL; is_detained=0; TakeFirstMobile splits StuMobile1/2 on '/',',' caps at 10 digits for father_mobile/mother_mobile; full raw stored in spare_field_1/2; skip key: admission_no|academic_year_id
    ├── BusesMigrator.cs              ← SAS_BussData → buses (no FK deps); BusCapacity varchar→int TryParse; skip key: bus_name
    ├── PaymentModesMigrator.cs       ← SAS_PaymentMods → payment_modes (no FK deps); is_online=0 for legacy rows; appends fixed Online row (is_online=1) after legacy rows; skip key: mode_name
    └── FeeReceiptsMigrator.cs        ← SAS_FeeReceipts → fee_receipts + fee_receipt_items; year scope via `fee_receipts.academicYear` App.config key (latest/all/specific year); latest resolved via `SELECT TOP 1 AcademicYear FROM SAS_AcdYear ORDER BY AcademicYear DESC`; per-receipt transaction; skip key: receipt_no
```

## Sync Tool Structure (Phase 41 — ongoing EOD sync GUI)
```
AscentSyncTool/                 (.NET 4.8 WinForms; Dapper + System.Data.SqlClient + Newtonsoft.Json; net48 needs explicit <Reference Include="System.Net.Http" />)
├── App.config                  LegacyDb connection string + ApiBaseUrl + ApiKey + ReceiptBatchSize/StudentBatchSize
├── Program.cs                  STAThread Application.Run(new MainForm())
├── MainForm.cs                 `const string Version` (title bar + big navy header label); TabControl (4 tabs); enables ShowAcademicYearFilter on the 2 legacy tabs + UseMonthYearFilter on Download Attendance; Load → GetAcademicYears() populates both (latest pre-selected, try/catch); wires each SyncTab's OnShow/OnShowPeriod/OnSave (legacy OnShow passes SelectedAcademicYear); batching + result dialogs
├── SyncTab.cs                  reusable UserControl: From/To DateTimePickers, optional Academic Year combo (ShowAcademicYearFilter / SelectedAcademicYear / SetAcademicYears), optional Month+Year combos instead of From/To (UseMonthYearFilter / SelectedMonth / SelectedYear → routes Show to OnShowPeriod), Show/Save/Cancel, read-only DataGridView, status label (code-only, no designer)
├── Config/AppSettings.cs       reads App.config (LegacyConnectionString, ApiBaseUrl, ApiKey, batch sizes)
├── Api/AscentApiClient.cs      HttpClient + X-Api-Key; GetWebappReceipts (createdAfter/createdBefore + source=webapp), GetReceiptDetail, GetAttendanceSummary(month,year), BulkImportReceipts, BulkImportStudents (Upsert=true)
├── Data/LegacyDb.cs            Dapper on legacy SAS DB: GetAcademicYears (DISTINCT SAS_AcdYear.AcademicYear, DESC), GetReceipts(FeeDat range, +AcdYear), GetStudents(CrtDat range, +StuAcdYear), ReceiptExists(FeeReciptID), InsertReceipt, GetBranchId (SAS_LicenceDet), SaveBulkAttendance (supersede-then-insert into SAS_BulkAttendance)
├── Models/                     standalone copied POCOs — LegacyModels.cs (LegacyFeeReceipt, LegacyStudent), ApiModels.cs (BulkReceiptRow, StudentBulkRow, BulkImportResult, ReceiptListItem, ReceiptDetail, AttendanceExportRow, ApiResponse<T>)
└── Mappers/Mappers.cs          ToBulkReceipt (legacy→export), ToBulkStudent (legacy→export, FirstMobile splits '/'',' caps 10), ToLegacyReceipt (api receipt-detail item→legacy row)
```
> **Tabs:** 1. Export Receipts (legacy `SAS_FeeReceipts` by FeeDat **+ academic year filter** → `POST /school/fees/receipts/bulk`), 2. Export Students (legacy `SAS_StudentMaster` by CrtDat **+ academic year filter** → `POST /school/students/bulk` Upsert), 3. Download Receipts (`GET /school/fees/receipts?source=webapp` by created_at → per-receipt detail → insert one legacy row per fee-type item, dedup by FeeReciptID; legacy insert fills ClassNam + resolves FeeMasterID/FeeID via SAS_FeeMaster/SAS_FeeTypes name lookups; **no academic-year filter — new-app API, not legacy**), 4. Download Attendance (**Month + Year dropdowns instead of From/To**; `GET /school/attendance/monthly-export?month=&year=` → one row per admission no + academic year → `SAS_BulkAttendance`; re-run supersedes rather than deletes — Phase 109). The academic-year dropdown (Export tabs only) loads DISTINCT `SAS_AcdYear.AcademicYear` on startup, latest selected by default. Set `LegacyDb` connection string + `ApiKey` in App.config before first run.

---

## API Project Structure (current)
```
AscentSchools.sln
├── AscentSchools.API          ← Web API project (.NET 4.8)
│   ├── App_Start/
│   │   └── WebApiConfig.cs    ← CORS, JSON formatting, global filters, routes
│   ├── Controllers/
│   │   ├── BaseApiController.cs           ← response helpers only (no auth)
│   │   ├── Control/
│   │   │   ├── BaseControlController.cs   ← [ControlAuth], response helpers
│   │   │   ├── ControlAuthController.cs   ← POST /control/auth/login|refresh|logout
│   │   │   ├── SchoolGroupsController.cs  ← CRUD + school CRUD + user management; invalidates TenantConnectionFactory credential cache on group update
│   │   │   ├── SubscriptionsController.cs ← GET/PUT /control/school-groups/{id}/subscription
│   │   │   ├── ModulesController.cs       ← GET modules, GET/PUT group modules
│   │   │   ├── BrandingController.cs      ← GET/PUT /control/school-groups/{id}/branding
│   │   │   └── ControlSupportTicketsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("control/support-tickets")] — cross-group list + detail + status update (Phase 132; named Control* to stay unique from the school-side SupportTicketsController — Phase 87 trap)
│   │   └── School/
│   │       ├── BaseSchoolController.cs    ← [SchoolAuth], response helpers, Tenant property
│   │       ├── PublicController.cs        ← GET /branding (AllowAnonymous)
│   │       ├── SchoolAuthController.cs    ← POST /school/auth/login|refresh|logout
│   │       ├── RolesController.cs         ← CRUD roles + GET/PUT permissions per role
│   │       ├── SchoolUsersController.cs   ← CRUD users + reset-password + status
│   │       ├── MasterDataController.cs    ← [RoutePrefix("school/master")] — 10 entities (incl. sections + fee periods), 3 endpoints each
│   │       ├── StudentsController.cs      ← GET list (?bloodGroup, ?joinType, ?busRouteId, ?hostelId filters), GET by id, POST create, PUT update, POST /{id}/photo, POST /bulk, GET /promote/preview, POST /promote, PUT /{id}/block|unblock|detain|release
│   │       ├── FeeController.cs           ← [RoutePrefix("school/fees")] — structure, collect, receipts (GET ?createdAfter for sync), POST structure/bulk, POST receipts/bulk (legacy import), gateway settings, payment orders + webhook; pending-order reconciliation (GET payment-orders/pending|{id}/status|scan, POST {id}/reconcile); shared ReconcileOrderToReceipt + EvaluateStatus
│   │       ├── FeeConcessionController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/fees/concessions")] — GET list, POST individual (upsert), POST /bulk, DELETE /{id} (soft-cancel); validation: FeeTypeId==null && BusRouteId==null && HostelId==null → 400
│   │       ├── HostelController.cs        ← [RoutePrefix("school/hostel")] — GET/POST hostels, GET/POST fee-structure, POST fee-structure/bulk, GET/PUT students
│   │       ├── AttendanceController.cs    ← [RoutePrefix("school/attendance")] — mark by date + monthly summary + monthly-export (school-wide present-day counts for the sync tool); classId+sectionId both required on the summary
│   │       ├── MarksController.cs         ← [RoutePrefix("school/marks")] — exam types + marks grid (class_subjects-driven, per-subject max/activity from exam_master, stores exam_id) + save
│   │       ├── ClassSubjectsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/class-subjects")] — GET mapping, available-subjects (ALL active subjects, NOT year-filtered), for-class (no year filter — exam setup), POST bulk-replace (Phase 106)
│   │       ├── ExamMasterController.cs    ← [RoutePrefix("school/exam-master")] — per-class/subject exam definitions; GET, POST (one row per subject, INSERT-if-not-exists), POST /bulk (CSV, names→ids, skips existing), PUT/{id}, DELETE/{id} (Phase 106)
│   │       ├── MarksGradeMasterController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/marks-grades")] — standalone grading scales for the TOTAL: GET list (band + exam counts), POST create, PUT/{id} rename, DELETE/{id} (refused while exams use it), GET/PUT {id}/bands (replace); validates grade/min≤max/no duplicate grade/no overlap (Phase 117→119)
│   │       ├── MarksCardController.cs     ← [RoutePrefix("school/marks-card")] — GET marks cards for year+exam+class+section (Phase 118)
│   │       ├── GradeTypesController.cs    ← [RoutePrefix("school/grade-types")] — grade-band CRUD + POST /bulk (CSV import) (single source; feeds the exam grade dropdown) (Phase 106)
│   │       ├── HomeworkController.cs      ← [RoutePrefix("school/homework")] — server-paged list (classId/sectionId/assignedDate/page/pageSize), create, POST batch (daily multi-subject), update, delete
│   │       ├── AnnouncementsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/announcements")] — list, create, update, delete
│   │       ├── CalendarController.cs      ← [RoutePrefix("school/calendar")] — school activity calendar events CRUD (Holiday/Exam/Celebration/Event, date range); read via GET /mobile/student/calendar
│   │       ├── ClassTeachersController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/class-teachers")] — list/assign/unassign class teachers (Master Data); drives parent→teacher message routing
│   │       ├── MessageReportsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/message-reports")] — admin review of reported messages (SETTINGS.MANAGE); resolve = Removed/Reviewed
│   │       ├── SchoolMessagesAdminController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/messages")] — READ-ONLY admin conversation viewer; [RequirePermission(MESSAGES.VIEW)] (first real backend perm enforcement); GET threads (filters: academicYear/class/section/studentUnique/teacher/date, default last 5 days) + GET threads/{id} (Phase 103)
│   │       ├── ReportsController.cs       ← [RoutePrefix("school/reports")] — class-students, total-strength, absents, attendance-sheet, transport-students, study-certificate, daily-attendance-summary, monthly-attendance-sheet, attendance-register, exam-hall-ticket, toppers, failed-students, homework, staff-salary-statement, detained-students, regular-absentees
│   │       ├── SmsController.cs           ← [RoutePrefix("school/sms")] — GET recipients (by type), POST send (per-school account+template from sms_configs/sms_templates; parallel dispatch via Task.WhenAll), GET logs, GET/PUT config, POST/DELETE templates
│   │       ├── SchoolSettingsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/settings")] — GET (returns row or empty defaults), PUT (upserts via SchoolSettingsRepository using master DB)
│   │       └── SupportTicketsController.cs ← [RoutePrefix("school/support-tickets")] — branch-wide ticket list/detail/create, master-DB-backed via SupportTicketRepository, no permission gate (Phase 132)
│   │   └── Mobile/
│   │       ├── MobileAuthController.cs        ← student auth (backend-only) + parent request-otp (gates on student in school DB by father_mobile)/verify-otp (auto-creates parent_account + upserts child links by admission_no — updates class_name/student_id on existing links)/refresh/logout/children (scoped to current school group)/select-child (validates link.GroupId == tenant.GroupId)/link-child
│   │       ├── MobileStudentController.cs     ← profile, attendance, marks, homework, announcements (student + parent JWT)
│   │       ├── MobileFeesController.cs        ← [MobileAuth(requireChildContext:true)] GET /mobile/fees/outstanding (cross-year by category), GET /mobile/fees/gateway-config, POST /mobile/fees/payment-orders (accepts MobileParentOrderRequest; looks up student_unique_id + year student_id + online payment mode id server-side), POST /mobile/fees/payment-orders/{id}/verify (HMAC verify + receipt creation)
│   │       ├── MobileTeacherAuthController.cs ← POST /mobile/auth/teacher/login|refresh|logout (tokenType=teacher)
│   │       ├── MobileTeacherController.cs     ← [MobileTeacherAuth] GET classes, GET sections, GET/POST attendance, GET homework (legacy list) + GET homework/paged (Phase 104), POST homework (optional SectionId), GET/POST announcements, marks (Phase 106: GET exam-types, GET marks-subjects, GET marks [one subject], POST marks — year resolved server-side)
│   │       ├── MobileMessagesController.cs    ← [MobileAuth(requireChildContext:true)] parent messaging (mobile/messages) — GET chat view, POST send, read, report, block, unblock
│   │       ├── MobileTeacherMessagesController.cs ← [MobileTeacherAuth] teacher messaging (mobile/teacher/messages) — thread list, GET/POST reply/read/report/block/unblock per {threadId}
│   │       ├── MobileNotificationsController.cs ← register/unregister FCM device token (parent or teacher; reads MobileContext/TeacherContext)
│   │       └── MobileAppConfigController.cs   ← [AllowAnonymous] GET /mobile/app/config — force/soft update gate (auto-prompt gated by auto_update_enabled)
│   ├── Filters/
│   │   ├── ControlAuthAttribute.cs        ← validates tokenType=control, optional role check
│   │   ├── SchoolAuthAttribute.cs         ← validates TenantContext populated (school JWT)
│   │   ├── MobileAuthAttribute.cs         ← validates MobileContext populated; requireChildContext flag for data endpoints
│   │   ├── MobileTeacherAuthAttribute.cs  ← validates TeacherContext populated (tokenType=teacher)
│   │   ├── JwtAuthenticationFilter.cs     ← global filter, validates tokens, populates TenantContext/MobileContext/TeacherContext
│   │   ├── RequireModuleAttribute.cs      ← checks group_modules + school_modules hierarchy → 403
│   │   └── RequirePermissionAttribute.cs  ← checks permissions[] in TenantContext → 403
│   ├── Helpers/

│   │   └── SmsHelper.cs                   ← SendOtp + SendSms (two overloads: global account for OTP; per-account for SMS Center). School SMS templates/keys now live in DB (sms_configs/sms_templates), not constants. OTP stays on the hardcoded global account.

StudentUniqueId + JoinType) + BulkImportDtos.cs (StudentBulkRow, FeeStructureBulkRow, BulkStudentImportRequest, BulkFeeStructureImportRequest, BulkImportResult, BulkRowError, PromoteStudentsRequest, PromoteStudentsResult, PromotePreviewDto)

        └── School/
            ├── SchoolAuthRepository.cs            ← GetById, GetByUsername, GetUserSchoolIds, GetUserPermissions, UpdateLastLogin, Create/Get/RevokeRefreshToken
            ├── SchoolBrandingRepository.cs        ← GetBySubdomain (school_branding → group_branding fallback)
            ├── SchoolRbacRepository.cs            ← GetRoles, CreateRole, UpdateRole, GetAllPermissions, GetRolePermissionIds, SetRolePermissions
            ├── MasterDataRepository.cs            ← Get/Create/Update for all 10 master tables (academic_years, class_groups, fee_categories, classes, sections, fee_types, terms, subjects, payment_modes, fee_periods)
            ├── StudentRepository.cs               ← GetAll (filtered, incl. bloodGroup, joinType, busRouteId, hostelId; SELECT includes student_unique_id + join_type + hostel_id), GetById, Create (auto-assigns student_unique_id via MAX+1), Update, UpdatePhotoPath, BulkCreate, GetForPromotion (status IN 'Active','Y', excludes detained), CountDetained, Promote, BlockStudent, UnblockStudent, DetainStudent, ReleaseStudent
            ├── FeeRepository.cs                   ← GetFeeStructure (incl. fee_period_id/payment_type), SaveFeeStructure (scoped by admission_type slice), GetStudentFeeSummary, GetCrossYearFeeSummary (by student_unique_id; transport branch reads bus_fee_structures; hostel branch reads hostel_fee_structures via GetHostelLineItems; outstanding = structure - paid - concession); CollectFee (stores bus_route_id + hostel_id in receipt items), GetReceipts (?createdAfter for end-of-day sync), GetReceiptById (JOINs bus_routes + hostels for display names), CancelReceipt, BulkSaveFeeStructure, BulkImportReceipts (groups by LegacyReceiptNo or AdmissionNo+date+mode; name-based lookups; per-receipt transaction)
            ├── FeeConcessionRepository.cs          ← GetConcessions (JOINs fee_types+bus_routes+hostels+terms+fee_periods; returns BusRouteId/RouteName/HostelId/HostelName), SaveConcession (upsert by student+fee_type+bus_route+hostel+term/period match; INSERT includes bus_route_id + hostel_id), BulkSaveConcessions (same), DeleteConcession (soft-cancel status→Cancelled)
            ├── HostelRepository.cs                 ← GetAll, Create, Update; GetFeeStructure/SaveFeeStructure (Term/Monthly, delete+insert per hostel+year); BulkSaveFeeStructure; GetStudentHostels (filters: academicYearId, classId, sectionId, hostelId), UpdateStudentHostel (WHERE student_unique_id + academic_year_id)
            ├── MarksRepository.cs                 ← GetExamTypes, CreateExamType, UpdateExamType, GetMarksGrid, SaveMarks
            ├── HomeworkRepository.cs              ← GetHomework (teacher mobile legacy — unpaged by class), GetHomeworkPaged (web + teacher mobile /paged — classId/sectionId/assignedDate filters + OFFSET/FETCH, returns (items,total); `includeClassWide` param [Phase 104] → when set with a sectionId, matches that section + class-wide (section_id NULL); two queries not QueryMultiple — GridReader IAsyncDisposable won't compile on net48), CreateHomework, CreateBatchHomework (daily multi-subject — resolves target sections [all active → one class-wide NULL row set], soft-cancels the day's rows for those sections **plus every class-wide row**, then inserts one row per subject per section, in a transaction; returns BatchHomeworkResult — Phase 108), UpdateHomework, DeleteHomework
            ├── AnnouncementsRepository.cs         ← GetAnnouncements, CreateAnnouncement, UpdateAnnouncement, DeleteAnnouncement
            ├── ClassTeacherRepository.cs          ← GetAssignments/GetAssignableTeachers/AssignmentExists/CreateAssignment/DeleteAssignment; routing: GetTeachersForClass (current-year class+section → assigned teachers), GetAssignmentsForTeacher
            ├── MessagingRepository.cs             ← parent↔teacher threads (key student_unique_id) + messages + reports; GetStudentClassContext, FindThreadId/GetOrCreateThread, GetThreadsForTeacher, TeacherCanAccessThread, SendMessage/MarkRead, SetThreadBlocked/GetThreadBlocker, ReportMessage/GetReports/ResolveReport/CountOpenReports; GetThreadsForAdmin (read-only web viewer — student placement in selected year, teacher-name concat, date-window filter — Phase 103)
            ├── ReportsRepository.cs               ← GetClassStudents, GetTotalStrength, GetAbsents, GetAttendanceSheet, GetTransportStudents, GetStudyCertificate, GetDailyAttendanceSummary, GetMonthlyAttendanceSheet, GetAttendanceRegister, GetExamHallTicket, GetExamToppers, GetClassToppers, GetFailedStudents, GetAcademicYearToppers, GetHomeworkStatement, GetSubjectHomework, GetStaffSalaryStatement, GetDetainedStudents, GetRegularAbsentees
            ├── TransportRepository.cs             ← GetBuses/CreateBus/UpdateBus, GetRoutes/CreateRoute/UpdateRoute, GetBusFeeStructure/SaveBusFeeStructure, GetStudentTransport (filters: routeId, academicYearId, classId, sectionId; SELECT includes student_unique_id, academic_year_id; IN ('Active','Y')), UpdateStudentTransport (WHERE student_unique_id + academic_year_id — stable cross-year, not student_id)
            ├── HostelRepository.cs                ← GetAll/Create/Update hostels; GetFeeStructure/SaveFeeStructure (Term/Monthly, delete+insert per hostel+year); BulkSaveFeeStructure; GetStudentHostels (filters: academicYearId, classId, sectionId, hostelId; IN ('Active','Y')), UpdateStudentHostel (WHERE student_unique_id + academic_year_id)
            ├── SmsRepository.cs                   ← GetAbsentRecipients, GetFeeDueRecipients (OUTER APPLY for outstanding), GetCustomRecipients, LogSms, GetLogs; per-school SMS config: GetSmsAccount (incl. api_key, send path), GetSmsConfig (HasApiKey flag + templates), UpsertSmsConfig (blank key keeps existing), GetTemplates/GetTemplate/UpsertTemplate/DeleteTemplate
            └── SchoolSettingsRepository.cs         ← Get (SELECT from master DB school_settings by schoolId; returns empty DTO if no row), Upsert (IF EXISTS UPDATE ELSE INSERT; writes master DB)
        └── Mobile/
            ├── MobileStudentAuthRepository.cs     ← student_mobile_accounts + student_refresh_tokens CRUD; GetStudentByAdmissionNo uses ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC; GetAccountByAdmissionNo fallback for post-promotion login; UpdateAccountStudentId re-points account after promotion; GetStudentsByParentMobile (looks up active students by father_mobile, latest year per student)
            ├── MobileParentAuthRepository.cs      ← parent_accounts + parent_children + parent_refresh_tokens + parent_otp (master DB); CreateOtp, GetValidOtp, MarkOtpUsed, GetRecentOtpCount, BindDevice; GetOrCreateParentByMobile (auto-creates OTP-only account on first login); GetChildLinkByAdmissionNo (lookup by admission_no+group_id for upsert); UpdateChildLink (refreshes student_id/student_name/class_name on existing link); GetChildren now filtered by group_id
            ├── MobileDataRepository.cs            ← GetStudentProfile, GetAttendance, GetMarks, GetHomework (TOP 20, assigned_date DESC), GetAnnouncements
            ├── DevicePushTokenRepository.cs       ← master device_push_tokens; UpsertParent/TeacherToken (stores current school's group/school/db per token), Delete; target resolution is SCHOOL-SCOPED (Phase 100 — filters the token join by dpt.db_name+group_id+school_id so a shared master parent_id doesn't leak a push to an app logged into another school): GetParentTokensForStudents(dbName,groupId,schoolId,ids)/GetSchoolParentTokens(dbName,groupId,schoolId)/GetTokensForParent(dbName,groupId,schoolId,parentId)/GetTokensForTeachers
            └── AppConfigRepository.cs             ← GetConfig (master DB app_config; exact applicationId row, falls back to '*'; includes auto_update_enabled)
```

## School App API Endpoints (Phase 2a + 2b + 3)

| Method | URL | Auth | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET  | `/branding` | Public | X-Subdomain header + optional ?schoolId |
| GET  | `/version` | Public | API assembly version + deploy date (bin DLL write time); shown in the school-app footer |
| GET  | `/school/settings/r2` | School JWT | R2 storage config (secret omitted; hasSecretKey flag) |
| PUT  | `/school/settings/r2` | School JWT | Upsert R2 storage config (blank secret keeps existing) |
| POST | `/school/uploads/presign` | School JWT | Presigned R2 PUT URL + public URL (purpose: student-photo/homework/announcement/event) |
| POST | `/school/media/attach` | School JWT | Record an uploaded R2 file for an entity (media_uploads) |
| GET  | `/school/media` | School JWT | List a homework/announcement/event's uploaded files (?entityType=&entityId=) |
| DELETE | `/school/media/{id}` | School JWT | Remove an uploaded file record |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}/photo` | School JWT | Save R2 photo URL after a presigned upload (JSON {photoUrl}) |
| POST | `/school/auth/login` | Public | X-Subdomain header, returns token + cookie |
| POST | `/school/auth/refresh` | Cookie | X-Subdomain + ?schoolId, rotates cookie |
| POST | `/school/auth/logout` | Cookie | Revokes refresh token |
| POST | `/school/auth/change-password` | School JWT | Self-service — verify current password, set new, revoke refresh tokens |
| GET  | `/school/roles` | School JWT | List roles with permission count |
| POST | `/school/roles` | School JWT | Create role |
| PUT  | `/school/roles/{id}` | School JWT | Update role name/description |
| GET  | `/school/roles/permissions` | School JWT | All available permissions |
| GET  | `/school/roles/{id}/permissions` | School JWT | Permission IDs for a role |
| PUT  | `/school/roles/{id}/permissions` | School JWT | Replace full permission set (atomic) |
| GET  | `/school/users` | School JWT | List all users in group (incl. linked staff name/designation) |
| POST | `/school/users` | School JWT | Create user from a staff record (EmployeeId required) + assign role to branches |
| GET  | `/school/users/roles` | School JWT | Roles list (for create-user dropdown) |
| GET  | `/school/users/schools` | School JWT | Branches list (for create-user dropdown) |
| GET  | `/school/users/staff` | School JWT | Active staff without a login (create-user staff dropdown) |
| PUT  | `/school/users/{id}/reset-password` | School JWT | Reset password + revoke refresh tokens |
| PUT  | `/school/users/{id}/status` | School JWT | Activate / Deactivate |
| GET  | `/school/master/academic-years` | School JWT | List academic years (?activeOnly=true filters to Active status; IsCurrent=true on most recent Active year) |
| POST | `/school/master/academic-years` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/academic-years/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/class-groups` | School JWT | List class groups |
| POST | `/school/master/class-groups` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/class-groups/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/fee-categories` | School JWT | List fee categories |
| POST | `/school/master/fee-categories` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/fee-categories/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/classes` | School JWT | List classes (with class group name via JOIN) |
| POST | `/school/master/classes` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/classes/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/sections` | School JWT | List sections for a class (?classId required) |
| POST | `/school/master/sections` | School JWT | Create section |
| PUT  | `/school/master/sections/{id}` | School JWT | Update section |
| GET  | `/school/class-teachers` | School JWT | List class→teacher assignments (?academicYearId, ?classId); drives parent→teacher message routing (Phase 92) |
| GET  | `/school/class-teachers/teachers` | School JWT | Active logins for the assign-teacher dropdown |
| POST | `/school/class-teachers` | School JWT | Assign a teacher to a class (+ optional section; NULL = whole class) |
| DELETE | `/school/class-teachers/{id}` | School JWT | Unassign (hard delete) |
| GET  | `/school/message-reports` | School JWT | Reported messages for admin review (?status); gated on SETTINGS.MANAGE (Phase 92) |
| GET  | `/school/messages/threads` | School JWT + `MESSAGES.VIEW` | Read-only admin conversation list (filters: academicYearId/classId/sectionId/studentUniqueId/teacherUserId/dateFrom/dateTo; default last 5 days) (Phase 103) |
| GET  | `/school/messages/threads/{id}` | School JWT + `MESSAGES.VIEW` | Full conversation, read-only (Phase 103) |
| GET  | `/school/message-reports/open-count` | School JWT | Count of open reports (nav badge) |
| POST | `/school/message-reports/{id}/resolve` | School JWT | Resolve a report (action: Removed hides the message both sides / Reviewed) |
| GET  | `/school/master/fee-types` | School JWT | List fee types |
| POST | `/school/master/fee-types` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/fee-types/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/terms` | School JWT | List terms (?academicYearId optional filter) |
| POST | `/school/master/terms` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/terms/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/subjects` | School JWT | List subjects |
| POST | `/school/master/subjects` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/subjects/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/payment-modes` | School JWT | List payment modes |
| POST | `/school/master/payment-modes` | School JWT | Create |
| PUT  | `/school/master/payment-modes/{id}` | School JWT | Update |
| GET  | `/school/master/fee-periods` | School JWT | List fee periods (?academicYearId optional) |
| POST | `/school/master/fee-periods` | School JWT | Create fee period |
| PUT  | `/school/master/fee-periods/{id}` | School JWT | Update fee period |
| GET  | `/school/students` | School JWT | List students (search, classId, sectionId, academicYearId, status, bloodGroup, joinType, busRouteId, hostelId, latestOnly); ?latestOnly=true returns only the highest academic_year_id row per admission_no via ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY admission_no, school_id ORDER BY academic_year_id DESC) — used by fee collection search to avoid duplicate rows for multi-year students |
| GET  | `/school/students/{id}` | School JWT | Full student detail |
| GET  | `/school/students/{id}/history` | School JWT | Temporal audit trail — all saved versions of the student (Phase 79) |
| POST | `/school/students` | School JWT | Create student |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}` | School JWT | Update student |
| POST | `/school/students/{id}/photo` | School JWT | Upload student photo (multipart) |
| POST | `/school/students/bulk` | School JWT | Bulk create students (max 500); default duplicate admissionNo returns error; `Upsert=true` in body updates existing rows (match admission_no+academic_year) personal/contact fields instead of erroring |
| GET  | `/school/students/promote/preview` | School JWT | Preview eligible students + detainedCount (fromYearId, fromClassId, fromSectionId optional) |
| POST | `/school/students/promote` | School JWT | Promote students — INSERT new rows for target year+class+section; skips already-promoted |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}/block` | School JWT | Set status=Blocked with mandatory reason |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}/unblock` | School JWT | Set status=Active, clear blocked_reason |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}/detain` | School JWT | Set is_detained=1 with mandatory reason |
| PUT  | `/school/students/{id}/release` | School JWT | Set is_detained=0, clear detained_reason |
| GET  | `/school/fees/structure` | School JWT | Get fee structure (classId, feeCategoryId, academicYearId, admissionType optional) |
| POST | `/school/fees/structure` | School JWT | Save fee structure (delete+insert scoped by admissionType slice; supports payment_type=Term|Monthly and fee_period_id) |
| POST | `/school/fees/structure/bulk` | School JWT | Bulk upsert fee structure rows from CSV (max 500); each row = one Class+Category+FeeType+Term |
| GET  | `/school/fees/student/{studentId}` | School JWT | Student fee summary with outstanding dues |
| POST | `/school/fees/collect` | School JWT | Collect fee + generate receipt |
| GET  | `/school/fees/receipts` | School JWT | List receipts (search, dateFrom, dateTo, status, createdAfter, createdBefore, source, category); ?source=webapp + createdAfter/createdBefore for EOD legacy pull; ?category=Transport for the Transport Receipts page (Phase 131) |
| GET  | `/school/fees/receipts/{id}` | School JWT | Full receipt detail with line items |
| PUT  | `/school/fees/receipts/{id}/cancel` | School JWT | Cancel receipt (requires CancelReason) |
| POST | `/school/fees/receipts/bulk` | School JWT | Bulk import legacy receipts from CSV rows (max 1000); groups by LegacyReceiptNo or AdmissionNo+Date+Mode |

| PUT  | `/school/fees/gateway-settings` | School JWT | Save/update gateway API keys |
